① The birth of septuplets in the small town made the news all over the world.
= 那个小镇上出生了七胞胎，消息传遍了全世界。
septuplets = 七胞胎
② Each of the septuplets weighed less than a kilogram, so they all stayed in hospital for weeks.
= 那七胞胎每个都不到一公斤重，所以都在医院里住了好几个星期。
each of the septuplets = 七胞胎中的每一个

sepulchral
/səˈpʌlkrəl/
核心：形容词（书面）：阴森的、阴沉的、似坟墓的：a sepulchral voice、sepulchral silence。
① The old butler opened the door and said in a deep, sepulchral voice, "The master is expecting you."
= 老管家打开门，用低沉阴森的声音说："主人在等您。"
a sepulchral voice = 阴森的嗓音
sepulchral = 阴森的
② A sepulchral silence filled the empty cathedral after the last visitors had gone home.
= 最后一批游客回家后，空荡荡的大教堂里一片死寂。
sepulchral silence = 死一般的寂静

sepulchre
/ˈseplkə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（书面）：坟墓、墓穴（尤指在岩石中凿成的）。美式拼作 sepulcher。
① The family sepulchre was cut into the rock on the hill above the old village.
= 那个家族的墓穴凿在旧村子上方山上的岩石里。
sepulchre = 坟墓
a family sepulchre = 家族墓穴
② Pilgrims travel to Jerusalem to visit the church built over the sepulchre where Jesus was buried.
= 朝圣者前往耶路撒冷，参观建在耶稣墓穴之上的那座教堂。
the sepulchre = 墓穴

sequel
/ˈsiːkwəl/
核心：名词，可数：（书、电影的）续集、续篇：a sequel to something；（事件的）后续、结果。
① The first film was so popular that the studio made a sequel two years later with the same actors.
= 第一部电影太受欢迎了，两年后电影公司用原班演员拍了续集。
a sequel = 续集
sequel = 续篇
② She is writing a sequel to her first novel, in which the girl from the farm grows up and moves to London.
= 她在写她第一本小说的续篇，书里那个农场姑娘长大后搬到了伦敦。
a sequel to something = 某作品的续篇
③ As a sequel to the scandal, three ministers lost their jobs before the end of the year.
= 那桩丑闻的后续是，年底前三位部长丢了官。
as a sequel to something = 作为某事的后续

sequence
/ˈsiːkwəns/
核心：名词：（事情的）先后次序、顺序（可数或不可数）：in sequence、in the right sequence；一连串、一系列（可数）：a sequence of events；（电影的）一组镜头、片段（可数）。动词，及物：（科学上）测定…的序列：sequence genes；按顺序排列。
① Put the pictures in the right sequence so that they tell the story from beginning to end.
= 把这些图片按正确的顺序排好，让它们从头到尾讲出这个故事。
sequence = 顺序
in the right sequence = 按正确的顺序
② The police are trying to work out the sequence of events that led to the fire.
= 警方在努力弄清导致那场火灾的一连串事件。
a sequence of events = 一连串事件
sequence = 有先后的序列
近义对照：
series = 一系列（不强调顺序）
③ The numbers on the doors run in sequence, so room 12 is right next to room 11.
= 门上的号码是按顺序排的，所以 12 号房就在 11 号房隔壁。
in sequence = 按顺序
sequential = 按顺序的
④ The opening sequence of the film shows a car racing through the empty streets at night.
= 那部电影的开场片段是一辆车在夜里空荡荡的街道上飞驰。
the opening sequence = 开场片段
⑤ Scientists were able to sequence the genes of the virus within a few days.
= 科学家们几天之内就测出了那种病毒的基因序列。
sequence genes = 测定基因序列

sequencer
/ˈsiːkwənsə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：音序器（编排、播放电子音乐的设备或软件）；（基因）测序仪。
① He made the whole song at home on his computer, using a sequencer and a cheap keyboard.
= 他用音序器和一个便宜的电子琴，在家里的电脑上做出了整首歌。
a sequencer = 音序器
sequencer = 音序器
② The laboratory bought a new gene sequencer that can read a human genome in a day.
= 实验室买了一台新的基因测序仪，一天就能读完一个人的基因组。
a gene sequencer = 基因测序仪

sequencing
/ˈsiːkwənsɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：（基因）测序：gene sequencing、DNA sequencing；排序、安排先后。
① Thanks to gene sequencing, doctors can now find out which illnesses a baby is likely to have.
= 多亏了基因测序，医生现在能查出婴儿可能患哪些疾病。
gene sequencing = 基因测序
sequencing = 测序
② Good sequencing of lessons helps students build on what they learned the week before.
= 课程安排得先后得当，能帮助学生在上周所学的基础上继续学习。
the sequencing of lessons = 课程的先后安排

sequential
/sɪˈkwenʃl/
核心：形容词：按顺序的、相继的：in sequential order。副词 sequentially 是按顺序地、依次地。
① The pages of the report are numbered in sequential order, so it is easy to find the right section.
= 报告的页码是按顺序编的，所以很容易找到要看的部分。
in sequential order = 按顺序
sequential = 按顺序的
② The computer deals with the tasks sequentially, finishing one before it starts the next.
= 电脑依次处理那些任务，做完一个才开始下一个。
sequentially = 依次地

sequester
/sɪˈkwestə(r)/
核心：动词，及物：使隔离、使隐退：sequester yourself；（陪审团）隔离；（科学）封存（如二氧化碳）；（法律）扣押（财产）。
① The jury was sequestered in a hotel for three weeks so that they could not read the newspapers about the trial.
= 陪审团被隔离在一家酒店里三个星期，让他们看不到报纸上关于那场审判的报道。
be sequestered = 被隔离
sequester = 使隔离
② The writer sequestered herself in a cottage by the sea to finish her book.
= 那位作家隐居在海边一座小屋里，要把书写完。
sequester yourself = 隐居
③ Forests help fight climate change because trees sequester carbon from the air.
= 森林有助于对抗气候变化，因为树木能从空气中封存碳。
sequester carbon = 封存碳

sequestered
/sɪˈkwestəd/
核心：形容词（书面）：僻静的、与世隔绝的：a sequestered valley。
① They spent their holiday in a sequestered valley in the mountains, far from any road.
= 他们在山里一个远离公路的僻静山谷里度假。
a sequestered valley = 僻静的山谷
sequestered = 僻静的
② The old monk had lived a sequestered life in the monastery for over fifty years.
= 那位老修士在修道院里过了五十多年与世隔绝的生活。
a sequestered life = 与世隔绝的生活

sequestrate
/ˈsiːkwəstreɪt/
核心：动词，及物（法律）：扣押、没收（财产，直到还清债务）。
① The court ordered the bank to sequestrate the company's property until the debts were paid.
= 法院命令银行扣押那家公司的财产，直到还清债务。
sequestrate = 扣押
sequestrate property = 扣押财产
② After the business failed, all his cars and paintings were sequestrated by the court.
= 生意失败后，他所有的汽车和画作都被法院扣押了。
be sequestrated = 被扣押

sequestration
/ˌsiːkwəˈstreɪʃn/
核心：名词，不可数：（法律）扣押、查封；（科学）（碳的）封存：carbon sequestration；隔离。
① The sequestration of his property meant he could not even use his own car.
= 他的财产被查封，连自己的车都不能用了。
the sequestration of property = 查封财产
sequestration = 扣押、查封
② Carbon sequestration means storing carbon dioxide underground instead of letting it into the air.
= 碳封存是指把二氧化碳储存在地下，而不是排进空气里。
carbon sequestration = 碳封存

sequin
/ˈsiːkwɪn/
核心：名词，可数：（缝在衣服上做装饰的）亮片、闪光片。
① Her dress for the party was covered in silver sequins that sparkled under the lights.
= 她的派对礼服上缀满了银色亮片，在灯光下闪闪发亮。
sequins = 亮片
sequin = 亮片
② My little sister spent the whole afternoon stitching sequins onto her dancing costume.
= 我妹妹整个下午都在往她的舞蹈服上缝亮片。
stitch sequins onto something = 往某物上缝亮片

sequinned
/ˈsiːkwɪnd/

① The singer walked onto the stage in a long sequinned dress that shone like a mirror.
= 那位歌手穿着一条像镜子一样闪亮的缀片长裙走上舞台。
a sequinned dress = 缀有亮片的裙子
sequinned = 缀有亮片的
② The children wore sequinned hats and masks for the carnival parade through the town.
= 孩子们戴着缀满亮片的帽子和面具参加穿城而过的狂欢节游行。

sequoia
/sɪˈkwɔɪə/
核心：名词，可数：红杉、巨杉：产于美国加州的高大常绿树。
① Some of the giant sequoias in California are more than two thousand years old.
= 加州有些巨杉已经两千多岁了。
a giant sequoia = 巨杉
sequoia = 红杉
② We stood at the bottom of the sequoia and could not see the top because it was so tall.
= 我们站在那棵红杉底下，因为它太高了，看不到树顶。
the sequoia = 红杉

seraph
/ˈserəf/
核心：名词，可数：六翼天使、炽天使（《圣经》中最高级的天使；复数 seraphim 或 seraphs）。
① In the old painting, a seraph with six golden wings stands above the head of the sleeping king.
= 那幅古画里，一位长着六只金色翅膀的炽天使立在熟睡的国王头顶上方。
a seraph = 六翼天使
seraph = 炽天使
② The choir sang a hymn about the seraphim who praise God day and night in heaven.
= 唱诗班唱了一首赞美诗，歌唱在天堂日夜赞美上帝的众炽天使。
the seraphim = 众炽天使

seraphic
/səˈræfɪk/

① The baby gave her grandmother a seraphic smile and then fell asleep in her arms.
= 宝宝冲奶奶露出天使般的微笑，然后在她怀里睡着了。

seraphic = 天使般的
② The old priest had a seraphic look on his face as he listened to the children singing.
= 那位老神父听孩子们唱歌时，脸上带着安详幸福的神情。

Serb
/sɜːb/

① My neighbour is a Serb who moved to London from Belgrade twenty years ago and still cooks the food of his childhood.
= 我邻居是塞尔维亚人，二十年前从贝尔格莱德搬到伦敦，至今还做他小时候吃的饭菜。

② Serbs and Croats speak very similar languages, but they write them in different alphabets.

Serbian
/ˈsɜːbiən/

① We stayed with a Serbian family in a village near the Danube, and they fed us until we could not move.

② She learned Serbian at university and now works as a translator for a company in Belgrade.

serenade
/ˌserəˈneɪd/
核心：名词，可数：小夜曲（夜里在恋人窗下唱的情歌）；（器乐）小夜曲。动词，及物：为…唱小夜曲、对…唱情歌。
① Every night the young man stood under her window and sang a serenade with his guitar.
= 每天晚上，那个年轻人都站在她窗下弹着吉他唱小夜曲。
a serenade = 小夜曲
serenade = 情歌
② Mozart wrote a famous serenade for strings that is still played at weddings today.
= 莫扎特写过一首著名的弦乐小夜曲，至今还在婚礼上演奏。
a serenade for strings = 弦乐小夜曲
③ On their anniversary, he hired a band to serenade his wife at dinner in the restaurant.
= 结婚纪念日那天，他请了一支乐队在餐厅里为妻子演唱情歌。
serenade somebody = 为某人唱情歌

serendipitous
/ˌserənˈdɪpɪtəs/
核心：形容词：意外发现的、机缘巧合的：a serendipitous meeting。
① Their meeting was completely serendipitous: they both missed the same train and started talking on the platform.
= 他们的相遇完全是机缘巧合：两人都没赶上同一班火车，就在站台上聊了起来。
serendipitous = 机缘巧合的
② Many important medicines were serendipitous discoveries made while scientists were looking for something else.
= 许多重要的药物都是科学家在找别的东西时意外发现的。
a serendipitous discovery = 意外的发现

serendipity
/ˌserənˈdɪpəti/
核心：名词，不可数：意外发现珍宝的运气、机缘巧合。
① By pure serendipity, I found my grandmother's lost ring in an old coat at a second-hand shop.
= 纯属机缘巧合，我在一家二手店的一件旧外套里找到了奶奶丢失的戒指。
by pure serendipity = 纯属机缘巧合
serendipity = 机缘巧合
② The discovery of penicillin was a famous case of serendipity in the history of science.
= 青霉素的发现是科学史上一个著名的意外收获。
a case of serendipity = 意外收获的一例

serene
/səˈriːn/
核心：形容词：宁静的、安详的、平静的：a serene smile、a serene lake。副词 serenely 是平静地、安详地。名词 serenity 另立条。
① She looked calm and serene on her wedding day, even though the cake had arrived an hour late.
= 婚礼那天她看上去平静安详，尽管蛋糕晚到了一个小时。
serene = 宁静的
serene = 内心和外表都平静
近义对照：
calm = 平静（更通用）
quiet = 安静（指没声音）
② Early in the morning, the lake was serene and the mountains were reflected perfectly in the water.
= 清晨，湖面一片宁静，群山清清楚楚地倒映在水中。
a serene lake = 宁静的湖
serenity = 宁静
③ The old woman smiled serenely as her grandchildren ran around her chair, shouting and laughing.
= 孙辈们围着她的椅子跑来跑去，又叫又笑，老太太安详地微笑着。
serenely = 安详地

serenity
/səˈrenəti/
核心：名词，不可数：宁静、安详、平静。
① After a busy week in the city, we enjoyed the serenity of the little village in the hills.
= 在城里忙了一个星期之后，我们享受着山中小村的宁静。
the serenity of something = 某处的宁静
serenity = 宁静
② She faced her illness with great serenity and never once complained to the nurses.
= 她非常平静地面对自己的病，从没向护士们抱怨过一次。
with serenity = 平静地

serf
/sɜːf/
核心：名词，可数：（中世纪的）农奴：为地主种地、不能离开土地的人。
① In the Middle Ages, a serf had to work on his lord's land and could not leave the village without permission.
= 在中世纪，农奴必须给领主种地，没有许可不能离开村子。
a serf = 农奴
serf = 农奴
② Russia freed its serfs in 1861, but many of them stayed poor for generations.
= 俄国在 1861 年解放了农奴，但他们中许多人好几代都很穷。
free the serfs = 解放农奴

serfdom
/ˈsɜːfdəm/

① Serfdom ended in most of western Europe hundreds of years before it ended in Russia.
= 西欧大部分地区的农奴制比俄国早几百年就结束了。
serfdom = 农奴制
② The poor families on the estate lived in a kind of serfdom, working for almost nothing.
= 庄园里的穷人家过着近乎农奴的日子，几乎白干活。
a kind of serfdom = 近乎农奴的境地

serge
/sɜːdʒ/

① The old soldier still had his heavy blue serge uniform hanging in the wardrobe.
= 那位老兵的衣柜里还挂着他那身厚重的蓝色哔叽制服。

② In the 1950s, many men went to church on Sunday in a dark serge suit.

sergeant
/ˈsɑːdʒənt/

① The sergeant shouted at the young soldiers to stand up straight and stop talking.
= 中士冲那些年轻士兵大喊，叫他们站直、别说话。

serial
/ˈsɪəriəl/
核心：名词，可数：（电视、广播、杂志的）连续剧、连载：a television serial。形容词：连续的、一连串的；（作品）连载的；（计算机）串行的。副词 serially 是连续地、连载地。
① My grandmother never missed an episode of her favourite radio serial about a farming family.
= 我奶奶从没漏听过她最爱的那部讲农家的广播连续剧。
a radio serial = 广播连续剧
serial = 连续剧
② The police believe the five murders were the work of a serial killer who chooses his victims at night.
= 警方认为那五起谋杀案是一个连环杀手所为，他专在夜里挑选受害者。
a serial killer = 连环杀手
③ Please write down the serial number on the back of your television before you call the repair shop.
= 给维修店打电话之前，请把电视机背面的序列号记下来。
a serial number = 序列号
④ The old printer connects to the computer through a serial port at the back.
= 那台旧打印机通过后面的串行端口连到电脑上。
a serial port = 串行端口
⑤ Some people practise serial monogamy, moving from one long relationship to the next with no gap.
= 有些人奉行连续单配偶制，一段长期恋情结束就马上开始下一段。
serial monogamy = 连续单配偶制
⑥ The novel was first published serially in a weekly magazine before it came out as a book.
= 那部小说先在一份周刊上连载，后来才出单行本。
serially = 连载地
⑦ He is a serial offender who has been caught stealing cars four times this year.
= 他是个惯犯，今年已经四次因偷车被抓。
a serial offender = 惯犯

serialization
/ˌsɪəriəlaɪˈzeɪʃn/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：连载；改编成连续剧。英式也拼作 serialisation。
① The newspaper paid a lot of money for the serialization of the famous actor's life story.
= 那家报纸花大价钱买下了那位名演员传记的连载权。
the serialization of something = 某作品的连载
serialization = 连载
② The radio serialization of the novel was so popular that the book sold out in every shop.
= 那部小说改编的广播连续剧太受欢迎，书在每家书店都卖光了。
a radio serialization = 广播连续剧改编

serialize
/ˈsɪəriəlaɪz/
核心：动词，及物：连载（小说等）；把…改编成连续剧。英式也拼作 serialise。
① Many of Dickens's novels were serialized in magazines, one chapter every month.
= 狄更斯的许多小说都在杂志上连载，每月一章。
be serialized = 被连载
serialize = 连载
② The television company plans to serialize the book in six one-hour episodes next winter.
= 那家电视公司计划明年冬天把那本书改编成六集、每集一小时的连续剧。
serialize a book = 把书改编成连续剧

series
/ˈsɪəriːz/
核心：名词，可数（单复数同形）：一系列、一连串：a series of；（电视、广播的）系列节目、系列剧：a television series；（体育）系列赛；（书的）丛书。
① There has been a series of accidents on this road, so the town wants to lower the speed limit.
= 这条路上接连出了几起事故，所以镇上想降低限速。
a series of = 一系列
series = 一系列
② Have you watched the new crime series on television? The last episode was brilliant.
= 你看了电视上那部新的犯罪系列剧吗？最后一集精彩极了。
a crime series = 犯罪系列剧
③ England won the cricket series against Australia by three matches to one.
= 英格兰以三比一赢了对澳大利亚的板球系列赛。
a cricket series = 板球系列赛
④ The children love the books in this series and are waiting for the next one to come out.
= 孩子们喜欢这套丛书，正盼着下一本出版。
the books in this series = 这套丛书

serif
/ˈserɪf/
核心：名词，可数：衬线（印刷字母笔画末端的小短线）。比较 sans serif（无衬线字体）。
① Newspapers often use a font with serifs because it is easier to read in long articles.
= 报纸常用带衬线的字体，因为长文章读起来更轻松。
serifs = 衬线
serif = 衬线
② The designer chose a serif typeface for the wedding invitations to make them look elegant.
= 设计师给婚礼请柬选了衬线字体，让它们显得典雅。
a serif typeface = 衬线字体

serious
/ˈsɪəriəs/
核心：形容词：（问题、病情、事故）严重的：a serious accident、a serious illness；（人）认真的、严肃的、不开玩笑的：be serious about something、look serious；（事情）重要的、需要认真对待的：a serious decision；（书、音乐）严肃的、正经的。副词 seriously 是严重地、认真地；口语里 Seriously? 是「真的吗？不是开玩笑吧？」。名词 seriousness 另立条。
① There was a serious accident on the motorway this morning, and three people were taken to hospital.
= 今天早上高速公路上发生了一起严重事故，三人被送往医院。
serious = 严重的
a serious accident = 严重事故
② Are you serious about moving to Australia, or is it just an idea for now?
= 你是真打算搬去澳大利亚，还是现在只是个想法？
serious = 认真的
be serious about something = 认真对待某事
③ The teacher looked serious when she came into the room, so we all stopped talking at once.
= 老师进教室时表情严肃，所以我们都马上不说话了。
look serious = 表情严肃
④ Choosing a university is a serious decision, so take your time and visit a few first.
= 选大学是件大事，所以别着急，先去几所看看。
a serious decision = 重大决定
⑤ He was seriously injured when he fell off the roof, and he could not walk for months.
= 他从屋顶摔下来受了重伤，好几个月不能走路。
seriously injured = 受重伤
seriously = 严重地
⑥ You need to take your health seriously and stop working until midnight every day.
= 你得认真对待自己的健康，别再天天干到半夜了。
take something seriously = 认真对待某事
⑦ When my friend phoned to say she had won a free holiday to Hawaii, I shouted, "Seriously? That's amazing!"
= 朋友打电话说她中了免费去夏威夷度假的奖，我大叫："真的吗？太棒了！"
Seriously? = 真的吗？

seriousness
/ˈsɪəriəsnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：严重性：the seriousness of the situation；严肃、认真：in all seriousness。
① The doctor explained the seriousness of the illness to the family in a quiet room.
= 医生在一间安静的屋子里向家属说明了病情的严重性。
the seriousness of something = 某事的严重性
seriousness = 严重性
② In all seriousness, I think you should talk to a lawyer before you sign that contract.
= 说正经的，我觉得你签那份合同之前应该先找律师谈谈。
in all seriousness = 说正经的

sermon
/ˈsɜːmən/
核心：名词，可数：（教堂里的）布道、讲道：preach a sermon；（贬义）说教、训诫：give somebody a sermon on something。
① The priest preached a long sermon about forgiveness, and some of the old men fell asleep.
= 神父做了一场关于宽恕的长篇布道，有几个老人睡着了。
sermon = 布道
preach a sermon = 布道
词族：
preach = 布道
② When I came home late, my father gave me a sermon on the dangers of walking alone at night.
= 我回家晚了，父亲给我上了一课，讲夜里独自走路有多危险。
give somebody a sermon on something = 就某事对某人说教

sermonize
/ˈsɜːmənaɪz/
核心：动词，不及物（贬义）：说教、训诫、讲大道理。英式也拼作 sermonise。
① My uncle likes to sermonize about hard work, but he has never had a job for more than a year.
= 我叔叔爱讲勤劳的大道理，可他从没在一份工作上干满一年。
sermonize = 说教
sermonize about something = 就某事说教
② The film tells a strong story without sermonizing, so the audience can make up their own minds.
= 那部电影讲了一个有力的故事，却不说教，让观众自己判断。
without sermonizing = 不说教

serotonin
/ˌserəˈtəʊnɪn/
核心：名词，不可数：血清素、五羟色胺：脑中影响情绪、睡眠的化学物质。
① Doctors think that low levels of serotonin in the brain may be one cause of depression.
= 医生认为，脑中血清素水平低可能是抑郁症的原因之一。
serotonin = 血清素
low levels of serotonin = 血清素水平低
② Exercise and sunlight can both help your body to produce more serotonin and feel happier.
= 运动和阳光都能帮身体产生更多血清素，让人更快乐。
produce serotonin = 产生血清素

serpent
/ˈsɜːpənt/
核心：名词，可数（书面）：蛇、大蛇（尤指故事、神话中的）；（比喻）阴险的人。
① In the story of Adam and Eve, a serpent tells Eve to eat the fruit from the forbidden tree.
= 在亚当和夏娃的故事里，一条蛇叫夏娃吃禁树上的果子。
a serpent = 蛇
serpent = 大蛇
② Old maps of the ocean were decorated with sea serpents and other monsters.
= 古老的海图上画着海蛇和其他怪物。
a sea serpent = 海蛇（传说中的海怪）

serpentine
/ˈsɜːpəntaɪn/
核心：形容词（书面）：蜿蜒的、弯弯曲曲的：a serpentine road；（计谋）阴险复杂的。名词，不可数：蛇纹石（一种绿色岩石）。
① The serpentine road climbed slowly up the mountain, with a sharp bend every hundred metres.
= 那条蜿蜒的山路缓缓向上爬，每隔一百米就有一个急弯。
a serpentine road = 蜿蜒的道路
serpentine = 蜿蜒的
② Nobody could follow the serpentine plot of the novel, with its twenty characters and secret plans.
= 那部小说情节错综复杂，有二十个人物和种种秘密计划，谁也看不明白。
a serpentine plot = 错综复杂的情节
③ The table in the hotel lobby was made of green serpentine from a local quarry.
= 酒店大堂里那张桌子是用当地采石场的绿色蛇纹石做的。
green serpentine = 绿蛇纹石

serrated
/səˈreɪtɪd/
核心：形容词：有锯齿的：a serrated knife、a serrated edge。
① Use a serrated knife to cut the bread, or you will squash it flat.
= 用锯齿刀切面包，不然会把面包压扁。
a serrated knife = 锯齿刀
serrated = 有锯齿的
② The leaves of this plant have serrated edges that can cut your fingers if you are not careful.
= 这种植物的叶子边缘有锯齿，不小心会割破手指。
serrated edges = 锯齿状边缘

serration
/səˈreɪʃn/
核心：名词，可数（常用复数）：锯齿、锯齿状边缘。
① The serrations on the blade make it easy to cut through rope and thick plastic.
= 刀刃上的锯齿让它能轻松割断绳子和厚塑料。
serrations = 锯齿
serration = 锯齿
② Look closely at the leaf and you will see tiny serrations all along its edge.
= 仔细看那片叶子，你会看到它的边缘有一圈细小的锯齿。
tiny serrations = 细小的锯齿

serried
/ˈserid/
核心：形容词（书面）：（人、物）密集排列的、一排排的：serried ranks。
① Serried ranks of soldiers stood in the square, waiting for the king to arrive.
= 一排排密集的士兵站在广场上，等着国王驾到。
serried ranks = 密集的队列
serried = 密集排列的
② From the plane we could see serried rows of greenhouses covering the whole valley.
= 从飞机上我们能看到一排排密密麻麻的温室铺满了整个山谷。
serried rows = 密密麻麻的一排排

serum
/ˈsɪərəm/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：血清（复数 sera 或 serums）；（防毒、治病用的）血清、抗毒素；（护肤用的）精华液。
① The scientists separated the serum from the blood samples and tested it for the virus.
= 科学家们从血样中分离出血清，检测里面有没有病毒。
serum = 血清
② The hospital keeps a supply of snake serum in case someone is bitten on the hills.
= 医院备有蛇毒血清，以防有人在山上被蛇咬。
snake serum = 蛇毒血清
③ She puts a drop of face serum on her skin every night before she goes to bed.
= 她每晚睡前在脸上抹一滴精华液。
face serum = 面部精华液

servant
/ˈsɜːvənt/
核心：名词，可数：仆人、佣人：a servant、servants；（为国家、公众服务的）人员：a public servant、a civil servant。
① In the nineteenth century, a rich family in London might have ten servants living in the house.
= 十九世纪，伦敦的富人家里可能住着十个仆人。
servant = 仆人
servants = 仆人（复数）
② As a public servant, a police officer must treat everyone in the town fairly, rich or poor.
= 作为公职人员，警察必须公平对待镇上每一个人，不论贫富。
a public servant = 公职人员
③ Her father was a civil servant who worked in the same government office for forty years.
= 她父亲是个公务员，在同一个政府部门干了四十年。
a civil servant = 公务员

serve
/sɜːv/
核心：动词：（给人）端上、上（菜、饮料）：serve dinner、serve somebody something；（商店里）接待、招呼（顾客）：be served；为…工作、效力：serve your country、serve in the army；服（刑）、任（职）：serve a sentence、serve two terms；起…作用、用作：serve as something；够…吃：serves four；（网球等）发球。习语：it serves you right 是活该；first come, first served 是先到先得。
① We serve breakfast from seven until ten, and dinner from six o'clock.
= 我们七点到十点供应早餐，晚餐从六点开始。
serve = 招待、上（菜）
serve breakfast = 供应早餐
词族：
service = 服务
servant = 仆人
② I waited at the counter for ten minutes before anyone came to serve me.
= 我在柜台等了十分钟才有人来招呼我。
serve somebody = 接待某人
③ Her grandfather served in the navy for twenty years and travelled all over the world.
= 她祖父在海军服役二十年，走遍了世界各地。
serve in the navy = 在海军服役
④ He served six years in prison for robbery and then found a job as a cook.
= 他因抢劫坐了六年牢，出狱后找了份厨师的工作。
serve six years = 服刑六年
⑤ The old sofa in the garage now serves as a bed for the dog.
= 车库里那张旧沙发现在当了狗的床。
serve as something = 用作某物
⑥ This recipe serves four people, so double everything if your whole family is coming.
= 这个菜谱够四个人吃，要是你全家都来，就所有材料都加倍。
serve four people = 够四个人吃
⑦ She served so hard that her opponent could not even touch the ball.
= 她发球力量太大，对手连球都碰不到。
serve = 发球
⑧ You didn't study for the test, so it serves you right that you failed.
= 你考试前不复习，没及格是活该。
it serves you right = 活该
⑨ There are only fifty free tickets, and it is first come, first served, so get there early.
= 免费票只有五十张，先到先得，所以早点去。
first come, first served = 先到先得
⑩ The meeting served a useful purpose, because at last everyone knew what the others were doing.
= 那次会议起了作用，因为大家终于知道别人在干什么了。
serve a purpose = 起作用
⑪ The young striker served out the last year of his contract and then moved to a club in Spain.
= 那名年轻前锋踢完合同的最后一年，就转会去了西班牙的一家俱乐部。
serve out = 干满（任期、合同、刑期）
⑫ Every Friday night my grandmother served up a huge plate of fish and chips for the whole family.
= 每个星期五晚上，奶奶都给全家端上一大盘炸鱼薯条。
serve up = 端上（饭菜）；（比喻）提供
serve up 也可比喻「拿出、提供」：serve up a surprise。

server
/ˈsɜːvə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（计算机）服务器；（餐馆的）服务员；（网球等的）发球者；（分菜的）餐具：salad servers。
① The website was very slow because too many people were trying to use the server at once.
= 网站很慢，因为同时有太多人在访问服务器。
a server = 服务器
server = 服务器
② Our server brought the menus and asked if we would like something to drink first.
= 我们那桌的服务员拿来菜单，问我们要不要先来点喝的。
our server = 我们那桌的服务员
③ The tennis player is a powerful server, and she won most of her points that way.
= 那位网球运动员发球很有力，她大部分分都是这么拿的。
a powerful server = 发球很有力的选手
④ The company keeps all its customers' data in a server farm, a huge building full of computers.
= 那家公司把所有客户的数据存放在一个服务器群里，那是一座装满电脑的巨大建筑。
a server farm = 服务器群
⑤ She mixed the salad and handed me the wooden salad servers so that I could take some.
= 她拌好沙拉，把木制沙拉夹递给我，让我自己夹一些。
salad servers = 沙拉夹

servery
/ˈsɜːvəri/
核心：名词，可数（英式）：（食堂、餐厅的）配餐处、取餐台。
① In the school canteen, the children queue at the servery and choose their lunch.
= 在学校食堂里，孩子们在取餐台前排队挑选午饭。
the servery = 取餐台
servery = 配餐处
② The cook passed the hot plates through a hatch from the kitchen to the servery.
= 厨师通过一个窗口把热盘子从厨房递到配餐处。
from the kitchen to the servery = 从厨房到配餐处

service
/ˈsɜːvɪs/
核心：名词：（商店、餐馆、公司的）服务（不可数）：good service、customer service；（交通、邮政等的）公共服务、班次（可数）：a bus service；（政府的）部门、机构（可数）：the health service；（车、机器的）检修、保养（可数）；（宗教的）仪式、礼拜（可数）：a church service；（复数 the services）军队；（网球等）发球（可数）。动词，及物：检修、保养（车、机器）。
① The food in that restaurant is excellent, but the service is very slow.
= 那家餐厅的菜很棒，可上菜服务很慢。
service = 服务
② If the new phone does not work, call customer service and they will send you another one.
= 新手机要是不好用，就打电话给客服，他们会给你另寄一部。
customer service = 客服
这里 service 不可数，不说 a good service（指餐馆服务时）。
③ The lift is out of service again, so we had to carry the sofa up four floors.
= 电梯又停用了，所以我们只好把沙发扛上四楼。
out of service = 停用中
常见于电梯、电话、公交车上的告示。
④ There is a bus service every twenty minutes from the station to the airport.
= 从车站到机场每二十分钟有一班公交车。
a bus service = 公交车班次
⑤ The church service lasted an hour, and afterwards everyone had tea in the hall.
= 教堂礼拜持续了一个小时，之后大家在礼堂喝茶。
a church service = 教堂礼拜
⑥ I take my car to the garage for a service once a year, before the winter starts.
= 我每年入冬前把车送到修车厂保养一次。
a service = （车的）保养
service a car = 保养汽车
⑦ My grandfather joined the services at eighteen and was an officer in the air force for thirty years.
= 我祖父十八岁参军，在空军当了三十年军官。
the services = 军队
指陆海空三军，总是带 the、用复数。
⑧ We stopped at a service area on the motorway to buy petrol and have a coffee.
= 我们在高速公路的服务区停下来加油、喝杯咖啡。
a service area = （高速公路）服务区
⑨ The bill came to sixty pounds, plus a ten per cent service charge for the waiters.
= 账单一共六十英镑，另加百分之十的服务费给服务员。
a service charge = 服务费
⑩ My father belongs to a service club that raises money for the local children's hospital every year.
= 我父亲加入了一个公益社团，每年为当地儿童医院募款。
a service club = （扶轮社之类的）公益社团
⑪ Hotels, restaurants and banks are all part of the service industry, which employs most people in this city.
= 酒店、餐馆和银行都属于服务业，这座城市的大多数人都在服务业工作。
the service industry = 服务业
⑫ The museum offers free entry to any service member and their family on national holidays.
= 国家节日期间，博物馆对所有现役军人及其家属免费开放。
a service member = 现役军人
⑬ In America we stopped at a service plaza on the turnpike, which had a restaurant and a big shop.
= 在美国，我们在收费公路的服务广场停了一下，那里有一家餐馆和一个大商店。
a service plaza = （美国收费公路的）服务广场
⑭ Lorries must use the service road behind the shops to deliver goods early in the morning.
= 货车必须走商店后面的辅路，一大早送货。
a service road = 辅路、便道
⑮ We changed our internet service provider because the connection kept going down in the evenings.
= 我们换了网络服务商，因为一到晚上就老断网。
a service provider = 服务提供商（尤指网络）
⑯ We stopped at a service station on the motorway to fill up the tank and buy sandwiches.
= 我们在高速公路的加油站停下来加满油、买了三明治。
a service station = 加油站（常附带商店）
英式里 service station 和 service area 常可互换。

serviceable
/ˈsɜːvɪsəbl/
核心：形容词：可用的、能用的；耐用的、实用的（虽不漂亮）：a serviceable coat。
① The old car is not beautiful, but it is still serviceable and gets me to work every day.
= 那辆旧车不好看，但还能用，每天都能把我送去上班。
serviceable = 可用的
② She bought a serviceable pair of boots that would last through many winters in the mountains.
= 她买了一双结实耐穿的靴子，能在山里穿好几个冬天。
a serviceable pair of boots = 一双结实耐穿的靴子

serviceman
/ˈsɜːvɪsmən/
核心：名词，可数：军人、男军人（复数 servicemen）；（美式）维修工。
① A monument in the square remembers the servicemen from the town who died in the war.
= 广场上的一座纪念碑纪念着镇上在战争中牺牲的军人。
servicemen = 军人
serviceman = 男军人
② The serviceman from the washing machine company came on Tuesday and fixed it in ten minutes.
= 洗衣机公司的维修工星期二来了，十分钟就修好了。
the serviceman = 维修工

servicewoman
/ˈsɜːvɪswʊmən/
核心：名词，可数：女军人（复数 servicewomen）。
① She was the first servicewoman in her family, and her grandfather was very proud of her uniform.
= 她是家里第一个女军人，她祖父为她的军装感到十分骄傲。
a servicewoman = 女军人
servicewoman = 女军人
② Servicewomen now do almost every job in the army, including flying fighter planes.
= 女军人如今几乎在军队里干所有的工作，包括驾驶战斗机。
servicewomen = 女军人（复数）

servicing
/ˈsɜːvɪsɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：（车、机器的）检修、保养。
① Regular servicing keeps the boiler working safely and stops it from breaking down in winter.
= 定期检修能让锅炉安全运转，不至于冬天坏掉。
regular servicing = 定期检修
servicing = 检修
② The price of the car includes free servicing for the first three years.
= 车价包括头三年的免费保养。
free servicing = 免费保养

serviette
/ˌsɜːviˈet/
核心：名词，可数（英式）：餐巾、餐巾纸（美式用 napkin）。
① The waiter put a folded white serviette next to each plate before the guests arrived.
= 客人来之前，服务员在每个盘子旁边放了一块叠好的白餐巾。
a serviette = 餐巾
serviette = 餐巾纸
② The little boy wiped his mouth with a paper serviette after eating his ice cream.
= 小男孩吃完冰激凌，用餐巾纸擦了擦嘴。
a paper serviette = 纸餐巾

servile
/ˈsɜːvaɪl/
核心：形容词（贬义）：奴性的、卑躬屈膝的、一味讨好的。名词 servility 另立条。
① The new manager was annoyed by the servile way the staff agreed with everything he said.
= 新经理对员工卑躬屈膝、他说什么都附和的样子很恼火。
servile = 卑躬屈膝的
② He gave a servile smile to the rich customer and hurried to open the door for her.
= 他冲那位有钱的顾客讨好地一笑，赶紧上前给她开门。
a servile smile = 讨好的笑

servility
/sɜːˈvɪləti/
核心：名词，不可数：奴性、卑躬屈膝。
① The writer hated the servility of the courtiers, who praised the king even when he was wrong.
= 那位作家厌恶朝臣们的奴性，国王错了他们也照样称颂。
the servility of somebody = 某人的奴性
servility = 卑躬屈膝
② She refused to show any servility towards her boss and always said what she really thought.
= 她拒绝对老板卑躬屈膝，总是说出自己的真实想法。
show servility = 表现出奴性

serving
/ˈsɜːvɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数：（食物的）一份：a serving of；形容词：（军人、官员）现役的、在职的：a serving officer。
① A single serving of this cereal gives you a third of the iron you need each day.
= 这种麦片一份就能提供你每天所需铁质的三分之一。
a serving of = 一份
serving = 一份
② Use the big serving spoon to put the rice onto the plates.
= 用那把大号分菜勺往盘子里盛米饭。
a serving spoon = 分菜勺
③ He is the only serving soldier in the village; all the others retired long ago.
= 他是村里唯一的现役军人，其他人早就退役了。
a serving soldier = 现役军人

servitude
/ˈsɜːvɪtjuːd/
核心：名词，不可数（书面）：奴役、劳役、受制于人：in servitude、penal servitude（苦役）。
① For centuries, millions of people lived in servitude, working for masters who owned them.
= 几百年来，数以百万计的人生活在奴役之下，为拥有他们的主人干活。
in servitude = 处于奴役之中
servitude = 奴役
② In the old days, a thief could be sentenced to ten years of penal servitude.
= 旧时，小偷可能被判十年苦役。
penal servitude = 苦役

servo
/ˈsɜːvəʊ/
核心：名词，可数：伺服机构、伺服电机（servo-mechanism / servomotor 的简称）；（澳式口语）加油站。
① The robot arm uses small servos to move each finger exactly where it needs to go.
= 机械臂用小型伺服电机把每根手指精确移动到需要的位置。
servos = 伺服电机
servo = 伺服机构
② We stopped at a servo outside Sydney to fill up the car and buy some cold drinks.
= 我们在悉尼城外一家加油站停下来加满油，买了些冷饮。
a servo = （澳）加油站

sesame
/ˈsesəmi/
核心：名词，不可数：芝麻；芝麻籽：sesame seeds、sesame oil。习语：open sesame 是「芝麻开门」，比喻轻易得到某物的秘诀。
① The baker puts sesame seeds on top of the bread rolls before they go into the oven.
= 面包师在面包卷进烤箱前往上面撒芝麻。
sesame seeds = 芝麻
sesame = 芝麻
② A few drops of sesame oil make the noodles taste much better.
= 滴几滴芝麻油，面条就好吃多了。
sesame oil = 芝麻油
③ For many young people, a degree from that university is an open sesame to a good job in the city.
= 对许多年轻人来说，那所大学的学位是在城里找到好工作的敲门砖。
an open sesame = 敲门砖、成功秘诀

session
/ˈseʃn/
核心：名词，可数：（做某事的）一段时间、一场、一节：a training session、a photo session；（议会、法庭的）会议、开庭期：in session；（大学的）学年、学期（美式、苏格兰）。
① Each session lasts an hour, and you can book up to three sessions a week.
= 每节课一小时，你每周最多可以预约三节。
session = 一场、一节
② The players have a training session every morning at nine, even in the rain.
= 球员们每天早上九点有一场训练，下雨也照常进行。
a training session = 培训课
training session = 培训课
③ The band spent the whole afternoon at a photo session for the cover of their new album.
= 乐队整个下午都在为新专辑封面拍照。
a photo session = 拍摄
photo session = 拍摄
④ You cannot go into the courtroom while the court is in session.
= 法庭开庭期间你不能进入法庭。
in session = 开会中
指议会、法庭正在开会，不用于普通公司会议。
⑤ The new session of parliament opens in October with a speech by the king.
= 议会的新会期十月开幕，由国王发表讲话。
a session of parliament = 议会会期

set
/set/
核心：动词（set, set）：放、摆：set something on the table、set the table；设定、调定：set the alarm、set the clock；定下（日期、价格、规则）：set a date、set a price；树立、创下：set an example、set a record；布置（作业、任务）：set homework；使处于某种状态：set somebody free、set fire to something；（太阳）落下；（液体）凝固；（骨头）接好。名词，可数：一套、一副：a set of；（电视）机：a television set；（电影、戏剧的）布景、片场；（网球等的）一盘。形容词：固定的、规定的：a set menu、a set time；准备好的：be all set；坚决的（反对）。
① She set the vase of flowers on the table by the window.
= 她把那瓶花放在窗边的桌子上。
set something on the table = 把某物放在桌上
② Can you set the table, please? Dinner will be ready in five minutes.
= 你能摆一下餐具吗？晚饭五分钟就好。
set the table = 摆餐具
英式也说 lay the table。
③ I set the alarm for six o'clock because my train leaves at seven.
= 我把闹钟定在六点，因为我的火车七点开。
set = 设定
set the alarm = 定闹钟
④ They have not set a date for the wedding yet, but it will probably be next summer.
= 他们还没定婚期，不过很可能是明年夏天。
set a date = 定日期
⑤ Parents should set a good example by putting their phones away at dinner.
= 父母应该以身作则，吃饭时把手机收起来。
set an example = 树立榜样
反面是 set a bad example（带坏头）。
⑥ She set a new world record in the hundred metres at the age of nineteen.
= 她十九岁时创造了百米新的世界纪录。
set a record = 创纪录
⑦ The children opened the cage and set the bird free in the garden.
= 孩子们打开笼子，在花园里把那只鸟放了。
set somebody free = 释放某人
⑧ The angry crowd set fire to two cars outside the football ground.
= 愤怒的人群在足球场外放火烧了两辆车。
set fire to something = 放火烧某物
别和 catch fire（着火）混：set fire to 是有人故意点的。
⑨ We sat on the beach and watched the sun set behind the islands.
= 我们坐在海滩上，看着太阳落到群岛后面。
the sun sets = 太阳落下
⑩ Put the jelly in the fridge for three hours until it has set.
= 把果冻放进冰箱三个小时，直到凝固。
set = 凝固
⑪ The teacher set us twenty questions for homework, and they took all evening.
= 老师给我们布置了二十道作业题，做了一整个晚上。
set homework = 布置作业
⑫ My grandmother gave us a beautiful set of glasses as a wedding present.
= 奶奶送了我们一套漂亮的玻璃杯当结婚礼物。
a set of = 一套
后面接复数名词，主语是 a set 时动词用单数：A set of keys was found.
⑬ We bought a new television set because the old one kept losing the picture.
= 我们买了一台新电视机，因为旧的老是没图像。
a television set = 电视机
⑭ The famous actor was very friendly to everyone on the film set.
= 那位著名演员对片场里的每个人都很友好。
a film set = 电影片场
⑮ She lost the first set 6–2 but went on to win the match.
= 她第一盘以二比六输了，但最后赢下了比赛。
a set = （网球）一盘
⑯ He had a set point in the second set, but his opponent saved it with a brilliant shot.
= 第二盘他拿到了盘点，但对手一记妙球挽救了这一分。
a set point = 盘点（再赢一分即拿下这一盘）
⑰ The restaurant does a set menu at lunchtime: three courses for fifteen pounds.
= 那家餐厅午餐有套餐：三道菜十五英镑。
a set menu = 套餐
⑱ "Are you all set for the trip?" "Yes, the bags are in the car."
= "旅行都准备好了吗？""好了，包都在车上。"
be all set = 准备就绪
⑲ My father is set against the idea of me leaving school at sixteen.
= 我父亲坚决反对我十六岁辍学。
be set against something = 坚决反对某事
⑳ As soon as she got home, she set about cleaning the kitchen from top to bottom.
= 她一到家就开始把厨房彻底打扫一遍。
set about doing something = 开始做某事
set about = 着手
㉑ The storm last month set back the building work on the new school by three weeks.
= 上个月那场暴风雨使新学校的建筑工程推迟了三个星期。
set back = 推迟、阻碍
口语里 set somebody back 还能指「花掉某人多少钱」：That coat set me back £200.
㉒ The new law set neighbours against each other, and the village was never the same again.
= 那项新法让邻里反目，村子再也回不到从前了。
set A against B = 使 A 和 B 对立
㉓ Every student has to read the set books for the exam, including two plays by Shakespeare.
= 每个学生都要读考试规定的指定书目，包括莎士比亚的两部剧本。
a set book = 指定书目
㉔ "Once upon a time" is a set phrase that begins many fairy stories.
= "从前"是很多童话故事开头的固定说法。
a set phrase = 固定说法
㉕ The team scored from a set piece, when the tall defender headed in a corner kick.
= 球队通过定位球得分：那个高个子后卫头球攻进了角球。
a set piece = 定位球
㉖ We had to buy a set-top box so that the old television could get the new digital channels.
= 我们得买个机顶盒，旧电视才能收到新的数字频道。
a set-top box = 机顶盒
㉗ My grandfather is very set in his ways and still refuses to use a mobile phone.
= 我祖父的老习惯根深蒂固，至今不肯用手机。
be set in your ways = 积习难改
多用来说上了年纪的人。
㉘ The bus stopped at the corner and set down two old ladies with their shopping bags.
= 公交车在拐角停下，让两位提着购物袋的老太太下了车。
set down = 让（乘客）下车；放下
set down 也指「写下、规定」：The rules are set down in the contract.
㉙ Winter set in early that year, and the lake was frozen by the end of October.
= 那年冬天来得早，十月底湖面就结冰了。
set in = （坏天气、不好的事）开始并持续
主语多是 winter、rain、panic、infection 这类不受欢迎的东西。
㉚ The old stone was set into the wall of the church to remember the men who built it.
= 那块旧石头被嵌进教堂的墙里，纪念建造教堂的人。
set into = 嵌入
㉛ We set off at five in the morning so that we could reach the mountains before lunch.
= 我们早上五点出发，好在午饭前到达山里。
set off = 出发、动身
㉜ Someone opened the back door during the night and set off the burglar alarm.
= 夜里有人打开后门，触发了防盗警报。
set off an alarm = 触发警报
set off 还可指「引爆（炸弹）、引发（一连串事情）」。
㉝ The farmer threatened to set his dogs on anyone who climbed over the fence again.
= 农场主威胁说，谁再翻篱笆就放狗咬谁。
set a dog on somebody = 放狗咬某人
㉞ The explorers set out across the desert with ten camels and enough water for a month.
= 探险队带着十头骆驼和够喝一个月的水，出发穿越沙漠。
set out = 出发、启程
㉟ She set out to prove that a small village school could get better results than the big city schools.
= 她立志要证明，一所村小能比城里的大学校考得更好。
set out to do something = 立志做某事
set out to do 强调一开始就有明确目标。
㊱ My uncle set up his own building company when he was only twenty-five.
= 我叔叔才二十五岁就创办了自己的建筑公司。
set up = 创办、建立
㊲ We set up the tent next to the river before it got dark and started a fire.
= 我们在天黑前在河边搭好帐篷，生起了火。
set up a tent = 搭帐篷
set up 还常指「设圈套陷害」：He says the police set him up.
㊳ On the way home through the forest, the travellers were set upon by a gang of robbers.
= 在穿过森林回家的路上，那些旅人遭到一伙强盗袭击。
be set upon by somebody = 遭到某人袭击
set upon 多用被动，比 attack 更书面。
㊴ He took a set shot from just outside the line, and the ball dropped cleanly through the basket.
= 他在线外一点站定投篮，球干净利落地落进篮筐。
a set shot = （篮球）定点投篮
㊵ For the geometry test you will need a ruler, a protractor and a set square.
= 几何考试你需要带直尺、量角器和三角板。
a set square = 三角板
美式叫 triangle。
㊶ Our set text for the English exam this year is a novel by Jane Austen.
= 我们今年英语考试的指定课文是简·奥斯汀的一部小说。
a set text = 指定课文
㊷ Ever since she visited the hospital as a little girl, she has been set on becoming a doctor.
= 自从小时候去过那家医院，她就一心想当医生。
be set on something = 一心想要某事
和 be set against（坚决反对）正好相反。

setback
/ˈsetbæk/
核心：名词，可数：挫折、障碍、倒退：suffer a setback、a major setback。
① Losing their best player through injury was a serious setback for the team.
= 最好的球员受伤缺阵对球队是个严重挫折。
a setback = 挫折
setback = 障碍
② After a few setbacks at the beginning, the new business started to make money in its second year.
= 开头遇到几次挫折之后，那家新公司第二年开始赚钱了。
suffer a setback = 遭受挫折

sett
/set/
核心：名词，可数：（铺路用的）方石块、石板；（獾的）洞穴，也拼作 set。
① The old streets in the town centre are paved with granite setts that are slippery in the rain.
= 镇中心的老街是用花岗岩方石铺的，下雨天很滑。
setts = 铺路方石
sett = 方石块
② We sat quietly near the badger sett at dusk and waited for the animals to come out.
= 黄昏时我们静静坐在獾洞附近，等着它们出来。
a badger sett = 獾洞

settee
/seˈtiː/
核心：名词，可数（英式）：长沙发、长靠椅（同 sofa）。
① After the long walk, my father fell asleep on the settee in front of the television.
= 走了很久的路之后，我父亲在电视机前的长沙发上睡着了。
the settee = 长沙发
settee = 长靠椅
② They bought a second-hand settee for their first flat because a new one cost too much.
= 他们给第一套公寓买了一张二手长沙发，因为新的太贵了。
a second-hand settee = 二手长沙发

setter
/ˈsetə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：塞特犬（一种长毛猎犬）；（常构成复合词）制定者、创造者：a trendsetter、a pace-setter；（排球）二传手；（考试的）出题人。
① Our neighbour walks his red setter in the park every morning, even in the snow.
= 我们邻居每天早上都在公园遛他那条红色塞特犬，下雪也不例外。
a red setter = 红色塞特犬
setter = 塞特犬
② The setter on the volleyball team passed the ball perfectly for her teammate to smash it.
= 排球队的二传手把球传得恰到好处，让队友扣杀。
the setter = （排球）二传手
③ The question setters made this year's maths paper much harder than last year's.
= 出题人把今年的数学卷出得比去年难多了。
question setters = 出题人

setting
/ˈsetɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数：（事物所处的）环境、背景：a beautiful setting；（故事的）发生地点和时间；（机器上的）设置、挡位：the settings on your phone；（宝石的）底座；（一人份的）餐具：a place setting。
① The hotel is in a beautiful setting, with mountains behind it and a lake in front.
= 那家酒店环境优美，背靠群山，面朝湖泊。
setting = 环境
a beautiful setting = 优美的环境
② The setting for the novel is a small fishing village in Scotland in the 1950s.
= 那部小说的背景是二十世纪五十年代苏格兰的一个小渔村。
the setting for a story = 故事的背景
③ Go to the settings on your phone and turn off the sound for messages.
= 打开手机设置，把消息提示音关掉。
the settings = 设置
④ Turn the oven to its highest setting and cook the pizza for ten minutes.
= 把烤箱调到最高挡，把比萨烤十分钟。
the highest setting = 最高挡
⑤ She laid a place setting for each guest: two knives, two forks, a spoon and a glass.
= 她给每位客人摆好一份餐具：两把刀、两把叉、一把勺和一个杯子。
a place setting = 一份餐具

settle
/ˈsetl/
核心：动词：（在某地）定居：settle somewhere；解决、了结（争端）：settle an argument；结清、付清（账）：settle a bill；（使）安顿、舒服地坐下；（鸟、灰尘）停落、落下；决定、确定（选定）。
① After years of travelling, they decided to settle in a small town by the sea.
= 旅行多年之后，他们决定在海边一个小镇定居。
settle = 定居
settle in a place = 在某地定居
② Please settle the bill at reception before you leave the hotel on the last morning of your stay.
= 住店最后一天早上离开酒店前，请到前台结账。
settle = 结清
settle a bill = 结账
宾语多是账单、债务、账目，多见于酒店、商店等场合，口气较正式。
③ After two years of angry letters, the two companies finally settled their argument without going to court.
= 互相写了两年火药味十足的信之后，那两家公司终于不经法院就解决了纠纷。
settle an argument = 解决争端
④ Nobody had lived there for years, and thick dust had settled on all the furniture in the empty house.
= 那里多年没人住，空房子里所有家具上都落满了厚厚的灰尘。
dust settles = 灰尘落下
⑤ After years of moving from job to job, he finally got married and settled down in his home town.
= 换了多年工作之后，他终于结了婚，在家乡安定下来。
settle down = 安定下来
⑥ The children were too excited to settle down and go to sleep on Christmas Eve.
= 平安夜孩子们太兴奋了，静不下来睡觉。
settle down and go to sleep = 静下来睡觉
⑦ We wanted a house with a garden, but in the end we had to settle for a small flat.
= 我们想要一套带花园的房子，可最后只好将就租了一套小公寓。
settle for = 将就接受
⑧ It took our son a few weeks to settle in at his new school, but now he loves it.
= 我儿子花了几个星期才适应新学校，不过现在他很喜欢那里。
settle in = 适应（新环境）
⑨ She settled into her new job quickly and was soon managing a team of ten people.
= 她很快适应了新工作，不久就带起了一个十人团队。
settle into something = 适应某事
⑩ After hours of talking, they finally settled on a name for the baby.
= 讨论了好几个小时，他们终于给宝宝定下了名字。
settle on something = 选定某物
⑪ Once the guests had gone, she made a cup of tea and settled to her work for the evening.
= 客人一走，她就泡了杯茶，静下心来做晚上的工作。
settle to something = 静下心来做某事
⑫ A large bird settled on the roof of the barn and watched us all afternoon.
= 一只大鸟落在谷仓的屋顶上，整个下午都在看着我们。
a bird settles on something = 鸟停落在某物上

settled
/ˈsetld/
核心：形容词：安定的、稳定的：a settled life；（天气）稳定的、晴好持续的；（人在新地方）习惯了的、安顿好的：feel settled。
① After years of moving around the country, she wanted a settled life with a home of her own.
= 在全国各地漂泊多年之后，她想过一种有个自己的家的安定生活。
a settled life = 安定的生活
settled = 安定的
② The weather should stay settled for the rest of the week, so it is a good time for a picnic.
= 这周余下几天天气应该会一直晴好，正适合野餐。
settled weather = 晴好稳定的天气
③ It took her a few months to feel settled in London after leaving her family in Spain.
= 离开在西班牙的家人之后，她花了几个月才在伦敦安顿下来。
feel settled = 感觉安顿好了

settlement
/ˈsetlmənt/
核心：名词：（正式）协议、和解（可数）：reach a settlement；（争端的）解决（不可数）；（新建的）居民点、定居点（可数）；（账的）结清、付清（不可数）；（人的）定居、殖民（不可数）。
① After months of talks, the workers and the company reached a settlement on pay.
= 经过几个月的谈判，工人和公司在工资问题上达成了协议。
reach a settlement = 达成协议
settlement = 协议
② The settlement of the argument between the two farmers about the fence took longer than anyone expected.
= 两个农场主为篱笆起的那场争端，解决起来比任何人预料的都要久。
the settlement of something = 某事的解决
③ Archaeologists found the remains of a Viking settlement under the new car park.
= 考古学家在新停车场底下发现了一处维京人定居点的遗迹。
a settlement = 定居点
④ The bill must be paid in full within thirty days, and we will send a receipt on settlement.
= 账单须在三十天内全额付清，付清后我们会寄去收据。
on settlement = 付清时
⑤ In the 1890s, young volunteers lived in a settlement house in the poorest part of Chicago and taught English to immigrants.
= 十九世纪九十年代，年轻志愿者住在芝加哥最穷街区的一所社区服务中心里，给移民教英语。
a settlement house = 社区服务中心

settler
/ˈsetlə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（新到某地的）定居者、移民、殖民者。
① The first European settlers arrived in the valley in 1820 and built farms along the river.
= 第一批欧洲移民 1820 年来到这个山谷，沿河建起了农场。

settler = 移民
② Life was very hard for the early settlers, who had to build their own houses before winter.
= 早期定居者的生活十分艰苦，他们得在入冬前自己盖房子。
the early settlers = 早期定居者

seven
/ˈsevn/
核心：数词：七；七个；七岁；七点：at seven。
① There are seven days in a week and twelve months in a year.
= 一周有七天，一年有十二个月。
seven = 七
② My little brother is seven, and he has just learned to ride a bike without help.
= 我弟弟七岁，刚学会不用人扶骑自行车。

③ The shop opens at seven, so I buy my bread there on the way to work.
= 那家店七点开门，所以我上班路上去那里买面包。

seventeenth
/ˌsevnˈtiːnθ/

① The house was built in the seventeenth century and still has its original wooden doors.
= 那座房子建于十七世纪，至今还保留着原来的木门。
the seventeenth century = 十七世纪
seventeenth = 第十七
② My birthday is on the seventeenth of March, so we usually have a party in the garden if it is warm.
= 我的生日是三月十七日，天气暖和的话我们通常在花园里开派对。
the seventeenth of March = 三月十七日

seventh
/ˈsevnθ/
核心：序数词：第七；名词：七分之一。习语：in seventh heaven 是欣喜若狂。
① We live on the seventh floor, so we always take the lift with our shopping.
= 我们住七楼，所以买了东西总是坐电梯。
the seventh floor = 七楼

② About a seventh of the money we raised went to the children's hospital.

③ When she heard she had got the job, she was in seventh heaven for a week.

/ˈsevntiəθ/

seventy
/ˈsevnti/

sever
/ˈsevə(r)/
核心：动词，及物：切断、割断：sever a cable；断绝（关系）：sever ties with somebody。
① The workers accidentally severed a water pipe, and the whole street had no water for a day.
= 工人们不小心挖断了一根水管，整条街停水了一天。

② After the argument, she severed all ties with her brother and never spoke to him again.
= 那次争吵之后，她和哥哥断绝了一切来往，再也没和他说过话。
sever ties with somebody = 和某人断绝关系
③ His finger was nearly severed in the accident, but the doctors managed to save it.
= 那次事故中他的手指几乎被切断，但医生设法保住了它。
be nearly severed = 几乎被切断

several
/ˈsevrəl/
核心：限定词、代词：几个、数个（多于两个但不太多）：several times、several of。形容词（书面）：各自的、各别的。副词 severally 是分别地、各自地（法律、书面）。
① I have told him several times to close the door, but he never remembers.
= 我跟他说过好几次要关门，可他从来记不住。
several = 几个
several times = 好几次
② Several of my friends are coming to the party, but most of them will be late.
= 我有几个朋友要来参加派对，但大多会晚到。
several of = …中的几个
③ The three partners are jointly and severally responsible for the company's debts.
= 三位合伙人对公司的债务承担连带及各自的责任。
jointly and severally = 连带及各自地
severally = 各自地

severance
/ˈsevərəns/
核心：名词，不可数：（关系的）断绝、中止；遣散、解雇：severance pay（遣散费）。
① When the old shoe factory closed last spring, every worker received six months' severance pay.
= 去年春天那家老鞋厂关闭时，每个工人都领到了六个月的遣散费。
severance pay = 遣散费
severance = 遣散
② The severance of relations between the two countries meant that families could not visit each other.
= 两国断交意味着两边的家人无法互相探望。
the severance of relations = 断绝关系

severe
/sɪˈvɪə(r)/
核心：形容词：（问题、伤病、天气）严重的、剧烈的、恶劣的：a severe headache、severe weather；（人、规定）严厉的、严格的：a severe teacher；（样式）朴素的、不加装饰的。副词 severely 是严重地、严厉地。名词 severity 另立条。
① She had a severe headache and had to lie down in a dark room all afternoon.
= 她头痛得厉害，整个下午只好躺在一间暗屋子里。
severe = 严重的
a severe headache = 剧烈头痛
② Severe weather is expected tonight, with strong winds and heavy snow in the north.
= 预计今晚天气恶劣，北部有大风和大雪。
severe weather = 恶劣天气
③ Our old maths teacher was very severe and never smiled in class.
= 我们以前的数学老师很严厉，上课从来不笑。
a severe teacher = 严厉的老师
④ The town was severely damaged by the floods, and hundreds of families lost their homes.
= 那个镇子在洪水中受灾严重，几百户人家失去了家园。
severely = 严重地
severely damaged = 严重受损
⑤ He was severely punished for cheating in the exam and had to take the whole year again.
= 他考试作弊受到严厉处罚，得把这一年重读一遍。
severely punished = 受到严厉惩罚

severity
/sɪˈverəti/
核心：名词，不可数：严重性、剧烈程度：the severity of the injury；严厉、严格。
① Doctors could not tell the severity of the injury until they had seen the X-ray.
= 医生要看过 X 光片才能判断伤势有多严重。
the severity of something = 某事的严重性
severity = 严重性
② The judge spoke with great severity to the young men who had attacked the old woman.
= 法官对袭击那位老太太的年轻男子们说话极为严厉。
with severity = 严厉地

sew
/səʊ/
核心：动词（sewed, sewn 或 sewed）：缝、缝制、缝补：sew a button on、sew something together；（用针线）做针线活。注意读音和 so、sow（播种）相同，拼写别和 sow 混。
① My grandmother taught me to sew when I was eight, and I made a dress for my doll.
= 我八岁时奶奶教我缝东西，我给布娃娃做了一条裙子。
sew = 缝
② Could you sew this button back on my coat before I go out tonight?
= 我今晚出门前，你能帮我把这颗扣子缝回外套上吗？
sew a button on = 钉扣子
③ She has sewn all the curtains for the new house herself, which saved a lot of money.
= 新房子的窗帘全是她自己缝的，省了不少钱。
have sewn something = 已缝好某物
④ My mother bought an old sewing machine at the market and still uses it to make our clothes.
= 我母亲在集市上买了一台旧缝纫机，至今还用它给我们做衣服。
sewing machine = 缝纫机
a sewing machine = 一台缝纫机
⑤ The doctor sewed up the cut on his knee with six neat stitches.
= 医生给他膝盖上的伤口缝了六针，缝得很整齐。
sew up = 缝合
⑥ After weeks of talks, the company had the deal sewn up by the end of the month.
= 谈了几个星期之后，公司到月底把那笔交易敲定了。
have something sewn up = 把某事敲定
sew up 口语里比喻「稳操胜券、敲定」。

sewage
/ˈsuːɪdʒ/

① After the storm, raw sewage flowed into the river and the beaches were closed for a week.
= 暴风雨过后，未经处理的污水流进河里，海滩关闭了一个星期。
raw sewage = 未经处理的污水
sewage = 污水
② The town is building a new sewage plant to clean the water before it goes back into the sea.
= 镇上正在建一座新的污水处理厂，把水处理干净再排回海里。
a sewage plant = 污水处理厂
③ In the past, the city's waste was spread on fields at a sewage farm outside the walls.
= 过去，城里的污物都被运到城墙外的污水农场，撒在田里。
a sewage farm = 污水农场
④ The smell from the old sewage works reached the village whenever the wind blew from the east.
= 一刮东风，旧污水处理场的臭味就飘到村子里。
the sewage works = 污水处理场

sewer
/ˈsuːə(r)/

= 工人们下到下水道里，查看是什么堵住了管道。

sex
/seks/
核心：名词：性别（不可数或可数）：the opposite sex、people of both sexes；性行为、性交（不可数）：have sex。动词，及物：鉴别（动物）的性别。注意 sex 指生理上的性别，gender 更常指社会意义上的性别。
① You can't tell the sex of a kitten until it is a few weeks old.
= 小猫要长到几周大才能看出是公是母。

② The school is open to children of both sexes from the age of four.
= 那所学校招收四岁以上的男女儿童。

③ He was very shy and found it hard to talk to members of the opposite sex.
= 他很害羞，觉得很难和异性说话。
the opposite sex = 异性
the opposite sex 必须加 the，不说 an opposite sex。
④ Teenagers should learn about sex and relationships at school as well as at home.
= 青少年应该在学校和家里都学习性与人际关系方面的知识。

⑤ Some film stars have a natural sex appeal that makes everyone in the room look at them.
= 有些影星天生有性感魅力，一进屋所有人都会看他们。

⑥ A sex change is a series of medical operations that changes a person's body to match the gender they identify with.
= 变性手术是一系列医疗手术，使一个人的身体与其认同的性别相符。

⑦ In the biology lesson, we learned that the X and Y sex chromosomes decide whether a baby is a boy or a girl.
= 生物课上我们学到，X 和 Y 两种性染色体决定宝宝是男是女。
a sex chromosome = 性染色体
⑧ The newspaper printed stories about the singer's sex life, and he took them to court.
= 那家报纸刊登了那位歌手性生活的报道，他把报社告上了法庭。

⑨ The advert was criticized for treating the young woman as a sex object instead of a person.
= 那则广告因把那名年轻女子当成性对象而不是一个人来对待而受到批评。
a sex object = 性对象
⑩ The police were looking for a dangerous sex maniac who had attacked two women in the park.
= 警方在追捕一名在公园袭击了两名女子的危险性狂魔。

⑪ The court ordered that the sex offender must stay away from schools for the rest of his life.
= 法院下令那名性犯罪者终生不得接近学校。

⑫ In the 1950s, the actress was the biggest sex symbol in Hollywood.
= 二十世纪五十年代，那位女演员是好莱坞最大的性感偶像。
a sex symbol = 性感偶像
⑬ Researchers studied sex typing in children's books, where boys are always brave and girls are always gentle.
= 研究人员研究了童书中的性别定型现象：男孩总是勇敢，女孩总是温柔。

⑭ The editor wanted to sex up the report with more shocking stories so that more people would read it.
= 编辑想往报道里加些更耸人听闻的内容来增加吸引力，好让更多人看。

⑮ The charity gives free health checks to sex workers in the city centre.
= 那家慈善机构为市中心的性工作者提供免费体检。
a sex worker = 性工作者
⑯ On the farm, an expert came to sex the chicks so that the farmer knew which ones would lay eggs.
= 农场来了一位专家给小鸡鉴别雌雄，好让农场主知道哪些以后会下蛋。
sex the chicks = 鉴别小鸡雌雄

sexagenarian
/ˌseksədʒəˈneəriən/

/ˈseksɪst/

④ The workers feel they have been shafted by the company, which cut their pay while the bosses got bonuses.
= 工人们觉得被公司坑了：老板们拿奖金，他们却被减薪。
be shafted = 被坑

shag
/ʃæɡ/
核心：名词：粗烟丝（不可数）；长绒、粗绒（不可数）：a shag carpet；（英式粗俗）性交（可数）；鸬鹚（一种海鸟，可数）。动词（英式粗俗）：和…性交。
① My grandfather smoked strong shag tobacco in a pipe and the whole house smelled of it.
= 我祖父用烟斗抽浓烈的粗烟丝，满屋子都是那股味儿。
shag tobacco = 粗烟丝
shag = 粗烟丝
② The old house still had an orange shag carpet from the 1970s in the living room.
= 那座老房子的客厅里还铺着一块二十世纪七十年代的橙色长绒地毯。
a shag carpet = 长绒地毯
③ We watched a shag dive into the sea and come up with a fish in its beak.
= 我们看着一只鸬鹚扎进海里，嘴里叼着一条鱼浮了上来。
a shag = 鸬鹚
（英式粗俗用法指性交，日常交流中避免使用。）

shagged
/ʃæɡd/
核心：形容词（英式口语）：精疲力竭的，常用 shagged out。
① After walking up the mountain all day, I was completely shagged and went straight to bed.
= 爬了一整天山之后，我累得要命，直接上床睡了。
shagged = 精疲力竭的
② The players looked shagged out after extra time, and two of them could hardly walk.
= 加时赛之后球员们看上去累垮了，有两个几乎走不动路。
shagged out = 累垮了

shaggy
/ˈʃæɡi/

① A big shaggy dog ran across the field and jumped into the river.
= 一只毛发蓬乱的大狗跑过田野，跳进了河里。
a shaggy dog = 毛发蓬乱的狗
shaggy = 蓬乱的
② My uncle told a long shaggy-dog story at dinner, and when he finally finished, nobody laughed.
= 我叔叔在晚饭时讲了一个又长又啰嗦的笑话，等他终于讲完，谁也没笑。
a shaggy-dog story = 冗长无趣的笑话

shah
/ʃɑː/
核心：名词，可数：沙阿（旧时伊朗国王的称号）。
① The last shah of Iran left the country in 1979, and he never returned.
= 伊朗末代沙阿 1979 年离开了那个国家，再也没有回去。
the shah = 沙阿
shah = 伊朗国王
② The museum in Tehran shows the jewels and crowns that once belonged to the shahs.
= 德黑兰的那座博物馆展出曾属于历代沙阿的珠宝和王冠。
the shahs = 历代沙阿

shake
/ʃeɪk/
核心：动词（shook, shaken）：（使）摇动、抖动、震动：shake a bottle；（因害怕、寒冷而）发抖：shake with fear；使震惊、使不安：be shaken by something；动摇（信心）：shake somebody's faith。习语：shake hands 是握手；shake your head 是摇头（表示否定）。名词，可数：摇动；奶昔（milkshake 的简称）。
① Always shake the bottle well before you pour the orange juice into the glasses.
= 往杯子里倒橙汁之前一定要把瓶子摇匀。
shake = 摇
shake a bottle = 摇瓶子
② The two men shook hands and agreed to meet again next week.
= 两人握了握手，约好下周再见。
shake hands = 握手
③ When I asked if she wanted to come, she just shook her head and looked away.
= 我问她想不想来，她只是摇摇头，把脸转开了。
shake your head = 摇头
④ The little boy was shaking with cold when they pulled him out of the river.
= 他们把那个小男孩从河里拉上来时，他冻得直发抖。
shake with cold = 冻得发抖
⑤ She was badly shaken by the accident, although she was not hurt.
= 那次事故虽然没让她受伤，却把她吓得不轻。
be shaken by something = 被某事吓到
shaken = 受了惊吓的
⑥ Even after the trial, nothing could shake his belief that his son was innocent.
= 即使审判过后，什么也动摇不了他认定儿子无辜的信念。
shake somebody's belief = 动摇某人的信念
⑦ The house shook as the heavy lorry went past in the night.
= 夜里那辆重型卡车开过时，房子都震动了。
the house shook = 房子震动了
⑧ I have had a cold for two weeks, and I just can't shake it off.
= 我感冒两个星期了，就是好不了。
shake something off = 摆脱某物
shake off = 摆脱
⑨ She shook out the tablecloth in the garden to get rid of the crumbs.
= 她在花园里抖了抖桌布，把面包屑抖掉。
shake out = 抖开、抖掉
⑩ The new manager wants to shake up the company and get rid of the old ways of working.
= 新经理想整顿公司，摒弃老一套的工作方式。
shake up = 整顿、彻底改组
⑪ The gang shook down the shop owners on the street for money every week.
= 那伙人每周都向那条街的店主们敲诈钱财。
shake down = 敲诈
⑫ At the café by the beach I ordered a burger, some chips and a chocolate shake.
= 在海边的咖啡馆，我点了一个汉堡、一份薯条和一杯巧克力奶昔。
a chocolate shake = 巧克力奶昔
⑬ "Are you ready?" "I'll be with you in two shakes, I just need to find my keys."
= "准备好了吗？""马上就好，我找一下钥匙。"
in two shakes = 马上

shakedown
/ˈʃeɪkdaʊn/
核心：名词，可数（美式口语）：敲诈、勒索；彻底搜查；（新船、新飞机的）试航、试运行。
① The shop owner told the police about the shakedown, and the gang members were arrested.
= 店主把被敲诈的事告诉了警方，那伙人被捕了。
a shakedown = 敲诈
shakedown = 勒索
② The prison guards carried out a shakedown of every cell, looking for phones and knives.
= 狱警对每间牢房进行了彻底搜查，查找手机和刀具。
a shakedown of something = 对某处的彻底搜查
③ The new ship went on a shakedown cruise to make sure everything worked before its first long voyage.
= 那艘新船首次远航前先做了一次试航，确保一切运转正常。
a shakedown cruise = 试航

shaker
/ˈʃeɪkə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（撒盐、胡椒等的）瓶、罐：a salt shaker；（调鸡尾酒的）摇杯；（大写 Shaker）震颤派教徒；习语：a mover and shaker 是有影响力的人物。
① Could you pass me the salt shaker? The soup needs a little more salt.
= 能把盐瓶递给我吗？汤得再加点盐。
a salt shaker = 盐瓶
shaker = 撒料瓶
② The barman put ice and orange juice into the cocktail shaker and shook it hard.
= 酒保往鸡尾酒摇杯里放了冰块和橙汁，用力摇了起来。
a cocktail shaker = 鸡尾酒摇杯
③ She is one of the movers and shakers in the music business, and every new band wants to meet her.
= 她是音乐界的风云人物之一，每支新乐队都想见她。
a mover and shaker = 有影响力的人物

shaking
/ˈʃeɪkɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：摇动、颤抖。形容词：颤抖的：with shaking hands。
① The shaking of the ground woke everyone in the village just before the earthquake.
= 地震前一刻，大地的晃动惊醒了村里所有人。
the shaking of the ground = 大地的晃动
shaking = 摇动
② With shaking hands, she sat at the kitchen table and opened the letter from the hospital.
= 她坐在厨房桌前，双手颤抖着打开了医院寄来的信。
with shaking hands = 双手颤抖着

shaky
/ˈʃeɪki/
核心：形容词：（人、手、声音）颤抖的：feel shaky；（东西）不稳的、摇晃的：a shaky table；（情况、知识）不牢靠的、靠不住的：a shaky start。副词 shakily 是颤抖地、摇晃地。
① I still felt a bit shaky after the flu, so I stayed at home for another day.
= 得了流感之后我还觉得有点虚，就又在家待了一天。
feel shaky = 觉得发虚
shaky = 颤抖的
② Don't put the hot tea on that shaky old table, or it will fall over.
= 别把热茶放在那张摇摇晃晃的旧桌子上，不然会翻倒。
a shaky table = 摇晃的桌子
③ After a shaky start, the team played much better in the second half and won the game.
= 开局不稳之后，球队下半场踢得好多了，赢下了比赛。
a shaky start = 不稳的开局
④ The old man stood up shakily and walked slowly to the door with his stick.
= 老人颤巍巍地站起来，拄着拐杖慢慢走到门口。
shakily = 颤巍巍地

shale
/ʃeɪl/
核心：名词，不可数：页岩（一种易碎成薄片的软岩石）。形容词 shaly 是页岩的、含页岩的。
① The cliffs along this part of the coast are made of grey shale that breaks easily into thin pieces.
= 这段海岸的悬崖是灰色页岩，很容易碎成薄片。
shale = 页岩
② The company wants to drill for shale gas under the farmland, but the villagers are against it.
= 那家公司想在农田底下钻探页岩气，但村民们反对。
shale gas = 页岩气
③ The path up the hill was steep and shaly, and small stones kept sliding under our boots.
= 上山的小路又陡又满是页岩碎片，小石子不断在我们靴子底下滑动。
shaly = 含页岩的

shall
/ʃəl/（弱）/ʃæl/（强）
核心：情态动词：（用于 I、we 的疑问句）表示提议、征求意见：Shall I…?、Shall we…?；（书面、英式）将要、会（用于 I、we）；（正式、法律文件）必须、应当。否定缩略式 shan't；古旧第二人称单数 thou shalt。
① Shall I open the window for you? It's very hot in here with all these people.
= 要我帮你开窗吗？这里人这么多，太热了。
shall = 用于提议
Shall I…? = 要我…吗？
② Shall we go to the cinema tonight, or would you rather stay at home?
= 我们今晚去看电影好吗，还是你更想待在家里？
Shall we…? = 我们…好吗？
③ We shall arrive in Paris at about six o'clock, if the train is on time.
= 如果火车准点，我们大约六点到巴黎。
shall = 将要
④ I shan't be home for dinner tonight, because I have to work late at the office.
= 我今晚不回家吃晚饭了，因为得在办公室加班。
shan't = shall not 的缩略式
⑤ The contract says that the tenant shall pay the rent on the first day of each month.
= 合同规定租户必须在每月一日交房租。
shall = 必须（法律文件）
⑥ "Thou shalt not steal" is one of the Ten Commandments in the Bible.
= "不可偷盗"是《圣经》十诫之一。
thou shalt not = 你不可（古旧）

shallot
/ʃəˈlɒt/
核心：名词，可数：青葱、红葱头（一种小洋葱）。
① Chop two shallots very finely and fry them in butter until they are soft.
= 把两个红葱头切得很碎，用黄油炒到变软。
shallots = 红葱头
shallot = 青葱
② French cooks often use a shallot instead of an onion because it has a milder taste.
= 法国厨师常用红葱头代替洋葱，因为它的味道更温和。
a shallot = 一个红葱头

shallow
/ˈʃæləʊ/
核心：形容词：（水、容器）浅的：shallow water、a shallow dish；（人、想法）肤浅的、浅薄的；（呼吸）浅的。副词 shallowly 是浅浅地。名词 shallowness 另立条；复数名词 the shallows 是浅水处。
① The river is shallow here, so you can walk across it in summer.
= 这里河水很浅，所以夏天可以蹚过去。
shallow = 浅的
② The little children have to stay in the shallow end of the swimming pool.
= 小孩子们得待在游泳池的浅水区。
shallow end = 浅水区
③ The patient's shallow breathing worried the doctors, so they gave her oxygen.
= 病人呼吸很浅，医生很担心，就给她吸了氧。
shallow breathing = 浅呼吸
④ I thought he was a bit shallow, because all he talked about was money and cars.
= 我觉得他有点肤浅，因为他满嘴都是钱和车。
shallow = 肤浅的
⑤ Small fish swam in the shallows near the beach, and the children tried to catch them with nets.
= 小鱼在海滩附近的浅水处游动，孩子们想用网去捞。
the shallows = 浅水处
⑥ Lying in the hospital bed, she breathed shallowly and quickly, as if she had been running.
= 她躺在病床上，呼吸又浅又快，好像刚跑过步。
shallowly = 浅浅地

shallowness
/ˈʃæləʊnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：浅；肤浅、浅薄。
① The shallowness of the harbour means that big ships cannot come in at low tide.
= 港口水浅，意味着大船退潮时进不来。
the shallowness of something = 某处的浅
shallowness = 浅
② Critics laughed at the shallowness of the film, which had beautiful pictures but no real story.
= 评论家嘲笑那部电影肤浅：画面漂亮，却没有真正的故事。
shallowness = 肤浅

shalom
/ʃæˈlɒm/
核心：感叹词：（犹太人见面或告别时的问候语）平安、你好、再见。
① The old man in Jerusalem greeted every visitor to his little bookstall with a warm smile and a quiet shalom.
= 耶路撒冷那位老人以温暖的微笑和一声轻轻的「沙洛姆」（平安）迎接每一位来到他小书摊的客人。
shalom = 平安（犹太人的问候语）
② At the end of the service, the rabbi wished the whole congregation shalom.
= 礼拜结束时，拉比祝愿全体会众平安。
wish somebody shalom = 祝某人平安

sham
/ʃæm/
核心：名词：假象、骗局、虚伪（单数）：be a sham；（可数）骗子、假装的人。形容词：假的、虚假的：a sham marriage。动词（shammed, shammed）：假装、装作。
① The election was a sham, because only one party was allowed to take part.
= 那场选举是个骗局，因为只允许一个政党参加。
be a sham = 是个骗局
sham = 假象
② The couple were sent to prison for a sham marriage that let him stay in the country.
= 那对男女因假结婚让男方得以留在该国而被判入狱。
a sham marriage = 假结婚
③ He was not really ill; he was just shamming to get a day off school.
= 他不是真病了，只是装病想逃一天学。
sham = 假装

shaman
/ˈʃeɪmən/
核心：名词，可数：萨满、巫师（某些部落中被认为能和神灵沟通、治病的人）。
① The village shaman danced around the fire and called on the spirits to bring rain.
= 村里的萨满绕着火堆跳舞，祈求神灵降雨。

② People travelled for days to see the shaman, who was said to cure illnesses with plants and songs.
= 人们走好几天去见那位萨满，据说他能用草药和歌声治病。
the shaman = 巫师

shamanic
/ʃəˈmænɪk/

① The museum has a collection of drums and masks used in shamanic rituals in Siberia.
= 那座博物馆收藏了西伯利亚萨满仪式上用过的鼓和面具。

shamateur
/ˈʃæmətə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（贬义）：伪业余运动员（名义上是业余选手，实际上靠运动收钱）。
① In the old days, many Olympic athletes were shamateurs who were secretly paid by their governments.
= 早年许多奥运选手是伪业余运动员，偷偷拿着政府的钱。
shamateurs = 伪业余运动员

② The newspaper called the rugby player a shamateur after it found out about his free car and flat.
= 报纸查出那名橄榄球员白得了车和公寓后，称他是个伪业余选手。
a shamateur = 伪业余选手

shamateurism
/ˈʃæmətərɪzəm/
核心：名词，不可数（贬义）：伪业余主义（业余运动员暗中收钱的做法）。
① The new rules were meant to end the shamateurism that had been part of the sport for decades.
= 新规定旨在终结这项运动几十年来的伪业余做法。
shamateurism = 伪业余主义
② Critics said that shamateurism was unfair to honest players who really did have other jobs.
= 批评者说，伪业余做法对真正另有工作的诚实运动员不公平。
shamateurism was unfair = 伪业余做法不公平

shamble
/ˈʃæmbl/
核心：动词，不及物：（脚步拖沓地）蹒跚而行、慢吞吞地走。
① The tired old man shambled along the street in his slippers, carrying a bag of shopping.
= 那个疲惫的老人穿着拖鞋，提着一袋买的东西，在街上拖着脚步慢慢走。
shamble along = 蹒跚而行
shamble = 拖着脚走
② The prisoners shambled out of the van in chains and walked slowly into the court.
= 囚犯们戴着锁链拖着脚步走下囚车，慢慢走进法庭。
