shambles
/ˈʃæmblz/
核心：名词，单数（口语）：混乱、一团糟：be a shambles、in a shambles。
① The party was a complete shambles: the food was late, the music was too loud and half the guests went home early.
= 那场派对糟透了：菜上得晚，音乐太吵，一半客人早早就回家了。
be a shambles = 一团糟
shambles = 混乱
② After the storm, the garden was in a shambles, with broken branches everywhere.
= 暴风雨过后，花园里一片狼藉，到处是折断的树枝。
in a shambles = 一片狼藉

shambolic
/ʃæmˈbɒlɪk/
核心：形容词（英式口语）：混乱的、一团糟的、杂乱无章的。
① The first week of the new school year was shambolic, because nobody knew which room to go to.
= 新学年第一周乱成一团，因为谁也不知道该去哪间教室。
shambolic = 混乱的
② The minister was criticized in parliament for his shambolic handling of the floods last winter.
= 那位部长因去年冬天处理洪灾一团糟而在议会受到批评。
shambolic handling = 一团糟的处理

shame
/ʃeɪm/
核心：名词：羞愧、羞耻（不可数）：feel shame、to my shame；（单数）遗憾、可惜的事：It's a shame that…、What a shame!；（不可数）耻辱、丢脸。动词，及物：使羞愧、使蒙羞：shame somebody into doing something。习语：put somebody to shame 是使某人相形见绌；Shame on you! 是你真丢人！
① What a shame you can't come to the party! Everyone will miss you.
= 你来不了派对，真可惜！大家都会想你的。
shame = 遗憾
what a shame = 真可惜
② He felt a deep sense of shame after he lied to his best friend.
= 他对最好的朋友撒了谎之后，深感羞愧。
shame = 羞愧
词族：
ashamed = 羞愧的
shameful = 可耻的
③ It's a shame that the old cinema is closing, because it has been here for a hundred years.
= 那家老电影院要关门了，真可惜，它在这里开了一百年了。
It's a shame that… = …真可惜
④ When she heard her son had called his little sister stupid, she said, "Shame on you!"
= 她听说儿子骂妹妹笨，就说："你真丢人！"
Shame on you! = 你真丢人！
⑤ Every summer, her beautiful garden full of roses puts the rest of the street to shame.
= 每年夏天，她那开满玫瑰的漂亮花园都让整条街的其他人家相形见绌。
put somebody to shame = 使某人相形见绌
⑥ After the newspaper story about low wages, the company was shamed into paying its workers properly.
= 报纸刊出关于低工资的报道之后，那家公司羞愧得不得不给工人发足工资。
shame into doing something = 使羞愧而去做某事（常用被动 be shamed into doing）
⑦ He got drunk and shouted at the guests, and his behaviour at the wedding brought shame on the whole family.
= 他喝醉了冲客人大吼，他在婚礼上的行为让全家都蒙了羞。
bring shame on somebody = 使某人蒙羞

shamefaced
/ˌʃeɪmˈfeɪst/
核心：形容词：羞愧的、难为情的（因做错事）。副词 shamefacedly 是羞愧地。
① The boy looked shamefaced when his mother found the broken plate hidden under the sofa.
= 妈妈在沙发底下发现那只藏起来的破盘子时，男孩一脸羞愧。
look shamefaced = 一脸羞愧
shamefaced = 羞愧的
② Standing in the kitchen, he shamefacedly admitted that he had forgotten his wife's birthday again.
= 他站在厨房里，难为情地承认自己又忘了妻子的生日。
shamefacedly = 羞愧地

shameful
/ˈʃeɪmfl/
核心：形容词：可耻的、丢脸的：a shameful secret、It is shameful that…。副词 shamefully 是可耻地。
① It is shameful that so many old people live alone without enough heating in winter.
= 那么多老人冬天独自生活、连暖气都不够，真是可耻。
shameful = 可耻的
It is shameful that… = …是可耻的
② The family kept his time in prison a shameful secret for many years.
= 一家人多年来把他坐过牢当成一个不光彩的秘密。
a shameful secret = 不光彩的秘密
③ The workers were shamefully treated, and some of them were not paid for months.
= 工人们受到可耻的对待，有些人几个月都没拿到工钱。
shamefully = 可耻地

shameless
/ˈʃeɪmləs/
核心：形容词：无耻的、不知羞耻的：a shameless liar。副词 shamelessly 是无耻地、毫不掩饰地。名词 shamelessness 另立条。
① He is a shameless liar who will say anything to get what he wants.
= 他是个无耻的骗子，为了得到想要的东西什么话都说得出口。
a shameless liar = 无耻的骗子
shameless = 无耻的
② The advert shamelessly copies the famous song, changing only a few words.
= 那则广告毫不掩饰地抄袭了那首名曲，只改了几个词。
shamelessly = 毫不掩饰地

shamelessness
/ˈʃeɪmləsnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：无耻、不知羞耻。
① Everyone was shocked by the shamelessness of the politician, who lied even when the truth was on video.
= 那个政客连真相被录了像都还撒谎，大家都对他的无耻感到震惊。
the shamelessness of somebody = 某人的无耻
shamelessness = 无耻
② She laughed at the shamelessness of her cat, which stole fish from the table right in front of her.
= 她笑她那只猫不知羞耻，当着她的面从桌上偷鱼吃。
laugh at the shamelessness of something = 笑某物不知羞耻

shaming
/ˈʃeɪmɪŋ/
核心：形容词：令人羞愧的、丢脸的：a shaming experience。名词，不可数：（公开）羞辱：online shaming、body shaming。
① Being caught cheating in front of the whole class was a shaming experience he never forgot.
= 当着全班的面被抓到作弊，是让他终生难忘的丢脸经历。
a shaming experience = 令人羞愧的经历
shaming = 令人羞愧的
② The school runs lessons about body shaming and how unkind comments about people's looks can hurt.
= 那所学校开设课程，讲身材羞辱，以及对别人长相的刻薄评论会怎样伤人。
body shaming = 身材羞辱

shampoo
/ʃæmˈpuː/
核心：名词：洗发水、洗发剂（可数或不可数）；（地毯、汽车的）清洗剂；（单数）洗头。动词（shampooed, shampooed）：用洗发水洗（头发）；用清洗剂洗（地毯）。
① I've run out of shampoo, so I need to buy some on the way home.
= 我的洗发水用完了，得在回家路上买一瓶。
shampoo = 洗发水
② The hairdresser shampooed my hair with warm water and then cut it very short for the summer.
= 理发师用温水给我洗了头，然后为了过夏天剪得很短。
shampoo somebody's hair = 给某人洗头
③ We hired a machine to shampoo the carpets before we moved out of the flat.
= 搬出公寓之前，我们租了一台机器清洗地毯。
shampoo the carpets = 清洗地毯

shamrock
/ˈʃæmrɒk/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：三叶草（爱尔兰的国花和象征）。
① On St Patrick's Day, people in Dublin wear a shamrock on their coats.
= 圣帕特里克节那天，都柏林人在外套上别一枝三叶草。
a shamrock = 三叶草
shamrock = 三叶草
② The shamrock is a symbol of Ireland, and you can see it on rugby shirts and souvenirs.
= 三叶草是爱尔兰的象征，在橄榄球衣和纪念品上都能看到。
the shamrock = 三叶草（爱尔兰的象征）

shandy
/ˈʃændi/
核心：名词，可数或不可数（英式）：（啤酒兑柠檬汽水的）香迪酒。
① It was a hot afternoon, so I ordered a shandy instead of a pint of beer.
= 那天下午很热，所以我点了一杯香迪酒，没要一品脱啤酒。
a shandy = 一杯香迪酒
shandy = 香迪酒
② My grandfather let me have a small glass of shandy at the pub on my sixteenth birthday.
= 我十六岁生日那天，祖父让我在酒吧喝了一小杯香迪酒。
a glass of shandy = 一杯香迪酒

shanghai
/ˌʃæŋˈhaɪ/
核心：动词，及物（旧、口语）：（旧时用灌醉、暴力等手段）强迫…当水手；诱骗、强迫…做某事：shanghai somebody into doing something。
① In the nineteenth century, young men were sometimes shanghaied in port towns and woke up at sea on a ship.
= 十九世纪，年轻男子有时会在港口城镇被人强拉去当水手，醒来时已在海上的船里。
be shanghaied = 被强拉去当水手
shanghai = 强迫当水手
② My sister shanghaied me into helping at her son's birthday party all afternoon.
= 我姐姐硬拉我去她儿子的生日派对帮了一下午忙。
shanghai somebody into doing something = 硬拉某人做某事

shank
/ʃæŋk/
核心：名词，可数：（工具的）柄、杆；（动物的）小腿、胫肉：lamb shank；（人的）小腿（旧、幽默）。
① My grandmother cooked lamb shanks slowly for five hours and served them with potatoes for Sunday lunch.
= 奶奶把羊小腿慢炖了五个小时，星期天午饭配土豆端上桌。
lamb shanks = 羊小腿
shank = 胫肉
② The shank of the old key was bent, so it would not turn in the lock.
= 那把旧钥匙的柄弯了，在锁里转不动。
the shank of a key = 钥匙柄

shanty
/ˈʃænti/
核心：名词，可数：简陋的小屋、棚屋；（英式）水手号子、船歌：a sea shanty。
① Thousands of families live in shanties made of wood and metal sheets on the edge of the city.
= 成千上万的家庭住在城市边缘用木头和铁皮搭成的棚屋里。
shanties = 棚屋
shanty = 简陋小屋
② The shanty town grew quickly as people came from the countryside to look for work in the factories.
= 随着人们从农村来工厂找活干，那片棚户区迅速扩大。
a shanty town = 棚户区
③ The fishermen sang an old sea shanty as they pulled their boats up onto the beach.
= 渔民们唱着古老的水手号子，把船拖上海滩。
a sea shanty = 水手号子

shape
/ʃeɪp/
核心：名词：形状、外形（可数或不可数）：in the shape of something；（不可数）（身体的）状况、状态：in shape、out of shape、in good shape；（事物的）性质、特点：the shape of things to come；（模糊的）身影。动词，及物：使成形、塑造：shape something into something；影响、决定：shape somebody's life；（不及物）进展、好转。
① The cake was in the shape of a heart, with red icing on the top.
= 那个蛋糕是心形的，上面有红色糖霜。
shape = 形状
in the shape of something = 呈某物的形状
② My aunt goes running in the park every morning before work to stay in shape.
= 我姑姑每天早上上班前都去公园跑步，保持身材。
in shape = 身材好、状态好
③ I was completely out of shape after the winter and could not run for more than five minutes.
= 过了一个冬天我身体完全走样了，跑不了五分钟就不行了。
out of shape = 身体走样、状态差
④ The old car is still in good shape, so we are not going to sell it yet.
= 那辆旧车车况还很好，所以我们暂时不打算卖。
in good shape = 状况良好
⑤ After months of meetings with the families in the area, the plans for the new park are slowly taking shape.
= 和当地居民开了几个月的会之后，新公园的规划正慢慢成形。
take shape = 成形
⑥ The children shaped the wet sand into a castle with four towers.
= 孩子们把湿沙堆成一座有四个塔楼的城堡。
shape something into something = 把某物做成某形状
⑦ The ten years she spent as a nurse in Africa shaped the way she thinks about poverty.
= 她在非洲当护士的那十年塑造了她对贫困的看法。
shape something = 影响某事
⑧ The new players are shaping up well, and the coach thinks the team can win the league.
= 新球员表现越来越好，教练认为球队能夺得联赛冠军。
shape up = 进展良好
⑨ In the old stories, a shape-shifter can turn itself into a wolf or a bird whenever it likes.
= 在古老的故事里，变形者想变成狼或鸟随时都能变。
a shape-shifter = 变形者
⑩ The film is full of shape-shifting monsters that look like ordinary people until night falls.
= 那部电影里满是会变形的怪物，夜幕降临前看起来和普通人一样。
shape-shifting = 会变形的
⑪ The new coach promised to knock the team into shape before the first match of the season.
= 新教练保证在赛季首场比赛前把球队整顿好。
knock something into shape = 把某物整顿好
也可以说 lick / whip something into shape，意思一样。

shaped
/ʃeɪpt/
核心：形容词：（常构成复合词）有…形状的：heart-shaped、egg-shaped；（衣服）合身裁剪的。
① On Valentine's Day he left a heart-shaped box of chocolates on her desk at the office.
= 情人节那天，他在她办公室的桌上放了一盒心形巧克力。
heart-shaped = 心形的
shaped = 有…形状的
② The pool in the hotel garden is shaped like a guitar, which the children thought was very funny.
= 酒店花园里的游泳池形状像一把吉他，孩子们觉得特别好笑。
be shaped like something = 形状像某物

shapeless
/ˈʃeɪpləs/
核心：形容词：不成形的、无定形的；（衣服）不合身的、松垮的：a shapeless jumper。副词 shapelessly 是不成形地。名词 shapelessness 另立条。
① On cold winter evenings she wore a big shapeless jumper that hid her figure completely.
= 冬天寒冷的晚上，她穿一件松松垮垮的大毛衣，把身材完全遮住了。
a shapeless jumper = 松垮的毛衣
shapeless = 不成形的
② In the fog we could only see a few shapeless grey figures moving across the field.
= 雾里我们只能看见几个模模糊糊的灰色身影穿过田野。
shapeless figures = 模糊的身影
③ After his long illness, the old coat hung shapelessly from his thin body like a sack.
= 久病之后，那件旧外套像麻袋一样松垮地挂在他瘦削的身上。
shapelessly = 松垮地

shapelessness
/ˈʃeɪpləsnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：不成形、无定形；（文章、计划的）杂乱无章。
① The teacher criticized the shapelessness of my essay, which had no clear beginning or end.
= 老师批评我的作文杂乱无章，没有明确的开头和结尾。
the shapelessness of something = 某物的杂乱无章
shapelessness = 不成形
② The designer wanted to get away from the shapelessness of the baggy clothes that were popular last year.
= 那位设计师想摆脱去年流行的宽松衣服那种不成形的样子。
get away from the shapelessness of something = 摆脱某物的不成形

shapely
/ˈʃeɪpli/
核心：形容词：（人的身材、腿）匀称的、好看的；（物体）形状好看的：a shapely vase。
① The dancer had long, shapely legs and moved beautifully across the stage.
= 那位舞者双腿修长匀称，在舞台上舞姿优美。
shapely legs = 匀称的腿
shapely = 匀称的
② On the shelf stood a tall, shapely vase made of blue glass.
= 架子上立着一只高高的、造型优美的蓝玻璃花瓶。
a shapely vase = 造型优美的花瓶

shard
/ʃɑːd/
核心：名词，可数：（玻璃、陶瓷、金属的）碎片、尖片：shards of glass。也拼作 sherd（考古学多用）。
① Be careful where you walk, because there are still shards of glass on the kitchen floor.
= 走路小心点，厨房地上还有玻璃碎片。
shards of glass = 玻璃碎片
shard = 碎片
② The archaeologists found hundreds of pottery sherds in the field, some of them two thousand years old.
= 考古学家在那片田里发现了几百块陶片，有些已有两千年历史。
pottery sherds = 陶片

share
/ʃeə(r)/
核心：动词：共用、合用：share a room；分享、分摊：share something with somebody、share the cost；分担（责任、工作）；有同样的（看法、感受）：share a view；（向别人）讲述、透露（经历、消息）。名词：（一）份、份额（单数）：a share of something、your fair share；（公司的）股份、股票（可数）：buy shares in a company；（对某事的）贡献、作用（单数）。
① I share a bedroom with my little brother, and he snores all night.
= 我和弟弟共用一间卧室，他整夜打呼噜。
share = 共用
share a bedroom = 合住一间卧室
② She shared her sandwiches with the new girl who had forgotten her lunch.
= 她把三明治分给了那个忘带午饭的新来的女孩。
share something with somebody = 与某人分享
③ We shared the cost of the taxi, so it was only five pounds each.
= 我们平摊了出租车费，所以每人只花了五英镑。
share the cost = 分摊费用
④ I don't share your view that the new road will help the town.
= 我不同意你认为新公路会对小镇有利的看法。
share a view = 持相同看法
⑤ Everyone in the family should do their fair share of the housework.
= 家里每个人都该做自己应分担的那份家务。
your fair share = 应得的一份
share = 份额
⑥ He bought shares in the company when it was small, and now they are worth a fortune.
= 他在那家公司还小的时候买了它的股票，现在值一大笔钱了。
buy shares in a company = 买某公司的股票
⑦ The elder brother took the lion's share of the money, and his sisters got very little.
= 哥哥拿走了大部分钱，几个妹妹分得很少。
the lion's share = 最大的一份
⑧ At the end of the meeting, she shared some good news with the team.
= 会议结束时，她跟团队分享了一些好消息。
share news with somebody = 和某人分享消息
⑨ The share index fell by five per cent in one day, and many people lost money.
= 股票指数一天之内跌了百分之五，很多人亏了钱。
the share index = 股票指数
⑩ As part of his pay, the manager was given share options that he could use after three years.
= 作为薪酬的一部分，经理获得了三年后可以行使的股票期权。
share options = 股票期权
⑪ After the robbery, the gang met in a garage for the share-out of the money.
= 抢劫之后，那伙人在一个车库里碰头分赃。
the share-out = 分配、分赃
⑫ The sharing economy lets people rent out their cars, spare rooms and tools to strangers through apps.
= 共享经济让人们通过应用程序把汽车、空房间和工具租给陌生人。
the sharing economy = 共享经济
⑬ "Share and share alike," said our mother as she cut the cake into four equal pieces.
= 妈妈一边把蛋糕切成四等份，一边说："大家平分。"
share and share alike = 平均分享

sharecropper
/ˈʃeəkrɒpə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（尤美式）：佃农（用部分收成抵地租的农民）。
① His grandfather was a sharecropper in Mississippi who gave half of every cotton crop to the landowner.
= 他祖父是密西西比州的佃农，每季棉花都要交一半给地主。
a sharecropper = 佃农
sharecropper = 佃农
② Life was very hard for sharecroppers, who were often in debt to the landowner all their lives.
= 佃农的生活十分艰辛，他们常常一辈子都欠着地主的债。
sharecroppers = 佃农（复数）

shareholder
/ˈʃeəhəʊldə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：股东、股票持有人。
① The shareholders were angry when the company paid the boss a huge bonus but made a loss.
= 公司亏损却给老板发了巨额奖金，股东们很气愤。
shareholders = 股东
shareholder = 股票持有人
② As the largest shareholder, she has the final say on who runs the business.
= 作为最大的股东，她对谁来经营公司有最终决定权。
the largest shareholder = 最大股东

shareholding
/ˈʃeəhəʊldɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数：（持有的）股份、持股数额。
① The bank sold its shareholding in the airline for two hundred million pounds.
= 那家银行以两亿英镑卖掉了它在那家航空公司的股份。
a shareholding in something = 在某公司的股份
shareholding = 持股
② His family has a large shareholding in the football club, so he gets free tickets to every match.
= 他家在那家足球俱乐部持有大量股份，所以他每场比赛都有免费票。
a large shareholding = 大量股份

shareware
/ˈʃeəweə(r)/
核心：名词，不可数：共享软件（可免费试用、满意后再付费的软件）。
① In the 1990s, many people downloaded games as shareware and paid only if they liked them.
= 二十世纪九十年代，很多人把游戏当共享软件下载，喜欢才付钱。
shareware = 共享软件
② The program started as shareware, but now you have to buy it before you can use it.
= 那个程序一开始是共享软件，但现在得先买才能用。
start as shareware = 起初是共享软件

sharia
/ʃəˈriːə/
核心：名词，不可数：伊斯兰教法（以《古兰经》和圣训为依据的法律体系）。也写作 shariah。
① In some countries, family matters such as marriage and divorce are decided under sharia law.
= 在一些国家，婚姻、离婚等家庭事务按伊斯兰教法裁决。
sharia law = 伊斯兰教法
sharia = 伊斯兰教法
② The bank offers savings accounts that follow the rules of sharia, so no interest is paid.
= 那家银行提供遵循伊斯兰教法规定的储蓄账户，所以不支付利息。
the rules of sharia = 伊斯兰教法的规定

shark
/ʃɑːk/
核心：名词，可数：鲨鱼；（口语）骗子、敲竹杠的人：a loan shark（放高利贷者）。
① The beach was closed for a day after someone saw a shark near the swimmers.
= 有人在游泳者附近看到一条鲨鱼后，海滩关闭了一天。
a shark = 鲨鱼
shark = 鲨鱼
② He borrowed money from a loan shark and soon owed three times as much as he had borrowed.
= 他向放高利贷的人借了钱，很快就欠下了借款的三倍。
a loan shark = 放高利贷者
③ Be careful of the sharks who sell cheap holidays that do not really exist.
= 当心那些卖根本不存在的廉价度假套餐的骗子。
sharks = 骗子

sharp
/ʃɑːp/
核心：形容词：锋利的、尖的：a sharp knife；（变化）急剧的、大幅的：a sharp rise；（转弯）急的：a sharp bend；（疼痛）剧烈的、刺痛的：a sharp pain；（头脑、眼睛）敏锐的、灵敏的：a sharp mind；（话语）尖刻的、严厉的：a sharp tongue；（图像）清晰的；（对比）鲜明的；（音乐）升半音的。副词：（时间）整：at seven o'clock sharp。副词 sharply 是急剧地、严厉地。习语：look sharp 是赶快。
① Be careful when you wash that new kitchen knife, because it is very sharp.
= 洗那把新菜刀时要小心，它非常锋利。
sharp = 锋利的
sharp = 锋利
反义对照：
blunt = 钝
② The meeting starts at nine o'clock sharp, so please don't be late.
= 会议九点整开始，所以请别迟到。
sharp = 整（时间）
at ten o'clock sharp = 十点整
③ There has been a sharp rise in the price of petrol this month.
= 这个月汽油价格大幅上涨。
a sharp rise = 急剧上升
④ Slow down, there is a sharp bend in the road just after the bridge.
= 慢点，过了桥马上就有一个急弯。
a sharp bend = 急弯
⑤ I felt a sharp pain in my knee when I jumped off the wall.
= 我从墙上跳下来时，膝盖一阵刺痛。
a sharp pain = 剧痛
⑥ My grandmother is ninety, but she still has a very sharp mind and beats us all at cards.
= 我奶奶九十岁了，但头脑仍然非常敏锐，打牌把我们都赢了。
a sharp mind = 敏锐的头脑
⑦ She has a sharp tongue, and her comments often hurt people's feelings.
= 她说话尖刻，评论常常伤人。
a sharp tongue = 刻薄的嘴
⑧ A sharp-eyed shop assistant noticed that the banknote was not real and called the police.
= 一位眼尖的店员发现那张钞票是假的，就报了警。
sharp-eyed = 眼尖的
⑨ Food prices in the small shops fell sharply after the new supermarket opened in town.
= 新超市在镇上开张后，小店里的食品价格大幅下跌。
sharply = 急剧地
⑩ "Where have you been?" his father asked sharply when he came home at midnight.
= 他半夜回到家时，父亲严厉地问："你去哪儿了？"
sharply = 严厉地
⑪ Look sharp! The bus leaves in two minutes and we still have to buy tickets.
= 快点！公交车两分钟后就开了，我们还得买票。
look sharp = 赶快
⑫ The photos from the new camera are much sharper than the ones from my old phone.
= 新相机拍的照片比我旧手机拍的清晰得多。
be sharper than something = 比某物更清晰

sharpen
/ˈʃɑːpən/
核心：动词：（使）变锋利、削尖：sharpen a knife、sharpen a pencil；（使）增强、提高（技能、意识）：sharpen your skills；（使）变得清晰、尖锐。
① He sharpened the old kitchen knives on a stone before he started cutting the meat.
= 开始切肉之前，他在石头上把旧菜刀磨快了。
sharpen a knife = 磨刀
sharpen = 使锋利
② Can I borrow your knife to sharpen my pencil? The point has broken again.
= 能借你的小刀削一下铅笔吗？笔尖又断了。
sharpen a pencil = 削铅笔
③ She took an evening course to sharpen her computer skills before she applied for the job.
= 申请那份工作之前，她上了一门夜校课程来提高电脑技能。
sharpen your skills = 提高技能

sharpener
/ˈʃɑːpnə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：削尖器、磨具：a pencil sharpener、a knife sharpener。
① Every child in the class had a pencil, a rubber and a pencil sharpener in their bag.
= 班上每个孩子的书包里都有一支铅笔、一块橡皮和一个卷笔刀。
a pencil sharpener = 卷笔刀
sharpener = 削尖器
② The chef uses a steel knife sharpener before he cuts the vegetables every morning.
= 那位厨师每天早上切菜之前都用钢制磨刀棒磨刀。
a knife sharpener = 磨刀器

sharpish
/ˈʃɑːpɪʃ/
核心：副词（英式口语）：赶快、马上；形容词：相当锐利的、有点急的。
① You'd better get home sharpish, because your mother is waiting for you with dinner.
= 你最好赶快回家，你妈妈等着你吃晚饭呢。
sharpish = 赶快
② The path turns sharpish to the left just after the old farm, so watch for the sign.
= 那条小路过了老农场就急转向左，所以留意路标。
turn sharpish = 急转弯

sharpness
/ˈʃɑːpnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：锋利；（图像的）清晰度；（头脑的）敏锐；（言语的）尖刻。
① Test the sharpness of the blade on a piece of paper, not on your finger.
= 拿一张纸试刀刃锋不锋利，别用手指试。
the sharpness of the blade = 刀刃的锋利
sharpness = 锋利
② The new screen has amazing sharpness, so you can see every leaf on the trees in the film.
= 新屏幕清晰度惊人，电影里树上每一片叶子都看得清。
amazing sharpness = 惊人的清晰度
③ The sharpness of her reply to his joke surprised everyone at the dinner table.
= 她对他那个玩笑的回答那么尖刻，让饭桌上的人都吃了一惊。
the sharpness of a reply = 回答的尖刻

sharpshooter
/ˈʃɑːpʃuːtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：神枪手、狙击手。
① The army sent a sharpshooter to protect the president during his visit to the city.
= 总统访问那座城市期间，军方派了一名狙击手保护他。
a sharpshooter = 神枪手
sharpshooter = 狙击手
② At the fair, a sharpshooter hit a coin thrown into the air from twenty metres away.
= 集市上，一名神枪手在二十米外击中了抛向空中的一枚硬币。
a sharpshooter hit something = 神枪手击中某物

shatter
/ˈʃætə(r)/
核心：动词：（使）粉碎、破碎：shatter into pieces；（使）破灭、毁掉（希望、信心）：shatter somebody's hopes；（使）极度震惊、悲痛。
① The glass fell off the table and shattered into a hundred tiny pieces.
= 玻璃杯从桌上掉下来，摔成了上百块碎片。
shatter into pieces = 摔得粉碎
shatter = 粉碎
② The bomb shattered every window in the street, but luckily nobody was hurt.
= 那颗炸弹震碎了整条街的每一扇窗户，所幸没人受伤。
shatter a window = 震碎窗户
③ The injury shattered his hopes of playing for his country at the World Cup.
= 那次受伤让他代表国家参加世界杯的希望破灭了。
shatter somebody's hopes = 使某人的希望破灭
④ Car windscreens are made of shatter-proof glass, so they do not break into sharp pieces in an accident.
= 汽车挡风玻璃用的是防碎玻璃，出事故时不会碎成尖利的碎片。
shatter-proof = 防碎的

shattered
/ˈʃætəd/
核心：形容词：极度震惊的、悲痛欲绝的；（英式口语）筋疲力尽的。
① She was shattered when she heard that her best friend had been killed in a car crash.
= 听说最好的朋友在车祸中丧生，她悲痛欲绝。
be shattered = 悲痛欲绝
shattered = 极度震惊的
② After a twelve-hour shift at the hospital, the nurse was absolutely shattered and fell asleep on the bus.
= 在医院值了十二个小时的班后，那位护士累得筋疲力尽，在公交车上睡着了。
absolutely shattered = 累垮了

shattering
/ˈʃætərɪŋ/
核心：形容词：令人极度震惊的、打击巨大的：a shattering experience；（英式口语）令人筋疲力尽的。副词 shatteringly 是令人震惊地、极其。
① Losing the family business after forty years was a shattering experience for my grandfather.
= 经营了四十年的家族生意倒闭，对我祖父是个沉重的打击。
a shattering experience = 沉重的打击
shattering = 令人震惊的
② The final exams were shatteringly difficult, and half the students failed at least one paper.
= 期末考试难得惊人，一半学生至少挂了一门。
shatteringly = 极其

shave
/ʃeɪv/
核心：动词：刮（胡子、毛发）、剃：shave your legs、shave your beard；刮胡子；（从某物上）削下薄片；（稍微）削减：shave a few seconds off a record。名词，可数：刮脸、剃须：have a shave。习语：a close shave 是侥幸脱险。
① My father shaves in the bathroom every morning before breakfast, even at the weekend.
= 我父亲每天早饭前都在浴室刮胡子，周末也一样。
shave = 刮胡子
② He decided to shave off his beard for the summer, and his children did not recognize him.
= 他决定夏天把胡子刮掉，孩子们都认不出他了。
shave off a beard = 刮掉胡子
shave off = 刮掉
③ That was a close shave! The car missed us by a few centimetres.
= 好险！那辆车离我们只差几厘米。
a close shave = 侥幸脱险
④ Put some shaving cream on your face first, or the razor will hurt your skin.
= 先往脸上抹点剃须膏，不然剃刀会伤皮肤。
shaving cream = 剃须膏
⑤ He bought a can of shaving foam and a new razor at the chemist's.
= 他在药店买了一罐剃须泡沫和一把新剃刀。
shaving foam = 剃须泡沫
⑥ The new runner shaved two seconds off the world record in the final.
= 那名新秀在决赛中把世界纪录缩短了两秒。
shave something off a record = 把纪录缩短若干
⑦ The interview went well because he arrived clean-shaven, in a neat suit and on time.
= 面试很顺利，因为他到的时候胡子刮得干干净净，穿着整洁的西装，还很准时。
clean-shaven = 刮得干干净净的
⑧ I didn't have time for a shave this morning, so I look a bit untidy.
= 我今天早上没时间刮脸，所以看起来有点邋遢。
have a shave = 刮脸

shaven
/ˈʃeɪvn/
核心：形容词：剃过的、刮过的：a shaven head。
① The monks who walked past the temple gate wore orange robes and had shaven heads.
= 从寺庙门口走过的那些僧人身穿橙色袍子，剃着光头。
a shaven head = 剃光的头
shaven = 剃过的
② The young soldiers all had freshly shaven faces on the morning of the parade.
= 阅兵那天早上，年轻士兵们个个脸刮得干干净净。
freshly shaven = 刚刮过的

shaver
/ˈʃeɪvə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：电动剃须刀；（旧、口语）小家伙。
① For his eighteenth birthday, I bought my brother an electric shaver and a bottle of aftershave.
= 我弟弟十八岁生日时，我给他买了一个电动剃须刀和一瓶须后水。
an electric shaver = 电动剃须刀
shaver = 剃须刀
② The old farmer smiled at the little boy and said, "You're a cheeky young shaver, aren't you?"
= 老农场主冲小男孩笑笑说："你这个小家伙还挺调皮，是不是？"
a young shaver = 小家伙

Shavian
/ˈʃeɪviən/
核心：形容词：（剧作家）萧伯纳的、萧伯纳风格的（机智、爱唱反调）。名词，可数：萧伯纳的崇拜者、研究者。
① The play is full of Shavian wit, with clever characters who argue about money and morals.
= 那出戏充满萧伯纳式的机智，人物个个聪明，争论金钱与道德。
Shavian wit = 萧伯纳式的机智
Shavian = 萧伯纳的
② The old professor was a keen Shavian who had read every play Bernard Shaw ever wrote.
= 那位老教授是萧伯纳的狂热崇拜者，读过萧伯纳写的每一部剧本。
a Shavian = 萧伯纳的崇拜者

shavings
/ˈʃeɪvɪŋz/
核心：名词，复数：刨花、（削下的）碎片：wood shavings、chocolate shavings。
① The floor of the workshop was covered in wood shavings from the carpenter's work.
= 工作间的地上铺满了木匠干活刨下的刨花。
wood shavings = 刨花
shavings = 碎片
② She decorated the top of the cake with chocolate shavings and fresh strawberries.
= 她在蛋糕顶上撒了巧克力碎屑和新鲜草莓作装饰。
chocolate shavings = 巧克力碎屑

Shavuoth
/ʃəˈvuːəs/
核心：名词，不可数：七七节、五旬节（犹太教节日，纪念上帝在西奈山颁布律法）。也写作 Shavuot。
① During Shavuoth, many Jewish families eat dairy foods such as cheesecake and stay up late reading.
= 七七节期间，很多犹太家庭吃奶制品，比如奶酪蛋糕，还熬夜读经。
Shavuoth = 七七节
② Every spring, the synagogue in our street is decorated with flowers and green branches for Shavuoth.
= 每年春天，我们街上的犹太会堂都会为七七节装饰鲜花和绿枝。
for Shavuoth = 为七七节

shawarma
/ʃəˈwɑːmə/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：沙威玛（中东烤肉卷：把烤肉切片夹在饼里）。
① After the concert we bought chicken shawarma from a little shop that stayed open until three in the morning.
= 音乐会结束后，我们在一家开到凌晨三点的小店买了鸡肉沙威玛。
chicken shawarma = 鸡肉沙威玛
shawarma = 沙威玛
② The man cut thin slices of meat from the turning shawarma and wrapped them in warm bread.
= 那人从转动的烤肉上切下薄片，用热乎乎的饼卷起来。
the turning shawarma = 转动的沙威玛烤肉

shawl
/ʃɔːl/
核心：名词，可数：披肩、围巾。
① My grandmother always wears a woollen shawl around her shoulders in the evenings.
= 我奶奶晚上总在肩上披一条羊毛披肩。
a woollen shawl = 羊毛披肩
shawl = 披肩
② The baby was wrapped in a white shawl that her great-grandmother had knitted.
= 宝宝裹在一条她曾祖母织的白色披肩里。
be wrapped in a shawl = 裹在披肩里

Shawnee
/ʃɔːˈniː/
核心：名词，可数（复数 Shawnee 或 Shawnees）：肖尼人（北美原住民的一支，原居俄亥俄河谷一带）；（不可数）肖尼语。
① The Shawnee lived in the Ohio Valley before they were forced to move west.
= 肖尼人原先住在俄亥俄河谷，后来被迫西迁。
the Shawnee = 肖尼人
Shawnee = 肖尼人
② Only a few hundred people still speak Shawnee, so there are classes to teach it to children.
= 现在只有几百人还会说肖尼语，所以开了课教孩子们。
speak Shawnee = 说肖尼语

she
/ʃi/（弱）/ʃiː/（强）
核心：代词（主格）：她；（指雌性动物、船、国家等）它。缩略式 she'd（she had / she would）、she'll（she will）、she's（she is / she has）。名词，单数（口语）：女的、雌的：Is it a he or a she?
① My sister is a doctor, and she works at the hospital in the city centre.
= 我姐姐是医生，她在市中心的医院工作。
she = 她
② We loved our old boat; she sailed beautifully, even in bad weather.
= 我们很喜欢那条旧船，就算天气不好它也航行得很漂亮。
she = （指船）它
③ She'd already left the house by the time I arrived, so I phoned her.
= 我到的时候她已经出门了，所以我给她打了电话。
she'd = she had
④ Ask Mary to help with the garden; I'm sure she'll say yes.
= 叫玛丽来帮忙收拾花园吧，我肯定她会答应的。
she'll = she will
⑤ She's been working in Paris for two years, and she's very happy there.
= 她在巴黎工作两年了，在那里过得很开心。
she's = she has / she is
⑥ "Is the new puppy a he or a she?" "A she, and we've called her Daisy."
= "那只新来的小狗是公的还是母的？""母的，我们叫它黛西。"
a she = 雌的
⑦ The newspapers called her a she-devil, because she was so cruel to the people who worked for her.
= 报纸称她为母夜叉，因为她对手下的人十分刻薄。
a she-devil = 母夜叉、悍妇

sheaf
/ʃiːf/
核心：名词，可数（复数 sheaves）：（文件、纸张的）一叠、一沓：a sheaf of papers；（谷物的）一捆：a sheaf of wheat。
① The lawyer came into the room carrying a thick sheaf of papers.
= 律师抱着厚厚一叠文件走进房间。
a sheaf of papers = 一叠文件
sheaf = 一叠
② In the old days, farmers tied the wheat into sheaves and left them in the fields to dry.
= 过去，农民把麦子捆成一捆捆的，放在地里晾干。
sheaves of wheat = 一捆捆麦子

shear
/ʃɪə(r)/
核心：动词（sheared, sheared 或 shorn）：剪（羊毛）：shear sheep；（书面）剪掉（头发）；（金属）断裂、剪断：shear off。be shorn of something 是被剥夺某物。
① The farmer shears his sheep every summer and sells the wool at the market.
= 那个农场主每年夏天剪羊毛，拿到集市上卖。
shear sheep = 剪羊毛
shear = 剪（羊毛）
② The sheep looked very small and white after they had been shorn.
= 羊毛剪掉以后，羊看起来又小又白。
be shorn = 被剪了毛
③ One of the bolts sheared off, and the wheel came off the trailer on the motorway.
= 有一颗螺栓断了，拖车的轮子在高速公路上掉了下来。
shear off = 断裂脱落
④ The president was shorn of most of his powers after the new constitution was passed.
= 新宪法通过之后，总统的大部分权力被剥夺了。
be shorn of something = 被剥夺某物

shears
/ʃɪəz/
核心：名词，复数：大剪刀、园艺剪：a pair of shears、garden shears。
① He cut the long grass around the trees with a pair of garden shears.
= 他用一把园艺剪剪掉了树周围的长草。
garden shears = 园艺剪
shears = 大剪刀
② The farmer used a pair of electric shears to take the wool off the sheep in a few minutes.
= 那个农场主用电动剪子几分钟就把羊毛剪了下来。
a pair of electric shears = 一把电动剪

shearwater
/ˈʃɪəwɔːtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：鹱、剪水鹱（一种翅膀长而窄、贴着海面飞的海鸟）。
① From the ferry we watched hundreds of shearwaters flying low over the waves.
= 我们从渡轮上看着几百只剪水鹱贴着浪头低飞。
shearwaters = 剪水鹱
shearwater = 鹱
② The island is an important home for the Manx shearwater, which nests in holes in the ground.
= 那座岛是大西洋鹱的重要栖息地，它们在地上的洞里筑巢。
the Manx shearwater = 大西洋鹱

sheath
/ʃiːθ/
核心：名词，可数：（刀、剑的）鞘；（电线、植物等的）护套、外皮；紧身连衣裙：a sheath dress；（英式）避孕套（旧）。
① The hunter pulled the knife out of its leather sheath and cut the rope.
= 猎人把刀从皮鞘里拔出来，割断了绳子。
a leather sheath = 皮鞘
sheath = 刀鞘
② The cable has a plastic sheath to protect the wires inside from water.
= 电缆外有一层塑料护套，保护里面的电线不进水。
a plastic sheath = 塑料护套
③ He carried a sheath knife on his belt whenever he went fishing in the mountains.
= 他每次去山里钓鱼都在腰带上挂一把带鞘的刀。
a sheath knife = 带鞘的刀
④ She wore a black silk sheath dress to the dinner, with a simple gold necklace.
= 她穿了一条黑色丝绸紧身连衣裙赴宴，戴着一条简单的金项链。
a sheath dress = 紧身连衣裙

sheathe
/ʃiːð/
核心：动词，及物：把（刀、剑）插入鞘；（用东西）包住、覆盖：be sheathed in something。
① When the enemy ran away, the soldier sheathed his sword and walked slowly back to the camp.
= 敌人逃跑后，那名士兵收剑入鞘，慢慢走回营地。
sheathe a sword = 收剑入鞘
sheathe = 插入鞘
② The new tower is sheathed in glass, so it shines like a mirror in the sun.
= 新大楼外面包着一层玻璃，在阳光下像镜子一样闪亮。
be sheathed in something = 被某物包住

shebang
/ʃɪˈbæŋ/
核心：名词（口语）：the whole shebang 是所有的一切、全部东西。
① They sold the house, the car, the furniture, the whole shebang, and moved to a small island in Greece.
= 他们卖掉了房子、汽车、家具，所有的一切，搬到了希腊的一座小岛上。
the whole shebang = 所有的一切
shebang = 全部东西
② The wedding had a band, fireworks, a horse and carriage: the whole shebang cost more than a new car.
= 那场婚礼有乐队、烟花、马车，这一整套花的钱比一辆新车还多。
the whole shebang cost = 这一整套花费

shed
/ʃed/
核心：名词，可数：（花园里的）小棚屋、工具房：a garden shed；（工厂、农场的）大棚、库房。动词（shed, shed）：（树）落（叶）、（动物）蜕（皮）、掉（毛）：shed leaves；去掉、摆脱：shed weight、shed jobs；流（泪、血）：shed tears；（光）照射：shed light on something；（车）掉落（货物）。
① We keep the lawnmower and the bikes in a wooden shed at the bottom of the garden.
= 我们把割草机和自行车放在花园尽头的木棚里。
a garden shed = 花园工具房
shed = 小棚屋
② In autumn the trees in the park shed their leaves, and the paths turn yellow and red.
= 秋天公园里的树纷纷落叶，小路变得一片黄一片红。
shed leaves = 落叶
③ Every spring our old dog sheds hair all over the sofa, and I have to brush it twice a day.
= 每年春天我们家的老狗都掉得满沙发是毛，我一天得刷两次。
shed hair = 掉毛
④ He wants to shed a few kilos before his holiday, so he has stopped eating cakes.
= 他想在度假前减掉几公斤，所以不吃蛋糕了。
shed a few kilos = 减掉几公斤
⑤ The factory has shed three hundred jobs this year because orders have fallen.
= 由于订单减少，那家工厂今年裁掉了三百个岗位。
shed jobs = 裁员
⑥ Nobody shed a tear when the unpopular manager finally left the company.
= 那个不得人心的经理终于离开公司时，没有一个人掉眼泪。
shed a tear = 掉眼泪
⑦ The old letters shed new light on the poet's life and her love for the painter.
= 那些旧信件让人对那位诗人的一生和她对那位画家的爱有了新的认识。
shed light on something = 使人了解某事
⑧ A lorry shed its load of oranges on the motorway, and traffic stopped for two hours.
= 一辆卡车在高速公路上掉落了一车橙子，交通中断了两个小时。
shed its load = 掉落货物

shedload
/ˈʃedləʊd/
核心：名词（英式口语）：大量、一大堆：a shedload of something、shedloads of money。
① The film made a shedload of money, even though the critics hated it.
= 那部电影赚了一大笔钱，尽管评论家都很讨厌它。
a shedload of something = 一大堆某物
shedload = 大量
② We have got shedloads of apples from the tree this year, so please take some home with you.
= 今年树上结了一大堆苹果，请带些回家吧。
shedloads of something = 大量某物

sheen
/ʃiːn/
核心：名词，单数或不可数：（表面的）光泽、光彩：a healthy sheen；（比喻）表面光彩：a sheen of respectability。
① The horse's coat had a beautiful sheen after the girl had brushed it for an hour.
= 那个女孩刷了一个小时之后，马的毛皮泛着漂亮的光泽。
a sheen = 光泽
sheen = 光彩
② Behind the sheen of success, the company was losing money every month.
= 在成功的表面光彩背后，那家公司每个月都在亏钱。
the sheen of success = 成功的表面光彩

sheep
/ʃiːp/
核心：名词，可数（复数 sheep）：羊、绵羊；（贬义）盲从的人：like sheep。习语：the black sheep (of the family) 是（家里的）害群之马、败家子。
① There were hundreds of sheep on the hills, and the farmer's dog brought them down to the farm.
= 山坡上有几百只羊，农场主的狗把它们赶下山回到农场。
sheep = 羊
hundreds of sheep = 几百只羊
② People followed the new fashion like sheep, without asking whether they really liked it.
= 人们像羊群一样跟风追逐新时尚，也不问问自己是不是真喜欢。
like sheep = 像羊群一样盲从
③ My uncle was the black sheep of the family, because he left school at fifteen and was always in trouble.
= 我叔叔是家里的败家子，十五岁就辍学，老是惹麻烦。
the black sheep of the family = 家里的害群之马
④ Before the sheep are sheared, the farmer pushes them through a sheep dip to kill insects in their wool.
= 剪羊毛之前，农场主把羊赶进药浴池，杀死羊毛里的虫子。
a sheep dip = 羊药浴池

sheepdog
/ˈʃiːpdɒɡ/
核心：名词，可数：牧羊犬。
① The farmer whistled, and the sheepdog ran around the field and brought all the sheep to the gate.
= 农场主吹了声口哨，牧羊犬就绕着田野跑，把所有的羊赶到门口。
the sheepdog = 牧羊犬
sheepdog = 牧羊犬
② Every summer there are sheepdog trials in the village, where farmers show how well their dogs work.
= 每年夏天村里都有牧羊犬比赛，农场主们展示自己的狗干活有多出色。
sheepdog trials = 牧羊犬比赛

sheepfold
/ˈʃiːpfəʊld/
核心：名词，可数：羊圈。
① At night the shepherd kept his flock in a stone sheepfold on the side of the hill.
= 夜里那个牧羊人把羊群关在山坡上一个石头砌的羊圈里。
a sheepfold = 羊圈
sheepfold = 羊圈
② The walls of the old sheepfold had fallen down, and only a few stones were left in the grass.
= 老羊圈的墙已经倒塌，草丛里只剩下几块石头。
the old sheepfold = 老羊圈

sheepherder
/ˈʃiːphɜːdə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（美式）：牧羊人。
① The sheepherder lived alone in the mountains of Idaho for six months every year with two thousand sheep.
= 那位牧羊人每年有六个月独自带着两千只羊住在爱达荷州的山里。
a sheepherder = 牧羊人
sheepherder = 牧羊人
② In the old western films, the cattle ranchers and the sheepherders were always fighting over land.
= 在老西部片里，养牛的牧场主和牧羊人总是为了土地打架。
the sheepherders = 牧羊人们

sheepish
/ˈʃiːpɪʃ/
核心：形容词：（因做了蠢事而）难为情的、窘迫的：a sheepish smile。副词 sheepishly 是难为情地。
① He gave me a sheepish smile when I found him eating the last piece of cake.
= 我撞见他在吃最后一块蛋糕时，他难为情地冲我一笑。
a sheepish smile = 难为情的笑
sheepish = 难为情的
② "I think I left the keys in the car," she said sheepishly, as we stood outside the locked door.
= 我们站在锁着的门外时，她难为情地说："我想我把钥匙忘在车里了。"
sheepishly = 难为情地

sheepskin
/ˈʃiːpskɪn/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：（带毛的）羊皮：a sheepskin coat、sheepskin boots。
① My grandfather wore a heavy sheepskin coat that kept him warm on the coldest winter days.
= 我祖父穿着一件厚重的羊皮大衣，最冷的冬天也能保暖。
a sheepskin coat = 羊皮大衣
sheepskin = 羊皮
② There was a soft white sheepskin on the floor in front of the fire, and the cat slept on it.
= 炉火前的地上铺着一张柔软的白羊皮，猫就睡在上面。
a sheepskin = 一张羊皮

sheer
/ʃɪə(r)/
核心：形容词：（只用于名词前）纯粹的、十足的：sheer luck、sheer size；（悬崖、坡）陡峭的、几乎垂直的：a sheer drop；（布料）极薄的、透明的。副词：垂直地、陡峭地。动词：（船、车）突然转向、偏离。
① It was sheer luck that nobody was hurt when the tree fell on the car.
= 树砸在车上时没人受伤，纯粹是运气。
sheer luck = 纯粹是运气
sheer = 纯粹的
② When we walked into the square, the sheer size of the cathedral took our breath away.
= 我们走进广场时，那座大教堂光是体积就让我们叹为观止。
the sheer size of something = 某物的庞大体积本身
③ From the edge of the cliff there is a sheer drop of two hundred metres into the sea.
= 从悬崖边往下，是两百米深、直落入海的陡壁。
a sheer drop = 垂直的落差
④ She wore a long dress made of sheer silk that moved in the wind.
= 她穿着一条薄如蝉翼的丝绸长裙，裙摆随风飘动。
sheer silk = 极薄的丝绸
⑤ In the strong wind, the small fishing boat sheered away from the rocks just in time.
= 大风中，那条小渔船及时转向，避开了礁石。
sheer away = 突然转向避开
⑥ The car sheered off the road and stopped in a field full of cows.
= 那辆车突然偏离公路，停在了一片满是奶牛的田里。
sheer off = 突然偏离

sheesh
/ʃiːʃ/
核心：感叹词（口语）：哎呀、真是的（表示恼火、吃惊）。
① Sheesh, it's cold out here! Why didn't anybody tell me to bring a coat?
= 哎呀，外面好冷！怎么没人叫我带件外套？
sheesh = 哎呀
② "You forgot my birthday again? Sheesh," my sister said, and walked out of the kitchen.
= "你又忘了我的生日？真是的。"我姐姐说完就走出了厨房。
sheesh = 真是的

sheet
/ʃiːt/
核心：名词，可数：床单：change the sheets；（纸的）一张、一页：a sheet of paper；（玻璃、金属等的）薄片、薄板：a sheet of glass；（水、冰、火等的）一大片：a sheet of ice；（印有信息的）单子、表格：a fact sheet。
① Could you write your name and address on this sheet of paper, please?
= 请把你的姓名和地址写在这张纸上好吗？
a sheet of paper = 一张纸
纸的 = 一张
② I change the sheets on the beds every Saturday morning and wash them in the machine.
= 我每周六早上换床单，放进洗衣机里洗。
sheet = 床单
床上的 = 床单
③ The workmen carried a large sheet of glass very carefully into the shop.
= 工人们小心翼翼地把一大块玻璃搬进店里。
a sheet of glass = 一块玻璃
④ The road was covered with a sheet of ice, and several cars slid into the ditch.
= 路面结了一层冰，好几辆车滑进了沟里。
a sheet of ice = 一层冰
⑤ The doctor gave me a fact sheet that explained how to look after my knee after the operation.
= 医生给了我一张说明单，讲解手术后怎样护理膝盖。
a fact sheet = 资料单
⑥ For the family, the money from his parents was a sheet anchor that saved them when he lost his job.
= 对这一家人来说，他父母给的钱是救命稻草，在他失业时救了他们。
a sheet anchor = 最后的依靠
⑦ On hot summer nights we sat in the garden and watched the sheet lightning far away over the hills.
= 炎热的夏夜，我们坐在花园里看远处山那边的片状闪电。
sheet lightning = 片状闪电

sheeting
/ˈʃiːtɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：（用作覆盖物的）片材、薄板：plastic sheeting、metal sheeting。
① The builders covered the roof with plastic sheeting to keep the rain out until the tiles arrived.
= 建筑工人用塑料布盖住屋顶，在瓦片运到之前挡雨。
plastic sheeting = 塑料布
sheeting = 片材
② The old barn has walls made of rusty metal sheeting that rattles in the wind.
= 那座旧谷仓的墙是生了锈的铁皮，刮风时哗哗作响。
metal sheeting = 金属板

sheikh
/ʃeɪk/
核心：名词，可数：（阿拉伯的）酋长、首领；（伊斯兰教的）长老、教长。也拼作 sheik、shaikh。
① The sheikh welcomed the visitors to his tent and offered them coffee and dates.
= 酋长欢迎客人们来到他的帐篷，请他们喝咖啡、吃椰枣。
the sheikh = 酋长
sheikh = 酋长
② The old shaikh taught the boys of the village to read the Koran every morning in the mosque.
= 那位老教长每天早上在清真寺教村里的男孩们读《古兰经》。
the old shaikh = 老教长

sheikhdom
/ˈʃeɪkdəm/

① The small sheikhdom on the coast became very rich after oil was found under the desert.
= 沙漠底下发现石油后，海岸上那个小酋长国变得非常富有。
a sheikhdom = 酋长国
sheikhdom = 酋长国
② The United Arab Emirates was formed in 1971 when several sheikhdoms joined together.
= 阿拉伯联合酋长国于 1971 年由几个酋长国联合组成。
several sheikhdoms = 几个酋长国

shekel
/ˈʃekl/
核心：名词，可数：谢克尔（以色列货币单位；古代希伯来的银币、重量单位）；（复数 shekels，口语）钱。
① A cup of coffee in the old city of Jerusalem cost us fifteen shekels.
= 在耶路撒冷老城，一杯咖啡花了我们十五谢克尔。
fifteen shekels = 十五谢克尔
shekel = 谢克尔
② He spends all his shekels on computer games and never has any money left for clothes.
= 他把钱全花在电脑游戏上，从来没钱买衣服。
his shekels = 他的钱

shelduck
/ˈʃeldʌk/
核心：名词，可数：翘鼻麻鸭（一种羽毛黑白相间、嘴红色的大型野鸭）。
① We saw a pair of shelducks feeding on the mud by the river when the tide went out.
= 退潮时我们看到一对翘鼻麻鸭在河边的泥滩上觅食。
a pair of shelducks = 一对翘鼻麻鸭
shelduck = 翘鼻麻鸭
② The shelduck has a bright red beak and a wide brown band across its white chest.
= 翘鼻麻鸭长着鲜红的嘴，白色胸前横着一条宽宽的棕色带子。
the shelduck = 翘鼻麻鸭

shelf
/ʃelf/
核心：名词，可数（复数 shelves）：架子、搁板：on the shelf、a book shelf；（海底的）大陆架、（岩石的）突出部分。习语：off the shelf 是现成的、现货供应的；on the shelf 是（被）搁置的，（旧，指女子）嫁不出去的。
① Put the books back on the shelf when you have finished reading them.
= 书看完了要放回架子上。
shelf = 架子
② The bookshelf in my bedroom is full, so I have started putting books on the floor.
= 我卧室的书架满了，所以我开始把书放在地上。
bookshelf = 书架
③ You can buy the software off the shelf in any computer shop.
= 那个软件在任何一家电脑店都能买到现货。
off the shelf = 现货供应的
④ The plan to build a new bridge has been put on the shelf because there is no money.
= 修新桥的计划因为没钱被搁置了。
on the shelf = 被搁置
⑤ Everyone bought bread and water, and the supermarket shelves were empty the day before the big storm.
= 大家都去买面包和水，大风暴来临的前一天，超市的货架都空了。
supermarket shelves = 超市货架
⑥ Fresh bread has a short shelf life, so we only buy enough for two days.
= 新鲜面包保质期短，所以我们每次只买够吃两天的。
shelf life = 保质期
多用于食品、药品，也可比喻某种东西流行、有用的期限。
⑦ He worked as a shelf-stacker in a supermarket at night while he was studying at university.
= 他上大学期间晚上在一家超市当理货员。
a shelf-stacker = 理货员

shell
/ʃel/
核心：名词：（蛋、坚果、贝类、龟等的）壳（可数或不可数）：an egg shell、a sea shell；（建筑物、车辆的）框架、外壳；炮弹（可数）；（比喻）（人的）拘谨、腼腆。动词，及物：剥（壳）；炮击：shell a town；（口语）（不情愿地）掏钱。习语：come out of your shell 是变得开朗、不再拘谨。
① The children collected shells on the beach and took them home in a bucket.
= 孩子们在海滩上捡贝壳，装在小桶里带回家。
shell = 贝壳
collect shells on the beach = 在海滩上捡贝壳
② She was very shy at first, but she came out of her shell after a few weeks at the new school.
= 她一开始很害羞，但在新学校待了几周后就开朗起来了。
come out of one's shell = 变得开朗
③ After you crack the walnuts for the cake, throw the shells into the bin under the sink.
= 给蛋糕敲好核桃之后，把壳扔进水槽下面的垃圾桶。
crack nuts = 敲开坚果
词族：
nutshell = 坚果壳
④ In a nutshell, the plan failed because there was not enough money to finish it.
= 简而言之，那个计划失败了，因为钱不够，完不成。
in a nutshell = 简而言之
⑤ After the fire, only the shell of the old theatre was left standing.
= 大火之后，那座老剧院只剩下一个空架子。
the shell of a building = 建筑物的空壳
⑥ The town was hit by hundreds of shells during the war, and few buildings survived.
= 战争期间那个小镇遭到几百发炮弹轰击，没几座房子幸存。
shells = 炮弹
⑦ The army shelled the city for three days before the soldiers went in.
= 军队炮击那座城市三天后，士兵们才开进去。
shell a city = 炮击城市
⑧ On summer evenings we sat at the kitchen table shelling peas for my grandmother's soup.
= 夏天傍晚，我们坐在厨房桌边，给奶奶煮汤剥豌豆。
shell peas = 剥豌豆
⑨ I had to shell out two hundred pounds to get the car repaired.
= 我只好掏了两百英镑修车。
shell out = 掏钱
⑩ After a year at the front, many soldiers came home suffering from shell shock and could not sleep.
= 在前线待了一年之后，很多士兵回家时患上了炮弹休克症，睡不着觉。
shell shock = 炮弹休克症（战争创伤）
⑪ The survivors of the earthquake sat in the street in their pyjamas, looking shell-shocked.
= 地震幸存者穿着睡衣坐在街上，神情恍惚。
shell-shocked = 惊呆的、神情恍惚的
⑫ In the 1980s, my father wore a shiny purple shell suit every weekend.
= 二十世纪八十年代，我父亲每个周末都穿一身亮紫色的尼龙运动服。
a shell suit = （尼龙）运动套装
⑬ The man on the street corner was playing a shell game, hiding a pea under one of three cups and taking tourists' money.
= 街角那个人在玩猜豆子的骗局：把一颗豌豆藏在三个杯子之一底下，骗游客的钱。
a shell game = 猜豆子游戏；（比喻）骗局
⑭ "Can I have a word in your shell-like?" the coach asked, and took the young player aside.
= 教练问："能跟你说句悄悄话吗？"然后把那个年轻球员叫到一边。
a word in your shell-like = （英式幽默）跟你说句悄悄话

shellac
/ʃəˈlæk/
核心：名词，不可数：虫胶、紫胶（做清漆用）。动词（shellacked, shellacked）：给…涂虫胶漆；（美式口语）彻底击败。
① The old wooden table had been polished with shellac and shone like glass.
= 那张旧木桌用虫胶漆擦过，亮得像玻璃。
shellac = 虫胶
② Our team got shellacked eight goals to nil in the final, and we went home very quietly.
= 我们队在决赛中以零比八被打得落花流水，大家默默地回了家。

/ˈʃelfaɪə(r)/

shelter
/ˈʃeltə(r)/
核心：名词：躲避、庇护（不可数）：take shelter、find shelter；住处、栖身之所（不可数）：food and shelter；避难所、收容所（可数）：a homeless shelter、an animal shelter；遮蔽处（可数）：a bus shelter。动词：（为…）遮挡、庇护：shelter somebody from something；躲避：shelter from the rain。
① When the storm started, we took shelter in a small café by the road.
= 暴风雨一来，我们就躲进了路边一家小咖啡馆。

② We waited in the bus shelter outside the school until the heavy rain stopped.
= 我们在学校外面的公交候车亭里一直等到大雨停了。

③ On the cold hillside, the cows sheltered from the wind behind the old stone wall.
= 在寒冷的山坡上，奶牛们在旧石墙后面避风。

= 地震毁了他们的家之后，成千上万的人需要食物和住处。

shelter somebody from something = 使某人免受某事影响

shift
/ʃɪft/
核心：动词：（使）移动、挪动：shift the furniture；（看法、态度、重点）转变、改变：shift the focus；转移（责任、问题）：shift the blame onto somebody；（英式口语）去掉（污渍）；（美式）换挡。名词，可数：转变、改变：a shift in something；（轮班的）班、班次：a night shift；（同一班的）工人；（键盘上的）换挡键。
① There has been a big shift in public opinion about plastic bags in the last few years.
= 近几年公众对塑料袋的看法发生了很大转变。
shift = 转变
a shift in something = 某方面的转变
② My mother works in a hospital and often has to work the night shift.
= 我母亲在医院工作，常常得上夜班。
shift = 班次
the night shift = 夜班
③ He works on the day shift at the factory, from six in the morning until two in the afternoon.

④ Shift work makes it hard to see your friends, because you are always sleeping when they are free.
= 轮班工作让人很难和朋友见面，因为他们有空的时候你总在睡觉。
shift work = 轮班工作
⑤ Can you help me shift this sofa? I want to clean behind it.
= 你能帮我挪一下这张沙发吗？我想打扫后面。
shift something = 挪动某物
⑥ When the project failed, the manager tried to shift the blame onto his team.
= 项目失败后，经理想把责任推到他的团队身上。
shift the blame onto somebody = 把责任推给某人
⑦ I've tried everything, but I can't shift this coffee stain from the carpet.

⑧ When their parents died, the older children had to shift for themselves and look after the little ones.

⑨ In the hills, the farmers still practise shifting cultivation, clearing a new field every few years and letting the old one grow back.

① The carpenter pushed a thin wooden shim under the door frame to make it straight.
= 木匠在门框下塞了一片薄木楔，把它垫正。
a shim = 垫片
shim = 楔子
② We had to shim the washing machine because the kitchen floor was not level.
= 厨房地面不平，所以我们得给洗衣机垫上垫片。
shim something = 用垫片垫平某物

shimmer
/ˈʃɪmə(r)/
核心：动词，不及物：闪烁、发出微光：shimmer in the sunlight。名词，单数或不可数：微光、闪光。
① The lake shimmered in the early morning sun, and we could see fish jumping near the shore.
= 湖面在清晨的阳光下波光粼粼，我们能看到鱼在岸边跳跃。
shimmer = 闪烁
shimmer in the sun = 在阳光下闪烁
② On hot days the road seemed to shimmer, as if there was water lying on it.
= 炎热的日子里，路面好像在晃动发光，仿佛上面有一层水。
the road seems to shimmer = 路面似乎在晃动发光
③ There was a shimmer of silver on the sea where the moonlight touched the waves.
= 月光照在波浪上，海面泛着一片银光。
a shimmer of silver = 一片银光

shimmy
/ˈʃɪmi/
核心：动词（shimmied, shimmied）：扭动身体（跳舞）；扭着身子移动：shimmy up a tree。名词，单数：（20 世纪 20 年代的）西迷舞、扭摆舞。
① The singer shimmied across the stage in a dress covered in silver beads.
= 那位歌手穿着一条缀满银珠的裙子，扭着身子走过舞台。
shimmy across something = 扭动着穿过某处
shimmy = 扭动身体
② The boy shimmied up the drainpipe and climbed in through his bedroom window.
= 那个男孩顺着排水管扭着身子爬上去，从卧室窗户钻了进去。
shimmy up something = 扭着身子爬上某物
③ At the 1920s party, everyone tried to dance the shimmy and the Charleston.
= 在那场二十世纪二十年代主题派对上，大家都试着跳西迷舞和查尔斯顿舞。
the shimmy = 西迷舞

shin
/ʃɪn/
核心：名词，可数：胫、小腿前部：kick somebody in the shin。动词（shinned, shinned）：（用手脚）攀爬（树、绳、柱等）。
① He kicked me in the shin under the table to stop me from saying anything else.
= 他在桌子底下踢了我小腿一脚，叫我别再说了。
kick somebody in the shin = 踢某人的小腿
shin = 小腿
② The footballer broke his shin bone in a bad tackle and missed the rest of the season.
= 那名球员在一次恶意铲球中胫骨骨折，缺席了本赛季剩下的比赛。
the shin bone = 胫骨
③ All the players have to wear shin guards under their socks during the match.
= 比赛时所有球员都得在袜子里戴护胫。
shin guards = 护胫
④ After running every day on hard roads, she got shin splints and had to rest for a month.
= 每天在硬路面上跑步之后，她得了胫痛症，只好休息一个月。
shin splints = 胫痛症（胫骨疼痛）
⑤ The boy shinned up the tree to rescue the kitten that was stuck on a high branch.
= 那个男孩爬上树，去救卡在高枝上的小猫。
shin up = 爬上
⑥ When the fire alarm rang, the students shinned down a rope from the first-floor window.
= 火警响起时，学生们顺着绳子从二楼窗户滑了下来。
shin down = 顺着…滑下

shindig
/ˈʃɪndɪɡ/
核心：名词，可数（口语）：热闹的聚会、狂欢会。
① They are having a big shindig on Saturday to celebrate their twenty-fifth wedding anniversary.
= 他们星期六要办一场热闹的聚会，庆祝结婚二十五周年。
a big shindig = 热闹的聚会
shindig = 狂欢会
② The whole village came to the shindig in the barn, with music and dancing until midnight.
= 全村人都来参加谷仓里的狂欢会，唱歌跳舞一直到半夜。
the shindig = 那场狂欢会

shindy
/ˈʃɪndi/
核心：名词，单数（旧、口语）：喧闹、吵闹：kick up a shindy。
① The neighbours kicked up a shindy when the band played loud music until three in the morning.
= 乐队把音乐放得震天响直到凌晨三点，邻居们大吵大闹起来。
kick up a shindy = 大吵大闹
shindy = 喧闹
② There was a terrible shindy in the street outside the pub after the football match.
= 足球赛之后，酒吧外的街上一片喧闹。
a terrible shindy = 一片喧闹

shine
/ʃaɪn/
核心：动词（shone, shone；「擦亮」义用 shined）：照耀、发光、闪耀：the sun shines；（把灯光等）照向：shine a torch on something；（眼睛、脸）发亮、放光；出众、表现突出：shine at something；擦亮（鞋等）；（品质）显露出来。名词，单数：光泽、光亮。习语：take a shine to somebody 是一见就喜欢某人；rain or shine 是无论晴雨、不管怎样。
① The sun was shining, so we took our lunch to the park.
= 阳光明媚，所以我们把午饭带到公园去吃。
shine = 照耀
the sun shines = 阳光照耀
词族：
shiny = 闪亮的
sunshine = 阳光
② The moon shone through the window and lit up the whole bedroom.
= 月光透过窗户照进来，照亮了整间卧室。
shone = 照耀（过去式）
③ The guard shone his torch into the car to see who was inside.
= 警卫把手电照进车里，看里面坐的是谁。
shine a torch into something = 把手电照向某处
④ On her birthday morning, the little girl's eyes shone with excitement when she opened the present.
= 生日那天早上，小女孩打开礼物时兴奋得两眼放光。
eyes shine with excitement = 兴奋得两眼放光
⑤ She has always shone at maths, and now she wants to study it at university.
= 她数学一向出众，现在想在大学读数学。
shine at something = 在某方面表现突出
⑥ My grandfather sat on the back step and shined his shoes every Sunday morning before church.
= 我祖父每个星期天早上去教堂前，都坐在后门台阶上擦皮鞋。
shine shoes = 擦鞋
⑦ Even in her first small part in the film, her talent shone through.
= 即使在电影里演第一个小角色，她的才华也显露了出来。
shine through = 显露出来
⑧ The old lady took a shine to the new postman and gave him a cake every Friday.
= 那位老太太一下就喜欢上了新来的邮递员，每个星期五都送他一块蛋糕。
take a shine to somebody = 一见就喜欢某人
⑨ The market is open every Saturday, rain or shine, and it is always busy.
= 那个集市每个星期六都开，不管晴雨，总是人很多。
rain or shine = 无论晴雨
⑩ Rub the old table with a soft cloth until it has a good shine.
= 用软布擦那张旧桌子，直到擦得锃亮。
a shine = 光泽

shiner
/ˈʃaɪnə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（口语）：黑眼圈、乌眼青（被打出来的）。
① He came home from the football match with a big shiner and a cut lip.
= 他看完足球赛回家，带着一个大黑眼圈，嘴唇也破了。
a shiner = 黑眼圈
shiner = 乌眼青
② My little brother got a shiner when he walked into the door in the dark.
= 我弟弟黑暗中撞到门上，眼睛撞青了一块。
get a shiner = 眼睛被撞青

shingle
/ˈʃɪŋɡl/
核心：名词：（海滩上的）小圆石、卵石（不可数）：a shingle beach；（可数）（屋顶、墙上的）木瓦、墙面板。
① The beach is covered in shingle, so you need shoes to walk down to the sea.
= 海滩上满是卵石，所以走到海边得穿鞋。
shingle = 卵石
a shingle beach = 卵石海滩
② Some of the wooden shingles blew off the roof during the storm.
= 暴风雨中屋顶上有些木瓦被吹掉了。
wooden shingles = 木瓦

shingled
/ˈʃɪŋɡld/
核心：形容词：（屋顶、墙）铺着木瓦的。
① We stayed in a little shingled cottage by the lake, with a green door and a stone chimney.
= 我们住在湖边一座铺着木瓦的小屋里，绿色的门，石头烟囱。
a shingled cottage = 铺木瓦的小屋
shingled = 铺着木瓦的
② The church has a shingled spire that has turned silver-grey over the years.
= 那座教堂的尖塔铺着木瓦，多年下来已经变成了银灰色。
a shingled spire = 铺木瓦的尖塔

shingles
/ˈʃɪŋɡlz/
核心：名词，不可数：带状疱疹（一种沿神经出现成串水疱、非常疼痛的病毒性疾病）。
① My grandmother had shingles last winter, and the pain in her side lasted for weeks.
= 我奶奶去年冬天得了带状疱疹，腰侧疼了好几个星期。
shingles = 带状疱疹
have shingles = 得带状疱疹
② Family doctors in our town now offer a vaccine against shingles to everyone over seventy.
= 我们镇上的家庭医生现在为所有七十岁以上的人提供带状疱疹疫苗。
a vaccine against shingles = 带状疱疹疫苗

shingly
/ˈʃɪŋɡli/
核心：形容词：（海滩、河岸）多卵石的。
① We walked along the shingly beach, and the stones crunched under our boots.
= 我们沿着满是卵石的海滩走，石子在靴子底下咯吱作响。
a shingly beach = 多卵石的海滩
shingly = 多卵石的
② The river runs over a shingly bed, so the water is very clear.
= 那条河流过铺满卵石的河床，所以水很清。
a shingly bed = 多卵石的河床

shinny
/ˈʃɪni/
核心：动词（shinnied, shinnied）（美式口语）：（用手脚）攀爬（树、绳、旗杆等）。名词，不可数：（儿童玩的）简易曲棍球。
① The boy shinnied up the flagpole in the school yard to get his kite back.
= 那个男孩顺着校园里的旗杆爬上去，把风筝拿了回来。
shinny up something = 爬上某物
shinny = 攀爬
② When the fire started, the workers shinnied down a rope from the roof of the warehouse.
= 起火时，工人们顺着绳子从仓库屋顶滑了下来。
shinny down something = 顺着某物滑下
③ After school the children played shinny on the frozen pond with sticks and an old tin can.
= 放学后孩子们在结冰的池塘上用棍子和一个旧铁罐玩简易曲棍球。
play shinny = 玩简易曲棍球

Shinto
/ˈʃɪntəʊ/
核心：名词，不可数：神道教（日本的传统宗教，崇拜自然神灵和祖先）。也说 Shintoism。
① Many Japanese families follow both Shinto and Buddhism, and visit shrines at New Year.
= 许多日本家庭既信神道教也信佛教，新年时去神社参拜。
Shinto = 神道教
② The red gate at the entrance to the park is part of an old Shinto shrine.
= 公园入口那座红色大门是一座古老神道教神社的一部分。
a Shinto shrine = 神道教神社

shiny
/ˈʃaɪni/
核心：形容词：闪亮的、有光泽的：shiny shoes、a shiny new car。
① He wore a new suit and shiny black shoes to his sister's wedding.
= 他穿着新西装和锃亮的黑皮鞋去参加姐姐的婚礼。
shiny = 闪亮的
shiny shoes = 锃亮的鞋
② My uncle drove up in a shiny new car and offered to take us all to the beach.
= 我叔叔开着一辆崭新锃亮的车过来，说要带我们都去海边。
a shiny new car = 崭新锃亮的车

ship
/ʃɪp/
核心：名词，可数：船、舰（通常指能出海的大船）：on board a ship、by ship。动词（shipped, shipped）：（用船或其他方式）运送、运输：ship goods to somewhere；（产品）出货、上市；（口语）把（人）送走。习语：jump ship 是弃船而逃、（比喻）跳槽；run a tight ship 是管理严格。
① The ship sailed out of the harbour at dawn with two thousand passengers on board.
= 那艘船黎明时载着两千名乘客驶出了港口。
ship = 船
ship = 大船（能出海）
近义对照：
boat = 小船
② The small family company in our village ships its furniture to customers all over the world.
= 我们村那家小家族企业把家具运给世界各地的顾客。
ship = 运送
ship something to somewhere = 把某物运往某处
③ The new phone will ship in October, but you can order one now.
= 那款新手机十月出货，不过现在就可以预订。
ship = 出货
④ When their parents got divorced, the two boys were shipped off to a boarding school in the country.
= 父母离婚后，两个男孩被送到乡下一所寄宿学校去了。
ship off = 把（某人）送走（常拆开用：ship somebody off）
⑤ Three of the best players jumped ship and joined a rival team.
= 三名最好的球员跳槽去了对手球队。
jump ship = 跳槽、弃船而逃
⑥ The new head teacher runs a tight ship, and nobody is late any more.
= 新校长管理很严，再也没人迟到了。
run a tight ship = 管理严格
⑦ In the 1630s, the king made people pay ship money to build up the navy, and many refused.
= 十七世纪三十年代，国王让人们缴纳造船税来扩充海军，很多人拒绝了。
ship money = （英国史）造船税
⑧ The fishing boat called the coastguard on its ship-to-shore radio when the engine stopped.
= 发动机停了之后，那条渔船用船岸无线电呼叫了海岸警卫队。
ship-to-shore = 船对岸的（通信）

shipboard
/ˈʃɪpbɔːd/
核心：形容词：船上的、在船上发生的：a shipboard romance。名词：on shipboard 是在船上。
① My grandparents met during a shipboard romance on the way to Australia in 1958.
= 我祖父母是 1958 年去澳大利亚的途中，在船上谈恋爱认识的。
a shipboard romance = 船上的恋情
shipboard = 船上的
② Life on shipboard was hard for the sailors, who slept in small beds below the deck.
= 船上的生活对水手们来说很艰苦，他们睡在甲板下的小床上。
on shipboard = 在船上

shipbuilder
/ˈʃɪpbɪldə(r)/

① His father and grandfather were both shipbuilders who worked in the yards on the river.
= 他父亲和祖父都是造船工人，在河边的船厂干活。
shipbuilders = 造船工人
shipbuilder = 造船工人
② The navy has ordered three new ships from a shipbuilder in Korea.
= 海军向韩国的一家造船公司订购了三艘新船。
a shipbuilder = 造船公司

shipbuilding
/ˈʃɪpbɪldɪŋ/

① The town grew rich from shipbuilding, and thousands of men worked in the yards.
= 那座城镇靠造船业致富，成千上万的人在船厂干活。

② When the shipbuilding industry closed down, many families left the area to look for work.
= 造船业倒闭后，许多家庭离开那个地区去找工作。

shipload
/ˈʃɪpləʊd/

① A shipload of bananas arrived at the port this morning from South America.
= 今天早上一船从南美运来的香蕉抵达港口。

shipload = 一船货
② In the old days, shiploads of people left Ireland every week to start a new life in America.
= 过去，每周都有一船船的人离开爱尔兰，去美国开始新生活。

shipmate
/ˈʃɪpmeɪt/

= 四十年后，那位老水手又和战时的两个船友重聚了。

shipment
/ˈʃɪpmənt/

① The boxes of tea in the warehouse are packed and ready for shipment to Japan next week.
= 仓库里的一箱箱茶叶已经打包好，准备下周运往日本。

② Police found a shipment of stolen phones in a lorry at the port.
= 警方在港口的一辆卡车里发现了一批被盗的手机。
a shipment of something = 一批某货物

shipowner
/ˈʃɪpəʊnə(r)/

shipper
/ˈʃɪpə(r)/

= 货运公司保证我们的箱子六周内能运到加拿大。

② Wine shippers bring thousands of bottles from France to Britain every year.

shipping
/ˈʃɪpɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：（总称）船只、船舶：a danger to shipping；（货物的）运输、运送；运费：shipping costs。
① The strong winds are a danger to shipping in the North Sea tonight.
= 今晚北海的大风对过往船只构成危险。
a danger to shipping = 对船只的危险
shipping = 船只
② Shipping costs are included in the price, so you will not pay anything extra.
= 运费已包含在价格里，所以你不用额外付钱。
shipping costs = 运费
③ Every night my father listened to the shipping forecast on the radio, even though he had never been to sea.
= 每晚我父亲都听收音机里的航海天气预报，尽管他从没出过海。
the shipping forecast = 航海天气预报
④ The old newspaper had a page of shipping news that listed every ship arriving in the port.
= 那份旧报纸有一版船运消息，列出了每一艘抵港的船。
shipping news = 船运消息

shipshape
/ˈʃɪpʃeɪp/
核心：形容词（口语）：整整齐齐的、井井有条的：shipshape and Bristol fashion。
① She spent the morning cleaning the house until everything was shipshape for her parents' visit.
= 她花了一上午打扫房子，把一切收拾得整整齐齐，迎接父母来访。
shipshape = 井井有条的
② The captain always kept his boat shipshape, with every rope neatly coiled on the deck.
= 船长总是把船收拾得井井有条，甲板上每根绳子都盘得整整齐齐。
keep something shipshape = 使某物保持整齐

shipwreck
/ˈʃɪpriːk/
核心：名词：船只失事、海难（可数或不可数）；（可数）失事船只、沉船残骸。动词：be shipwrecked 是遭遇海难。
① The old sailor told us about the shipwreck that he survived when he was nineteen.
= 那位老水手给我们讲了他十九岁时死里逃生的那次海难。
a shipwreck = 海难
shipwreck = 船只失事
② Divers have found the shipwreck of a Spanish ship that sank three hundred years ago.
= 潜水员发现了一艘三百年前沉没的西班牙船的残骸。
the shipwreck of a ship = 某船的残骸

shipwrecked
/ˈʃɪprekt/
核心：形容词：遭遇海难的：shipwrecked sailors。
① In the story, the shipwrecked sailor lives alone on an island for twenty-eight years.
= 在那个故事里，遭遇海难的水手独自在岛上生活了二十八年。
a shipwrecked sailor = 遭遇海难的水手
shipwrecked = 遭遇海难的
② The fishermen rescued the shipwrecked passengers and gave them blankets and hot tea.
= 渔民们救起了遭遇海难的乘客，给他们毯子和热茶。
shipwrecked passengers = 遭遇海难的乘客

shipyard
/ˈʃɪpjɑːd/
核心：名词，可数：造船厂、船坞。
① Thousands of people worked at the shipyard when it built the biggest ships in the world.
= 那家造船厂建造世界最大的船只时，有成千上万的人在那里工作。
the shipyard = 造船厂

② The old shipyard has been turned into flats, shops and a museum about the town's history.
= 那个旧船厂被改造成了公寓、商店和一座讲述小镇历史的博物馆。
the old shipyard = 旧船厂

shire
/ˈʃaɪə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（英式，旧）：郡（同 county，现多见于郡名词尾，如 Yorkshire）；（复数 the Shires）英格兰中部乡村地区。
① In the old days, each shire had its own sheriff and its own court.
= 旧时每个郡都有自己的郡长和法庭。
a shire = 郡
shire = 郡
② The rich families from London liked to go hunting in the Shires every winter.
= 伦敦的富人家每年冬天都喜欢去英格兰中部乡村打猎。
the Shires = 英格兰中部乡村
③ The farmer still uses a pair of huge shire horses to pull the plough in his fields.
= 那个农场主仍用两匹高大的夏尔马在地里拉犁。
a shire horse = 夏尔马（英国大型挽马）

shirk
/ʃɜːk/
核心：动词：逃避、推卸（工作、责任）：shirk your responsibilities。
① A good leader does not shirk difficult decisions, even when they make people angry.
= 好的领导者不会逃避艰难的决定，即使那些决定会让人生气。
shirk something = 逃避某事
shirk = 推卸
② He was accused of shirking his duties as a father after he moved abroad and stopped sending money.
= 他搬到国外、不再寄钱回家之后，被指责逃避做父亲的责任。
shirk your duties = 逃避职责

shirker
/ˈʃɜːkə(r)/

= 经理说他的团队里容不得偷懒的人，人人都得卖力干活。

② She is no shirker; she stayed late every night until the job was finished.
= 她可不是偷懒的人，每天晚上都留到很晚，直到把活干完。

shirt
/ʃɜːt/
核心：名词，可数：衬衫：a white shirt；（运动队的）队服、球衣。习语：keep your shirt on 是别着急、别发火；give somebody the shirt off your back 是倾其所有帮助某人。
① He wore a white shirt and a blue tie to the job interview.
= 他穿着白衬衫、打着蓝领带去面试。
shirt = 衬衫
② On hot summer days I usually wear an old T-shirt at home and in the garden.
= 夏天热的时候，我在家里和花园里通常穿一件旧 T 恤。
T-shirt = T 恤
③ After dinner he rolled up his shirt sleeves and started washing the dishes in the sink.
= 晚饭后他卷起衬衫袖子，开始在水槽里洗碗。
shirt sleeve = 衬衫袖子
④ The striker took off his shirt and threw it to the fans after the final whistle.
= 终场哨响后，那名前锋脱下球衣扔给了球迷。
his shirt = 他的球衣
⑤ When her friend started shouting at the station, she laughed and said, "Keep your shirt on! I'm only five minutes late."
= 朋友在车站嚷起来时，她笑着说：「别着急！我才晚了五分钟。」
keep your shirt on = 别着急、别发火
⑥ My grandfather was so kind that he would give you the shirt off his back if you needed it.
= 我祖父心肠太好了，你要是有需要，他能把身上的衬衫脱给你。
the shirt off your back = 倾其所有
⑦ He spilled red wine down his shirt front during the dinner and spent the evening trying to hide it.
= 他晚宴时把红酒洒在衬衫前襟上，整个晚上都在设法遮掩。
a shirt front = 衬衫前襟
⑧ His shirt tail was hanging out of his trousers when he ran for the bus.
= 他追公交车时，衬衫下摆露在裤子外面。
a shirt tail = 衬衫下摆

shirtsleeve

shirty

shisha
/ˈʃiːʃə/

shit
/ʃɪt/
核心：粗话，正式场合不用。名词：屎、粪便（不可数）；（不可数）废话、胡扯；垃圾东西、烂货；（可数）混蛋。动词（shit 或 shat, shit 或 shat）：拉屎。感叹词：（表示恼火、吃惊）妈的、糟了。英式也说 shite。
① As the doors closed, he shouted, "Shit! I've left my phone on the train!"
= 车门关上时，他大叫：「糟了！我把手机落在火车上了！」
shit = 糟了（感叹词）
② The farmer's boots were covered in cow shit after he had been in the field all morning.
= 那个农场主在地里待了一上午，靴子上沾满了牛粪。
cow shit = 牛粪
③ Don't believe a word he says about his rich uncle, he is talking shit again.
= 他说他有个有钱叔叔，一个字都别信，他又在胡扯了。
talk shit = 胡扯
④ Our dog shat on the neighbour's lawn again this morning, and he was furious.
= 我们家的狗今天早上又在邻居的草坪上拉了屎，邻居气坏了。
shat = 拉屎（过去式）
⑤ When the lights went out in the old house, the children were scared shitless.
= 老房子里的灯一灭，孩子们吓得要死。
scared shitless = 吓得要死
⑥ She is a shit-hot lawyer, and she has never lost a case.
= 她是个顶呱呱的律师，从来没输过官司。
shit-hot = 棒极了
⑦ I was shit-scared the first time I drove on the motorway in the rain at night.
= 我第一次在雨夜上高速公路开车时吓坏了。
shit-scared = 吓坏了
⑧ He got completely shit-faced at his brother's wedding and fell asleep under the table.
= 他在他哥哥的婚礼上喝得烂醉，在桌子底下睡着了。
shit-faced = 喝得烂醉
⑨ Ignore her, she is a shit stirrer who loves telling people what others said behind their backs.
= 别理她，她是个爱搬弄是非的人，就喜欢把别人背后说的话传来传去。
a shit stirrer = 搬弄是非的人
⑩ He was sacked from the office for shit stirring between the two managers who already hated each other.
= 他因在两个本来就互相憎恨的经理之间挑拨离间，被办公室开除了。
shit stirring = 搬弄是非
⑪ After twenty minutes my friend from Glasgow decided the film was total shite and walked out of the cinema.
= 二十分钟后，我那位格拉斯哥朋友断定这电影烂透了，就走出了电影院。
shite = 烂货（英式）

shithole
/ˈʃɪthəʊl/

shitstorm
/ˈʃɪtstɔːm/

② When the company lost all the customers' data, the boss knew he was in the middle of a shitstorm.

shitty
/ˈʃɪti/

① I had a really shitty day at work, and then I missed the last bus home.
= 我上班一整天糟透了，然后又没赶上回家的末班车。

shiver
/ˈʃɪvə(r)/
核心：动词，不及物：（因冷、怕、激动）发抖、哆嗦：shiver with cold。名词，可数：颤抖、哆嗦：a shiver of fear；（复数 the shivers）（口语）一阵寒战、毛骨悚然：give somebody the shivers。习语：send shivers down your spine 是使人毛骨悚然或激动得发抖。
① The children stood shivering in the rain, waiting for the school bus.
= 孩子们站在雨里瑟瑟发抖，等校车来。
shiver = 发抖
② She was shivering with cold when they finally found her on the mountain.
= 人们终于在山上找到她时，她冻得直哆嗦。
shiver with cold = 冻得发抖
③ A shiver of fear ran through me when I heard footsteps behind me in the dark.
= 黑暗中听到身后有脚步声，我吓得打了个寒战。
a shiver of fear = 一阵恐惧的颤抖
④ That empty old house gives me the shivers, and I never walk past it at night.
= 那座空荡荡的老房子让我毛骨悚然，我晚上从不从那儿经过。
give somebody the shivers = 使某人毛骨悚然
⑤ The music at the end of the film sent shivers down my spine.
= 电影结尾的音乐让我激动得浑身发抖。
send shivers down your spine = 使人激动得发抖

shivery
/ˈʃɪvəri/

① I felt hot and shivery all night, and in the morning the doctor said I had the flu.
= 我整夜忽冷忽热、直打寒战，早上医生说我得了流感。

② It was a grey, shivery morning, and the wind came straight off the sea.
= 那是个阴沉、让人发抖的早晨，风直接从海上吹来。
a shivery morning = 让人发抖的早晨

shoal
/ʃəʊl/

① A shoal of small silver fish swam past the boat and then disappeared into the deep water.
= 一群银色的小鱼从船边游过，然后消失在深水里。
a shoal of fish = 一群鱼
shoal = 鱼群
② After the programme, the television company received shoals of letters from angry viewers.
= 节目播出后，电视台收到了大量愤怒观众的来信。
