= 警方提醒，在树林里采野生迷幻蘑菇非常危险。
wild shrooms = 野生迷幻蘑菇

shroud
/ʃraʊd/
核心：名词，可数：裹尸布；（书面）遮蔽物、笼罩之物：a shroud of mist、a shroud of secrecy。动词，及物：笼罩、遮蔽：be shrouded in something。
① In the old days, the dead were wrapped in a white shroud before they were buried.
= 过去，死者下葬前要用白色裹尸布裹起来。
a shroud = 裹尸布
shroud = 裹尸布
② A shroud of mist hung over the lake early in the morning.
= 清晨，一层薄雾笼罩在湖面上。
a shroud of mist = 一层薄雾
③ Thirty years later, the death of the famous singer is still shrouded in mystery.
= 三十年过去了，那位著名歌手的死至今仍是个谜。
be shrouded in mystery = 笼罩在神秘之中
④ The doctors accused the minister of shroud-waving, using stories of dying patients to win more money for hospitals.
= 医生们指责那位部长打悲情牌，拿垂死病人的故事为医院多争取经费。
shroud-waving = （英式）打悲情牌、借死亡渲染危机

shrub
/ʃrʌb/
核心：名词，可数：灌木。比 bush 更书面，多用于园艺。
① We planted a row of small shrubs along the fence to hide the road from the garden.
= 我们沿着篱笆种了一排小灌木，把公路从花园里挡住。
shrubs = 灌木
shrub = 灌木
② This shrub has bright yellow flowers in early spring, before the leaves come out.
= 这种灌木早春先开鲜黄色的花，然后才长叶子。
this shrub = 这种灌木
近义对照：bush 更口语，也指长得乱的一丛。

shrubbery
/ˈʃrʌbəri/
核心：名词：灌木丛（可数）；（总称）灌木（不可数）。
① The children hid in the shrubbery at the bottom of the garden during the game.
= 玩游戏时，孩子们躲进了花园尽头的灌木丛里。
the shrubbery = 灌木丛
shrubbery = 灌木丛
② The new owners cut back all the shrubbery so that the house got more light.
= 新房主把灌木都剪短了，好让房子更亮堂。
cut back the shrubbery = 修剪灌木

shrubby
/ˈʃrʌbi/
核心：形容词：灌木状的；长满灌木的。
① Rosemary is a shrubby plant that can grow quite tall in a warm garden.
= 迷迭香是一种灌木状植物，在温暖的花园里能长得相当高。
a shrubby plant = 灌木状植物
shrubby = 灌木状的
② We walked across the shrubby hillside, looking for the path down to the sea.
= 我们穿过长满灌木的山坡，寻找下到海边的小路。
a shrubby hillside = 长满灌木的山坡

shrug
/ʃrʌɡ/
核心：动词（shrugged, shrugged）：耸（肩）：shrug your shoulders；耸肩表示（不知道、不在乎）。名词，可数：耸肩：with a shrug。
① When I asked where the keys were, he just shrugged and went on reading.
= 我问钥匙在哪儿，他只是耸耸肩，接着看书。
shrug = 耸肩
② "I don't know," she said with a shrug, and turned back to her computer.
= 她耸了耸肩说"我不知道"，又转回去看电脑。
with a shrug = 耸耸肩
③ The champion shrugged off the injury and won the final the next day.
= 那位冠军没把伤当回事，第二天就赢了决赛。
shrug off = 不把…当回事、摆脱
④ At the press conference, the minister shrugged aside all the questions about his expenses.
= 在记者会上，那位部长对有关他开支的所有问题都不予理会。
shrug aside = 对…不予理会
⑤ She tried to shrug away the bad news and carried on with her day.
= 她想把坏消息抛在脑后，照常过她的日子。
shrug away = 把…抛开不理
耸肩在英语国家多表示「不知道」或「无所谓」。

shtetl
/ˈʃtetl/
核心：名词，可数（复数 shtetls 或 shtetlach）：（旧时东欧的）犹太人小镇、犹太村落。
① My great-grandparents grew up in a shtetl in Poland before they moved to New York.
= 我曾祖父母移居纽约之前，在波兰的一个犹太小镇长大。
a shtetl = 犹太小镇
shtetl = 犹太村落
② The musical tells the story of a poor milkman and his family in a Russian shtetl.
= 那部音乐剧讲的是俄国一个犹太村落里一位穷送奶工和他一家的故事。
a Russian shtetl = 俄国的犹太村落

shtook
/ʃtʊk/
核心：名词（英式俚语）：in shtook 是陷入麻烦、处境困难。也拼作 schtook。
① If my father finds out I crashed his car, I'll be in real shtook.
= 要是我爸发现我把他的车撞了，我可就惨了。
in shtook = 陷入麻烦
shtook = 麻烦
② The company is in serious shtook after losing its two biggest customers.
= 那家公司失去两个最大的客户后，陷入了严重的困境。
in serious shtook = 陷入严重困境

shuck
/ʃʌk/
核心：名词，可数（美式）：（玉米、豆类、坚果的）外壳、外皮；（牡蛎等的）壳。动词，及物（美式）：剥去…的壳：shuck corn、shuck oysters；（口语）脱掉（衣服）。
① On summer evenings we sat on the porch and shucked the corn for dinner.
= 夏天傍晚，我们坐在门廊上剥玉米，准备晚饭。
shuck corn = 剥玉米
shuck = 剥壳
② The chef can shuck a dozen oysters in less than a minute.
= 那位厨师不到一分钟就能剥开一打牡蛎。
shuck oysters = 剥牡蛎
③ He shucked off his wet jacket and sat down by the fire.
= 他脱掉湿夹克，在炉边坐下。
shuck off a jacket = 脱掉夹克

shucks
/ʃʌks/
核心：感叹词（美式口语，旧）：哎呀、唉（表示失望、懊恼，或被夸时的不好意思）。
① "Shucks, I've missed the bus again," the boy said, kicking the ground.
= "唉，我又没赶上公交车。"那个男孩一边踢地一边说。
shucks = 唉
② When we thanked him for fixing the roof, the old farmer just smiled and said, "Aw, shucks, it was nothing."
= 我们谢他帮忙修好屋顶，那位老农只是笑笑说："哎呀，这算不了什么。"
aw, shucks = 哎呀（不好意思）

shudder
/ˈʃʌdə(r)/
核心：动词，不及物：（因害怕、寒冷、厌恶）发抖、战栗：shudder at something；（机器、车辆）剧烈震动。名词，可数：战栗、发抖；（单数）剧烈震动。I shudder to think 是一想起就害怕。
① Years later, she still shuddered when she remembered the accident on the mountain road.
= 多年以后，她一想起山路上那次事故仍会浑身发抖。
shudder = 战栗
② I shudder to think what would have happened if the boat had sunk.
= 我一想到要是那条船沉了会怎样，就不寒而栗。
I shudder to think = 一想起就害怕
③ The old bus shuddered to a stop at the top of the hill.
= 那辆旧公交车在山顶剧烈抖动着停了下来。
shudder to a stop = 剧烈震动着停下
④ A shudder ran through the crowd when they saw the tiger walk out of its cage.
= 人群看到老虎走出笼子时，一阵战栗传遍全场。
a shudder = 一阵战栗

shuffle
/ˈʃʌfl/
核心：动词：拖着脚走：shuffle along；（因不安）挪动（脚、身体）；洗（牌）：shuffle the cards；把（纸张、东西）搬来搬去、整理；（职位等）调动。名词，单数：拖着脚走；洗牌；（职位的）改组：a cabinet shuffle；（音乐播放器的）随机播放。
① Early every morning, the old man shuffled across the kitchen in his slippers.
= 每天清早，那位老人都穿着拖鞋，拖着脚走过厨房。
shuffle across something = 拖着脚走过某处
shuffle = 拖着脚走
② Shuffle the cards well before you deal them, or someone will say you are cheating.
= 发牌之前把牌洗匀，不然有人会说你作弊。
shuffle the cards = 洗牌
③ The students in the back row shuffled their feet nervously while the head teacher spoke.
= 校长讲话时，后排的学生们不安地挪着脚。
shuffle your feet = 不安地挪动双脚
④ He shuffled the papers on his desk, but he could not find the letter.
= 他把桌上的文件翻来翻去，可就是找不到那封信。
shuffle papers = 把文件翻来翻去
⑤ I put my phone on shuffle and listened to my music on the train.
= 我把手机调到随机播放，在火车上听音乐。
on shuffle = 随机播放

shuffleboard
/ˈʃʌflbɔːd/
核心：名词，不可数：推圆盘游戏（用长杆把圆盘推到画有分数的场地上，多在船甲板上玩）。
① On the cruise ship, my grandparents played shuffleboard on the deck every afternoon.
= 在游轮上，我祖父母每天下午都在甲板上玩推圆盘游戏。
play shuffleboard = 玩推圆盘游戏
shuffleboard = 推圆盘游戏
② The bar has a long shuffleboard table where customers play for drinks.
= 那家酒吧有一张长长的推圆盘桌，顾客们玩游戏赌酒。
a shuffleboard table = 推圆盘桌

shufti
/ˈʃʊfti/
核心：名词，单数（英式口语，旧）：看一眼、瞧一瞧：have a shufti at something。
① Have a shufti at this map and tell me which road we should take.
= 瞧一眼这张地图，告诉我们该走哪条路。
have a shufti at something = 瞧一眼某物
shufti = 瞧一瞧
② The mechanic had a quick shufti under the bonnet and said the engine was fine.
= 修车工往发动机罩下面瞧了一眼，说发动机没问题。
a quick shufti = 很快瞧一眼

shun
/ʃʌn/
核心：动词（shunned, shunned）：避开、回避、躲开（人或事）。
① After the scandal, he was shunned by his old friends and colleagues.
= 丑闻之后，他的老朋友和同事都躲着他。
be shunned by somebody = 被某人回避
shun = 避开
② The famous writer shuns publicity and has never given a television interview.
= 那位著名作家不愿抛头露面，从没接受过电视采访。
shun publicity = 避开公众关注
比 avoid 更书面，常带「刻意疏远」的意思。

shunt
/ʃʌnt/
核心：动词，及物：（尤指不公平地）把（人）调走、打发到（不重要的地方）：shunt somebody aside；把（火车）转轨、调轨。名词，可数：（英式口语）（汽车的）追尾；（医学）分流术、分流管。
① After the new manager arrived, she was shunted into a small office at the back of the building.
= 新经理来了以后，她被打发到大楼后面的一间小办公室里。
be shunted into something = 被打发到某处
shunt = 把（人）调走
② The old wagons were shunted onto a side track to let the express train pass.
= 那些旧车厢被调到一条侧线上，给特快列车让路。
shunt something onto a side track = 把某物调到侧线上
③ There was a small shunt on the motorway, and the traffic stopped for an hour.
= 高速公路上发生了一起轻微追尾，交通中断了一个小时。
a small shunt = 一起轻微追尾
多带不满的口气，暗示被不公平地对待。

shush
/ʃʊʃ/
核心：感叹词：嘘（叫人安静）。动词，及物：嘘（某人）叫其安静。
① "Shush, the film is starting," my sister whispered as the lights went down.
= 灯光暗下来时，我姐姐小声说："嘘，电影开始了。"
shush = 嘘
② The librarian shushed the two boys who were laughing loudly at the back of the room.
= 图书管理员嘘了一声，叫后排那两个大声笑的男孩安静。
shush somebody = 嘘某人叫其安静
比 sh 更常作动词用。

shut
/ʃʌt/
核心：动词（shut, shut）：关上、关闭：shut the door、shut your eyes；（商店、机构）关门、停止营业：the shop shuts at six；（把人、动物）关在（某处）。形容词：关着的、关闭的：keep your mouth shut。
① Please shut the door when you go out; it's very cold today.
= 出去时请把门关上，今天很冷。
shut = 关
shut the door = 关门
② The bank in the village shuts at four o'clock on Fridays, so we'll have to hurry.
= 村里的银行星期五四点关门，所以我们得快点了。
shut = （商店等）关门
③ Keep your mouth shut about the party, or it won't be a surprise.
= 派对的事别说出去，不然就不是惊喜了。
keep your mouth shut = 闭口不说
④ The factory shut down last year, and three hundred people lost their jobs.
= 那家工厂去年关闭了，三百人失去了工作。
shut down = 关闭、停业；关机
⑤ Remember to shut off the water before you start fixing the pipe.
= 修水管之前记得先关掉水。
shut off = 关掉（水、电、机器）
⑥ The village was shut off from the rest of the world by the snow for two weeks.
= 那个村子被大雪与外界隔绝了两个星期。
shut off from something = 与某处隔绝
⑦ After the argument, he shut himself off from his family and refused to speak to anyone.
= 吵架之后，他把自己封闭起来，跟谁都不说话。
shut yourself off = 把自己封闭起来
⑧ The dog was shut in the kitchen while the guests were having dinner.
= 客人们吃晚饭时，那条狗被关在厨房里。
shut in = 把…关在里面
⑨ Close the thick curtains in the bedroom to shut out the light from the street.
= 拉上卧室的厚窗帘，挡住街上的光。
shut out = 挡在外面、把…关在外面
⑩ "Shut up and listen to what the teacher is saying!" the boy whispered to his noisy friend.
= 那个男孩小声对吵闹的朋友说："闭嘴，听老师在说什么！"
shut up = 闭嘴（不礼貌）
⑪ The old letters were shut away in a box in the attic for fifty years.
= 那些旧信被锁在阁楼的一个盒子里五十年。
shut away = 把…锁起来、藏起来
⑫ When she is writing a book, she shuts herself away in a cottage by the sea for months.
= 她写书的时候，会把自己关在海边一座小屋里好几个月。
shut yourself away = 闭门不出、与世隔绝
⑬ I only had four hours of shut-eye last night, so I'm very tired today.
= 我昨晚只睡了四个小时，所以今天很累。
shut-eye = （口语）睡觉

shutdown
/ˈʃʌtdaʊn/
核心：名词，可数：（工厂、机构的）关闭、停工；（电脑、机器的）关机；（美式）（政府的）停摆。
① The shutdown of the steel works was a disaster for the whole town.
= 钢铁厂的关闭对整个小镇都是一场灾难。
the shutdown of something = 某处的关闭
shutdown = 关闭
② During the long government shutdown last winter, the national parks were closed to visitors.
= 去年冬天政府长期停摆期间，国家公园都不对游客开放。
a government shutdown = 政府停摆

shutout
/ˈʃʌtaʊt/
核心：名词，可数（美式）：（让对手）零得分的比赛、完封。
① Our goalkeeper kept a shutout in the final, and we won two goals to nil.
= 我们的守门员在决赛中保持零失球，我们以二比零获胜。
keep a shutout = 保持零失球
shutout = 完封
② The pitcher threw a shutout, and the other team did not score a single run.
= 那位投手投出了完封，对方一分都没得到。
throw a shutout = 投出完封

shutter
/ˈʃʌtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（常用复数）：（窗户外的）百叶窗、护窗板；（照相机的）快门。动词，及物：关上…的百叶窗；（使）关闭、停业。
① In the summer, we close the wooden shutters in the afternoon to keep the house cool.
= 夏天下午我们关上木百叶窗，让屋里保持凉快。
shutters = 百叶窗
shutter = 护窗板
② Many of the shops on the high street have been shuttered since the new supermarket opened.
= 新超市开张以来，大街上很多商店都关门了。
be shuttered = 关门停业
③ Press the shutter release halfway to focus, and then all the way to take the photo.
= 把快门按钮按下一半对焦，再按到底拍照。
the shutter release = 快门按钮
④ Use a fast shutter speed to take sharp photos of moving cars.
= 拍行驶中的汽车要用高速快门，照片才清晰。
a fast shutter speed = 高速快门

shutterbug
/ˈʃʌtəbʌɡ/
核心：名词，可数（美式口语）：摄影迷、业余摄影爱好者。
① My uncle is a real shutterbug who takes his camera everywhere, even to the supermarket.
= 我叔叔是个十足的摄影迷，走到哪儿都带着相机，连去超市都不例外。
a shutterbug = 摄影迷
shutterbug = 业余摄影爱好者
② The old bridge at sunset is a favourite spot for local shutterbugs.
= 夕阳下的老桥是当地摄影爱好者最喜欢的取景点。
local shutterbugs = 当地摄影爱好者

shuttered
/ˈʃʌtəd/
核心：形容词：装着百叶窗的、百叶窗关着的；（商店、工厂）关门的、停业的。
① We walked through the quiet village, past white houses with green shuttered windows.
= 我们走过安静的村子，经过一座座装着绿色百叶窗的白房子。
shuttered windows = 装着百叶窗的窗户
shuttered = 装着百叶窗的
② Ten years after the factory closed, the town centre is full of shuttered shops and empty offices.
= 工厂关闭十年后，镇中心到处是关了门的商店和空荡荡的办公室。
shuttered shops = 关门的商店

shuttle
/ˈʃʌtl/
核心：名词，可数：（往返于两地之间的）班车、班机、穿梭巴士：a shuttle bus；航天飞机：the space shuttle；（织布机的）梭子。动词：（使）往返、穿梭：shuttle between A and B。
① A free shuttle bus runs between the hotel and the airport every twenty minutes.
= 酒店和机场之间每二十分钟有一班免费穿梭巴士。
a shuttle bus = 穿梭巴士
shuttle = 班车
② In the 1990s, the space shuttle carried teams of seven astronauts into space for weeks at a time.
= 二十世纪九十年代，航天飞机每次把七人组成的宇航员小组送上太空，一去就是几个星期。
the space shuttle = 航天飞机
③ Since the divorce, the children shuttle between their parents' houses every week.
= 离婚之后，孩子们每周在父母两家之间来回跑。
shuttle between A and B = 在 A 与 B 之间往返
④ The foreign minister's shuttle diplomacy between the two capitals finally ended the war.
= 外交部长在两国首都之间的穿梭外交终于结束了那场战争。
shuttle diplomacy = 穿梭外交

shuttlecock
/ˈʃʌtlkɒk/
核心：名词，可数：羽毛球（羽毛球运动用的球）。美式常说 birdie。
① The wind blew the shuttlecock over the fence, so we had to stop playing.
= 风把羽毛球吹过了篱笆，所以我们只好不打了。
the shuttlecock = 羽毛球
shuttlecock = 羽毛球
② Good shuttlecocks are made with real feathers, but cheaper ones are plastic.
= 好的羽毛球是用真羽毛做的，便宜的是塑料的。
good shuttlecocks = 好的羽毛球
这项运动叫 badminton，球才叫 shuttlecock。

shy
/ʃaɪ/
核心：形容词：害羞的、腼腆的：be shy of somebody；（动物）易受惊的；（口语）（数量）不足的、差一点：just shy of something。动词（shied, shied）：（马）受惊、惊退：shy at something；回避、躲开。副词 shyly 是害羞地。名词 shyness 另立条。
① She was very shy as a child and hid behind her mother when visitors came.
= 她小时候非常害羞，家里来客人就躲到妈妈身后。
shy = 害羞的
shy = 害羞（性格）
近义对照：
ashamed = 羞愧（做错事）
② Deer are shy animals, so you have to be very quiet if you want to see them.
= 鹿是易受惊的动物，所以想看到它们就得非常安静。
shy animals = 易受惊的动物
③ The horse shied at the plastic bag and nearly threw its rider off.
= 那匹马被塑料袋惊着了，差点把骑手甩下来。
shy at something = （马）被某物惊着
shied = 受惊（过去式）
④ Many people shy away from talking about money, even with their own families.
= 很多人避而不谈钱的事，连对自己家人也是如此。
shy away from something = 回避某事
shy away = 回避、退缩
⑤ He was just shy of his eighteenth birthday when he joined the army.
= 他参军时还差一点才满十八岁。
just shy of something = 差一点到某数
⑥ The little girl smiled shyly and gave the flowers to the teacher.
= 小女孩害羞地笑着，把花递给了老师。
shyly = 害羞地

Shylock
/ˈʃaɪlɒk/
核心：名词：夏洛克（莎士比亚《威尼斯商人》中的犹太放债人）。用小写 shylock 指「狠心的放债人」时带反犹的冒犯意味，不要使用。
① In The Merchant of Venice, Shylock demands a pound of flesh when the merchant cannot pay back the money.
= 在《威尼斯商人》里，商人还不起钱时，夏洛克要求割他一磅肉。
Shylock = 夏洛克
② The actor's performance as Shylock showed the character as a lonely man who had been badly treated.
= 那位演员演的夏洛克，展现的是一个孤独、受尽欺凌的人。
play Shylock = 扮演夏洛克

shyness
/ˈʃaɪnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：害羞、腼腆。
① His shyness made it hard for him to make friends at his new school.
= 他的害羞让他在新学校很难交到朋友。
somebody's shyness = 某人的害羞
shyness = 害羞
② She slowly overcame her shyness by joining the school drama club in her second year.
= 她二年级时参加了学校戏剧社，慢慢克服了害羞。
overcome your shyness = 克服害羞

shyster
/ˈʃaɪstə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（美式口语，贬义）：奸商、骗子；（尤指）讼棍、不讲道德的律师。
① My grandfather lost all his savings to a shyster who promised to double his money.
= 我祖父被一个承诺让他钱翻倍的骗子骗光了积蓄。
a shyster = 骗子
shyster = 奸商
② Make sure you find an honest lawyer, not some shyster who only wants your money.
= 一定要找个诚实的律师，别找那种只想要你钱的讼棍。
some shyster = 某个讼棍

sib
/sɪb/
核心：名词，可数（口语，尤美式）：兄弟姐妹（sibling 的简称）。
① I have three sibs, and we all meet at our parents' house every Christmas.
= 我有三个兄弟姐妹，每年圣诞节我们都在父母家团聚。
sibs = 兄弟姐妹
sib = 兄弟姐妹
② The study compared children who had no sibs with children from big families.
= 那项研究把没有兄弟姐妹的孩子和大家庭的孩子作了比较。
have no sibs = 没有兄弟姐妹
口语缩略词，正式场合用 sibling。

sibilant
/ˈsɪbɪlənt/
核心：形容词：发咝咝声的。名词，可数（语音）：咝音（如 /s/、/z/、/ʃ/）。
① The snake made a soft sibilant sound as it moved through the dry leaves.
= 那条蛇在枯叶中游动，发出轻轻的咝咝声。
a sibilant sound = 咝咝声
sibilant = 发咝咝声的
② In English, sibilants such as /s/ and /z/ change the sound of the plural ending.
= 在英语里，像 /s/、/z/ 这样的咝音会改变复数词尾的读音。
sibilants = 咝音

sibling
/ˈsɪblɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数（正式）：兄弟姐妹（之一）：brothers and sisters 的统称。
① On the first day at the new school, the teacher asked each of us whether we had any siblings.
= 新学校开学第一天，老师问我们每个人有没有兄弟姐妹。
siblings = 兄弟姐妹
sibling = 兄弟姐妹
② There is often some sibling rivalry when a new baby arrives in the family.
= 家里添了新宝宝时，兄弟姐妹之间常会有些争宠。
sibling rivalry = 兄弟姐妹间的竞争
比 brothers and sisters 正式，多见于书面、调查表格。

sibyl
/ˈsɪbl/
核心：名词，可数：（古希腊、罗马的）女预言家、女巫。
① In the old story, the king travelled to a cave to ask the sibyl about his future.
= 在那个古老的故事里，国王跋涉到一个山洞，向女预言家询问自己的未来。
the sibyl = 女预言家
sibyl = 女预言家
② The ceiling of the chapel shows five sibyls painted beside the prophets.
= 那座礼拜堂的天花板上画着五位女预言家，与众先知并列。
five sibyls = 五位女预言家

sibylline
/ˈsɪbəlaɪn/
核心：形容词（书面）：预言性的；神秘难解的：sibylline words。
① The old woman gave a sibylline warning that nobody understood until the storm arrived.
= 那位老妇人说了一句神秘难解的警告，直到暴风雨来临大家才明白。
a sibylline warning = 神秘难解的警告
sibylline = 神秘难解的
② The poet's last book is full of sibylline lines about death and the sea.
= 那位诗人的最后一本诗集满是关于死亡与大海的晦涩诗句。
sibylline lines = 晦涩难解的诗句

sic
/sɪk/
核心：副词：（拉丁语，放在引文里的括号中）原文如此（表示错误不是引者造成的）。动词（sicced, sicced）（美式口语）：唆使（狗）去咬：sic a dog on somebody。
① When the historian copied the old soldier's letters into her book, she put sic in brackets after every spelling mistake.
= 那位历史学家把老兵的信抄进书里时，在每个拼写错误后面都用括号注上「原文如此」。
sic = 原文如此
② The farmer threatened to sic his dogs on anyone who came onto his land.
= 那个农场主威胁说，谁敢踏上他的地就放狗咬谁。
sic a dog on somebody = 唆使狗去咬某人
sic on = 唆使（狗）去咬
写作时把 sic 放在方括号里，紧跟在原文的错误后面。

sick
/sɪk/
核心：形容词：有病的、生病的：be sick、a sick child；恶心的、想吐的：feel sick、be sick（英式：呕吐）；厌倦的、腻烦的：be sick of something；（笑话、言行）病态的、令人反感的：a sick joke；（俚语）极好的、酷的。名词，不可数（英式口语）：呕吐物。习语：sick and tired of 是厌烦透了；call in sick 是打电话请病假；worried sick 是担心死了。
① My little brother is sick, so he can't go to school today.
= 我弟弟病了，所以今天不能去上学。
sick = 生病的
② I felt sick on the boat and had to lie down for the whole journey.
= 我在船上觉得恶心，整个行程都只好躺着。
sick = 恶心的、不舒服的
feel sick = 想吐
③ I'm sick of eating pasta every night this week; let's have something different tonight.
= 这个星期我受够了每晚都吃意面，今晚咱们换点别的吧。
be sick of = 厌烦
④ After three weeks, she was sick and tired of waiting for him to phone her back.
= 三个星期过去了，她等他回电话等得烦透了。
sick and tired of = 厌烦透了
⑤ He called in sick on Monday, but his boss saw him at the football match.
= 他星期一打电话请了病假，可他老板在足球赛上看到了他。
call in sick = 打电话请病假
⑥ Your mother was worried sick when you didn't come home last night.
= 你昨晚没回家，你妈妈担心死了。
worried sick = 担心死了
⑦ At the funeral dinner, my uncle told a sick joke, and nobody at the table laughed.
= 葬礼后的晚宴上，我叔叔讲了个恶心的笑话，桌上没一个人笑。
a sick joke = 令人反感的笑话
⑧ The airline gives every passenger a sick bag in case they feel ill during the flight.
= 航空公司给每位乘客备了一个呕吐袋，以防飞行中不舒服。
a sick bag = 呕吐袋
⑨ Workers in the new office kept getting headaches, and experts said it was sick building syndrome.
= 新办公楼里的员工老是头痛，专家说这是病态建筑综合征。
sick building syndrome = 病态建筑综合征
⑩ I had to take a sick day yesterday because I had a high temperature.
= 我昨天发高烧，只好请了一天病假。
a sick day = 一天病假
⑪ My mother used to get terrible sick headaches that kept her in bed all day.
= 我母亲以前常犯严重的偏头痛，一整天都下不了床。
a sick headache = （伴有恶心的）偏头痛
⑫ After the operation on his back, he was on sick leave from the factory for three months.
= 背部手术后，他从工厂休了三个月病假。
sick leave = 病假
⑬ The film was so violent that I found it sick-making and walked out after half an hour.
= 那部电影暴力得让我恶心，看了半小时就走了。
sick-making = （英式口语）令人作呕的
⑭ Most companies give their workers sick pay for the first few weeks of an illness.
= 大多数公司在员工生病的头几个星期照发病假工资。
sick pay = 病假工资
⑮ Just as we were leaving for the party, the baby sicked up some milk all over my shirt.
= 我们正要出门去派对，宝宝把一些奶吐在了我的衬衫上。
sick up = （英式口语）吐出

sickbay
/ˈsɪkbeɪ/
核心：名词，可数：（船上、学校、军营里的）医务室、病房。

= 那名割伤手的水手被送到了船上的医务室。

sickbed

sicken
/ˈsɪkən/
核心：动词：使厌恶、使恶心：be sickened by something；（旧）生病、得病：sicken for something（英式，快要生病）。
① We were sickened by the pictures of the animals that had been treated so cruelly.
= 看到那些受到如此残忍对待的动物的照片，我们感到恶心。

② The child is very tired today; I think she is sickening for something.
= 那孩子今天很累，我想她快要生病了。
be sickening for something = 快要生病

sickener
/ˈsɪknə(r)/

① Losing the match in the last minute was a real sickener for the players.
= 最后一分钟输掉比赛，对球员们真是个沉重的打击。
a real sickener = 让人沮丧的事
sickener = 令人沮丧的事
② Finding out that the holiday had been cancelled was a sickener after months of saving.
= 攒了好几个月的钱，却发现假期泡汤了，真让人泄气。
a sickener = 让人泄气的事

sickening
/ˈsɪkənɪŋ/
核心：形容词：令人作呕的、令人厌恶的：a sickening crime；（声音）让人心惊的：a sickening crack。副词 sickeningly 是令人作呕地、（口语）让人嫉妒地。
① The attack on the old man was a sickening crime, and the whole town was shocked.
= 袭击那位老人是一桩令人发指的罪行，全镇都震惊了。
a sickening crime = 令人发指的罪行
sickening = 令人作呕的
② There was a sickening crack as the player's leg broke in the tackle, and the crowd went quiet.
= 那名球员在铲抢中腿断了，发出让人心惊的咔嚓一声，全场安静了下来。
a sickening crack = 让人心惊的咔嚓声
③ She is sickeningly good at everything, from maths to music to football.
= 她样样都好得让人嫉妒，从数学、音乐到足球。
sickeningly = （口语）好得让人嫉妒地

sickie
/ˈsɪki/

① He pulled a sickie on Friday so that he could go to the beach.
= 他星期五装病请假，好去海滩玩。

② The manager noticed that a lot of people took sickies on the day after the big match.

take a sickie = 装病请假

sickly
/ˈsɪkli/
核心：形容词：多病的、体弱的：a sickly child；病态的、苍白的：a sickly yellow；（气味、味道）令人作呕的、甜得发腻的：a sickly smell。
① He was a sickly child who missed a lot of school, but he grew up to be very strong.
= 他小时候体弱多病，缺了很多课，可长大后身体非常强壮。
a sickly child = 体弱多病的孩子
sickly = 多病的
② The walls of the old hospital were painted a sickly green that made everyone look ill.
= 老医院的墙刷成了一种病恹恹的绿色，让每个人看上去都像病了。
a sickly green = 病恹恹的绿色
③ The cake was covered in a thick, sickly sweet icing that nobody could finish.
= 那块蛋糕上抹着一层厚厚的、甜得发腻的糖霜，谁都吃不完。
sickly sweet = 甜得发腻

sickness
/ˈsɪknəs/
核心：名词：疾病、患病（不可数或可数）：in sickness and in health；恶心、呕吐（不可数）：travel sickness；（社会的）弊病（可数）。
① There was a lot of sickness in the village during the long cold winter.
= 漫长寒冷的冬天里，村里很多人生病。
sickness = 疾病
② My daughter suffers from travel sickness, so she always sits in the front of the car.
= 我女儿晕车，所以她总坐在前排。
travel sickness = 晕车、晕船
③ When he lost his job because of his illness, he lived on sickness benefit for a year.
= 他因病失业后，靠疾病补助金过了一年。
sickness benefit = （英国旧称）疾病补助金
多和 travel、morning、sea 连用，表示特定的不适。
④ In parts of Africa, the bite of the tsetse fly can give people sleeping sickness.
= 在非洲部分地区，被采采蝇叮咬可能会让人染上昏睡病。
sleeping sickness = 昏睡病

sicko
/ˈsɪkəʊ/
核心：名词，可数（复数 sickos）（口语，贬义）：变态、心理有病的人。
① The police are looking for the sicko who has been sending threatening letters to children.
= 警方正在寻找那个一直给孩子们寄恐吓信的变态。
the sicko = 变态

② Only a sicko would think it was funny to hurt an animal.
= 只有心理变态的人才会觉得伤害动物好玩。
only a sicko would = 只有变态才会

sickout
/ˈsɪkaʊt/

side
/saɪd/
核心：名词，可数：（物体的）边、面、侧面：the side of the box；（左右的）一边、一侧：on the left side；（身体的）侧身、肋部；（争论、比赛中的）一方、一派：take sides；（英式）（体育）队：the home side；（事情的）方面：the funny side of something；（家族的）一方：on my mother's side。形容词：侧面的、次要的。习语：on the side 是兼职、私下；on the safe side 是为保险起见；look on the bright side 是往好处想；be on somebody's side 是支持某人。
① In the exam, write your name and number on one side of the paper only.
= 考试时，只在纸的一面写上你的姓名和考号。
side = 边
② On the last day of term, the children stood side by side for the school photo.
= 学期最后一天，孩子们并排站着拍学校合影。
side by side = 并排
③ There is a small café on the other side of the road.
= 马路对面有一家小咖啡馆。
on the other side = 在另一边
④ Don't worry about what the others said, I'm on your side; I think you were right.
= 别管别人怎么说，我支持你，我觉得你是对的。
be on someone's side = 支持某人
⑤ We missed the train, but look on the bright side: now we have time for lunch.
= 我们没赶上火车，不过往好处想：现在有时间吃午饭了。
look on the bright side = 往好处想
⑥ My parents always argue about money, and I refuse to take sides.
= 我父母老为钱吵架，我拒绝偏袒任何一方。
take sides = 偏袒一方
⑦ It probably won't rain, but take an umbrella just to be on the safe side.
= 大概不会下雨，不过为保险起见还是带把伞。
on the safe side = 为保险起见
⑧ He works in a bank, but he sells his paintings on the side.
= 他在银行上班，不过私下还卖自己的画。
on the side = 兼职、私下
⑨ My grandmother on my father's side came from a small farm in the west of Ireland.
= 我父亲那边的奶奶来自爱尔兰西部的一个小农场。
on my father's side = 父亲那一方
⑩ The home side scored twice in the last ten minutes and won the match.
= 主队在最后十分钟进了两球，赢下了比赛。
the home side = 主队
⑪ At the restaurant by the river, I ordered a steak with a side dish of green beans.
= 在河边那家餐馆，我点了一份牛排，配一份四季豆小菜。
a side dish = 配菜
⑫ The young drummer at the front of the marching band plays a small side drum.
= 走在军乐队最前面的年轻鼓手打的是一面小军鼓。
a side drum = 小军鼓
⑬ The doctor warned me that this medicine can have side effects, such as feeling sleepy.
= 医生提醒我，这种药可能有副作用，比如犯困。
side effects = 副作用
⑭ The question of money is only a side issue; the real problem is time.
= 钱的问题只是次要的，真正的问题是时间。
a side issue = 次要问题
⑮ As a side note for visitors to the city, the museum is free on Sundays.
= 顺便给来这座城市的游客提一句：博物馆星期天免费。
a side note = 附带说明
⑯ Excuse me, can I have a side order of chips with my burger, please?
= 打扰一下，我的汉堡能再配一份薯条吗？
a side order = 配菜（单点）
⑰ Put the bread on the side plate next to your dinner plate.
= 把面包放在你餐盘旁边的小碟子上。
a side plate = （放面包的）小碟
⑱ We turned off the main road and drove down a narrow side road to the farm.
= 我们拐出大路，沿着一条窄小的岔路开到农场。
a side road = 岔路、小路
⑲ She had eaten a big breakfast, so she only ordered a side salad for lunch.
= 她早饭吃得很多，所以午饭只点了一份配菜沙拉。
a side salad = 配菜沙拉
⑳ In the last minute, the striker calmly side-footed the ball into the corner of the net.
= 最后一分钟，前锋冷静地用脚内侧把球推进了球门死角。
side-foot = 用脚内侧踢
㉑ In the old days, women rode side-saddle, with both legs on one side of the horse.
= 旧时女人侧坐骑马，两条腿都在马的同一侧。
side-saddle = 侧坐（骑马）
㉒ The small car was hit side-on by a lorry at the crossroads outside the school.
= 那辆小车在学校外的十字路口被一辆卡车拦腰撞上。
side-on = 从侧面
㉓ The comedy was side-splitting, and the whole audience was crying with laughter.
= 那出喜剧让人捧腹大笑，全场观众笑出了眼泪。
side-splitting = 令人捧腹的
㉔ The school play was side-splittingly funny from beginning to end, and the parents loved it.
= 那出学校话剧从头到尾都滑稽得让人笑破肚皮，家长们喜欢极了。
side-splittingly = 滑稽得让人捧腹地
㉕ We found a little restaurant in a quiet side street behind the market.
= 我们在市场后面一条安静的小街上找到一家小餐馆。
a side street = 小街
㉖ The side view of the house shows the new kitchen at the back.
= 那座房子的侧视图显示了后面的新厨房。
a side view = 侧视图、侧面
㉗ The old soldier had long grey side whiskers that reached his chin.
= 那位老兵留着长长的灰色络腮胡，一直到下巴。
side whiskers = 络腮胡
㉘ My brother always sides with my mother when we argue about the television.
= 我们为看电视争吵时，我哥哥总站在我妈妈那边。
side with somebody = 站在某人一边

sidebar
/ˈsaɪdbɑː(r)/

sideline

sidelong
/ˈsaɪdlɒŋ/

sidereal
/saɪˈdɪəriəl/

① A sidereal day is about four minutes shorter than an ordinary day.

② Astronomers use sidereal time to know where the stars will be in the sky.
= 天文学家用恒星时来推算星星会出现在天空中的位置。

sideshow
/ˈsaɪdʃəʊ/

① At the fair, we threw balls at coconuts at one of the sideshows.

② The argument about the minister's car is just a sideshow; the real issue is the budget.

sidestep
/ˈsaɪdstep/
核心：动词（sidestepped, sidestepped）：（向旁边）跨一步避开；回避（问题、困难）：sidestep a question。名词，可数：横跨一步；回避。
① The young player sidestepped the tall defender and ran straight towards the goal.
= 那名年轻球员一个侧步晃过高个子后卫，直冲球门。

② The minister sidestepped the question about the hospital and talked about schools instead.
= 那位部长回避了关于医院的问题，转而谈起学校来。

sidestroke
/ˈsaɪdstrəʊk/
核心：名词，单数或不可数：侧泳。
① My grandmother still swims the sidestroke every morning, because it is easy on her back.
= 我奶奶每天早上还游侧泳，因为对她的背不费劲。
the sidestroke = 侧泳
sidestroke = 侧泳
② Lifeguards learn sidestroke because it lets them pull someone through the water.
= 救生员要学侧泳，因为侧泳便于把人拖着游。
learn sidestroke = 学侧泳

sideswipe
/ˈsaɪdswaɪp/
核心：名词，可数：顺带的批评、旁敲侧击：take a sideswipe at somebody；（车的）擦撞。动词，及物（美式）：擦撞（车的侧面）。
① In his speech, the mayor took a sideswipe at the newspaper that had criticized him.
= 市长在讲话中顺带讥讽了一下批评过他的那家报纸。
take a sideswipe at somebody = 顺带讥讽某人
sideswipe = 旁敲侧击
② A truck sideswiped our car on the highway, but luckily nobody was hurt.
= 一辆卡车在公路上擦撞了我们的车，幸好没人受伤。
sideswipe a car = 擦撞汽车

sidetrack
/ˈsaɪdtræk/
核心：动词，及物（常用被动）：使离开正题、使分心：get sidetracked。名词，可数：（美式）（铁路的）侧线。
① I meant to finish my homework, but I got sidetracked by a programme on television.
= 我本打算做完作业，可被电视上一个节目分了心。
get sidetracked = 分心、跑题
sidetrack = 使分心
② The teacher tried to sidetrack the class into a discussion about football.
= 老师想把全班引到关于足球的讨论上去。
sidetrack somebody into something = 把某人引到某事上去

sidewalk
/ˈsaɪdwɔːk/
核心：名词，可数（美式）：人行道（英式用 pavement）。
① After school, the children drew pictures on the sidewalk outside their house with coloured chalk.
= 放学后，孩子们用彩色粉笔在家门口的人行道上画画。
the sidewalk = 人行道
sidewalk = 人行道
② A sidewalk artist drew a huge painting of the ocean outside the museum.
= 一位街头画家在博物馆外的人行道上画了一幅巨大的海洋画。
a sidewalk artist = 街头（人行道）画家
英美用词不同：英式说 pavement。

sideward
/ˈsaɪdwəd/
核心：形容词、副词：向旁边的（地）、侧向的（地）。也作 sidewards（副词）。
① In the middle of the song, the dancer took a quick sideward step and then turned round.
= 歌唱到一半，舞者快速向旁边跨了一步，然后转过身来。
a sideward step = 向旁边跨一步
sideward = 侧向的
② When the wave went out, the little crab moved sideward across the sand towards the sea.
= 浪退下去时，那只小螃蟹横着爬过沙滩，朝大海爬去。
move sideward = 横着移动

sideways
/ˈsaɪdweɪz/
核心：副词：向一侧、斜着、侧身：look sideways at somebody；（职位）平调。形容词：向一侧的、侧面的：a sideways glance。
① We had to turn the sofa sideways to get it through the door.
= 我们只好把沙发侧过来才搬进门。
turn something sideways = 把某物侧过来
sideways = 向一侧
② He gave her a sideways look to see if she was angry.
= 他斜着瞟了她一眼，看她生没生气。
a sideways look = 斜眼一瞟
③ She was not promoted; it was a sideways move into another department.
= 她没升职，只是平调到另一个部门。
a sideways move = 平调

sidewinder
/ˈsaɪdwaɪndə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：角响尾蛇（一种在沙漠中侧向爬行的响尾蛇）。
① The sidewinder moves across the hot sand by throwing its body sideways.
= 角响尾蛇在灼热的沙子上把身体往一侧甩，就这样爬行。
the sidewinder = 角响尾蛇
sidewinder = 角响尾蛇
② We saw the strange tracks left by a sidewinder in the desert.
= 我们在沙漠里看到了角响尾蛇留下的奇怪痕迹。
tracks left by a sidewinder = 角响尾蛇留下的痕迹

siding
/ˈsaɪdɪŋ/
核心：名词：（铁路的）侧线、岔道（可数）；（美式）（房屋的）外墙板、护墙板（不可数）。
① The old train has been left in a siding outside the station for years.
= 那列旧火车在车站外的侧线上停了好多年。
a siding = 侧线
siding = 岔道
② The old farmhouse in Vermont has white wooden siding and a bright red front door.
= 佛蒙特州那座老农舍外墙是白色木护墙板，配一扇鲜红色的前门。
wooden siding = 木护墙板

sidle
/ˈsaɪdl/
核心：动词，不及物：（悄悄、怯生生地）侧身走、挨近：sidle up to somebody。
① A young man sidled up to me in the street and asked if I wanted to buy a watch.
= 一个年轻人在街上悄悄凑到我跟前，问我要不要买手表。
sidle up to somebody = 悄悄挨近某人
sidle = 侧身挨近
② The boy sidled into the classroom, hoping that the teacher would not notice he was late.
= 那个男孩侧着身子溜进教室，希望老师不会发现他迟到了。
sidle into something = 侧身溜进某处

siege
/siːdʒ/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：包围、围攻、围城：under siege、lay siege to somebody；（警方的）包围、围捕。
① The castle was under siege for six months, and the people inside nearly starved.
= 那座城堡被围困了六个月，里面的人差点饿死。
under siege = 被包围
siege = 围攻
② The army laid siege to the city and cut off its water supply.
= 军队包围了那座城市，切断了城里的供水。
lay siege to something = 包围某地
③ After a long police siege, the gunman finally came out of the house with his hands up.
= 警方包围了很久之后，那名持枪者终于举着手从屋里走了出来。
a police siege = 警方的包围
比喻用法：the minister was under siege from reporters（部长被记者团团围住）。

sienna
/siˈenə/
核心：名词，不可数：赭色、赭土（一种黄褐或红褐色的颜料）：burnt sienna。
① The artist mixed burnt sienna with a little white to paint the old brick walls.
= 那位画家把熟赭色和一点白色调在一起，画那些旧砖墙。
burnt sienna = 熟赭色
sienna = 赭色
② As the sun went down behind us, the hills in the evening light turned a warm sienna colour.
= 太阳在我们身后落下，傍晚的光线下，群山染上了温暖的赭色。
a sienna colour = 赭色

sierra
/siˈerə/
核心：名词，可数：（西班牙、拉美的）锯齿状山脉。
① We drove for hours through the dry sierra before we reached the little village.
= 我们在干燥的山脉中开了好几个小时的车，才到达那个小村子。
the sierra = 山脉
sierra = 锯齿状山脉
② The snow on the high sierras melts in spring and fills the rivers below.
= 高山上的积雪春天融化，灌满山下的河流。
the high sierras = 高山山脉

siesta
/siˈestə/
核心：名词，可数：（尤指热带国家的）午睡、午休：have a siesta。
① In the village, all the shops close in the afternoon while everyone has a siesta.
= 在那个村子里，下午人人都午睡，所有商店都关门。
have a siesta = 午睡
siesta = 午休
② After a big lunch, my grandfather always took a short siesta in his armchair.
= 吃完丰盛的午饭，我祖父总要在扶手椅上小睡一会儿。
take a siesta = 午睡一会儿

sieve
/sɪv/
核心：名词，可数：筛子、滤网。动词，及物：筛、过滤。习语：have a memory like a sieve 是记性极差。
① When the pasta is ready, pour it into a sieve in the sink to drain the water.
= 意面煮好后，倒进水槽里的滤网沥干水。
a sieve = 滤网
sieve = 筛子
② For a light cake, sieve the flour into a bowl before you add the eggs.
= 想做松软的蛋糕，加鸡蛋之前要把面粉筛进碗里。
sieve the flour = 筛面粉
③ I'll forget your birthday if you don't remind me; I have a memory like a sieve.
= 你要是不提醒我，我准会忘了你的生日，我记性差得很。
have a memory like a sieve = 记性极差

sift
/sɪft/
核心：动词：筛、过筛：sift flour；仔细审查、筛选（信息、证据）：sift through something。
① Sift the flour and sugar together before you mix in the butter.
= 拌进黄油之前，先把面粉和糖一起过筛。
sift flour = 筛面粉
sift = 过筛
② The police are sifting through hundreds of photos to find the man.
= 警方正在仔细查看几百张照片，寻找那个男人。
sift through something = 仔细审查某物
③ The teacher sifted out the best stories and put them on the classroom wall.
= 老师挑出最好的几篇故事，贴在教室墙上。
sift out = 筛选出、挑出

sifter
/ˈsɪftə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（面粉、糖的）筛子、撒粉器。
① She used a flour sifter to make the cake mixture light and smooth.
= 她用面粉筛把蛋糕面糊弄得又松又滑。
a flour sifter = 面粉筛
sifter = 筛子
② A little sugar sifter stood on the table for people who wanted it on their fruit.
= 桌上放着一个小撒糖器，给想往水果上撒糖的人用。
a sugar sifter = 撒糖器

sigh
/saɪ/
核心：动词，不及物：叹气、叹息：sigh with relief；（风）呼啸、沙沙作响。名词，可数：叹气、叹息：a sigh of relief、heave a sigh。
① After an hour at the kitchen table, she sighed and closed the book; the homework was too difficult.
= 在厨房桌边坐了一小时后，她叹了口气，合上了书，作业太难了。
sigh = 叹气
② We all breathed a sigh of relief when the plane landed safely.
= 飞机安全着陆时，我们都松了一口气。
a sigh of relief = 松一口气
③ "I suppose I'll have to do it myself," he sighed, and picked up the brush.
= "我想只好我自己来了。"他叹口气说，拿起了刷子。
sigh = 叹着气说
④ The wind sighed in the tall trees outside the window all night.
= 窗外的高树间，风整夜沙沙作响。
the wind sighs = 风沙沙作响

sight
/saɪt/
核心：名词：视力（不可数）：lose your sight；看见、看到（不可数或单数）：at the sight of something；视野、视线（不可数）：in sight、out of sight；景象、情景（可数）：a beautiful sight；（复数 the sights）名胜、风景：see the sights；（枪的）瞄准器（常用复数）。动词，及物：看到、发现（尤指找了很久的东西）。习语：at first sight 是一见、乍看；catch sight of 是瞥见；lose sight of 是看不见了、忘记；know somebody by sight 是面熟。
① From the top of the cliff, the sunset over the sea was a beautiful sight.
= 从悬崖顶上看，海上的落日真是一幅美景。
sight = 景象
② My grandfather lost his sight when he was seventy, but he still loves listening to the radio.
= 我祖父七十岁时失明了，但他仍然喜欢听收音机。
sight = 视力
③ They fell in love at first sight and were married within a year.
= 他们一见钟情，不到一年就结了婚。
at first sight = 一见
④ I caught sight of my old teacher in the crowd, but she had gone before I could say hello.
= 我在人群中瞥见了我以前的老师，可没等我打招呼她就走了。
catch sight of = 瞥见
⑤ There was not a house in sight, only fields and trees for miles.
= 一眼望去一座房子都没有，方圆几英里只有田野和树木。
in sight = 在视野内
⑥ We waved from the harbour as the boat sailed away, and it was soon out of sight.
= 我们在港口挥手，那条船开走了，很快就看不见了。
out of sight = 看不见
⑦ Don't lose sight of why you started this job in the first place.
= 别忘了你当初为什么干这份工作。
lose sight of = 忘记、看不见了
⑧ I don't know her name, but I know her by sight; she works in the bank.
= 我不知道她叫什么，但她面熟，她在银行上班。
know somebody by sight = 面熟
⑨ We spent the first day in Rome seeing the sights and eating ice cream.
= 我们在罗马的第一天游览名胜古迹、吃冰激凌。
see the sights = 观光、游览名胜
⑩ The little children in the park screamed at the sight of the big black dog.
= 公园里的小孩子们一看到那条大黑狗就尖叫起来。
at the sight of something = 一看到某物
⑪ A tall pillar blocked the sight-line, so we could not see half of the stage.
= 一根高柱子挡住了视线，我们看不到半个舞台。
a sight-line = 视线
⑫ She can sight-read music, so she played the new piece without practising.
= 她能视奏乐谱，所以那首新曲子她没练就弹了出来。
sight-read = 视奏、视唱
⑬ He is a good sight-reader and is often asked to play at short notice.
= 他视奏能力很强，常被临时请去演奏。
a sight-reader = 能视奏的人
⑭ Sight-reading is part of the piano exam, so you have to play a piece you have never seen.
= 视奏是钢琴考试的一部分，你得弹一首从没见过的曲子。
sight-reading = 视奏

sighted
/ˈsaɪtɪd/
核心：形容词：有视力的、看得见的（与盲人相对）；（常构成复合词）视力…的：partially sighted。
① The class was for blind and sighted children, who learned to play music together.
= 那个班是给盲童和视力正常的孩子开的，他们一起学音乐。
sighted children = 视力正常的孩子
sighted = 有视力的
② Partially sighted people can use special phones with large buttons and loud voices.
= 视力部分受损的人可以用有大按键、声音响亮的特制电话。
partially sighted = 视力部分受损的

sighting
/ˈsaɪtɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数：（尤指稀有或寻找中的东西）被看见、目击：a sighting of something。
① There have been several sightings of a wolf in the forest this winter.
= 今年冬天有好几次有人在森林里看到了狼。
a sighting of something = 看到某物
sighting = 目击
② The police have had no reported sightings of the missing girl since Friday.
= 自星期五以来，警方没有接到任何看到那个失踪女孩的报告。
reported sightings = 报告的目击

sightless
/ˈsaɪtləs/
核心：形容词（书面）：失明的、看不见的。
① The old dog was sightless, but it still found its way around the garden by smell.
= 那条老狗失明了，但仍靠嗅觉在花园里认路。
sightless = 失明的
② In the rain, the old statue stared out across the square with sightless stone eyes.
= 雨中，那尊老雕像用一双看不见的石头眼睛凝视着广场。
sightless eyes = 失明的眼睛

sightsee
/ˈsaɪtsiː/
核心：动词，不及物（通常只用 -ing 形式或 go sightseeing）：观光、游览。
① We only had one day to sightsee in Paris, so we started early.
= 我们在巴黎只有一天时间观光，所以很早就出发了。
sightsee = 观光
② Tourists come to sightsee in the old town, but few of them stay the night.
= 游客们来老城观光，但很少有人在那里过夜。
come to sightsee = 来观光

sightseeing
/ˈsaɪtsiːɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：观光、游览：go sightseeing。
① On our first day in Lisbon, we spent the morning sightseeing and the afternoon on the beach.
= 在里斯本的第一天，我们上午观光，下午去了海滩。
spend time sightseeing = 花时间观光
sightseeing = 观光
② In summer the city offers sightseeing tours on an open-top bus every hour.
= 夏天那座城市每小时都有一班敞篷巴士观光游。
a sightseeing tour = 观光游

sightseer
/ˈsaɪtsiːə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：观光客、游客。
① The old bridge was full of sightseers taking photos of the river.
= 那座老桥上挤满了拍河景的观光客。
sightseers = 观光客
sightseer = 游客
② From April to October, the castle is open to sightseers every day except Monday.
= 四月到十月，那座城堡除星期一外每天对游客开放。
open to sightseers = 对游客开放

sigma
/ˈsɪɡmə/
核心：名词，可数：西格玛（希腊字母表第十八个字母，大写 Σ，小写 σ）；（数学）求和符号。
① In maths, a capital sigma means that you add all the numbers together.
= 在数学里，大写西格玛表示把所有的数加起来。
a capital sigma = 大写西格玛
sigma = 西格玛
② The students wrote the Greek letters alpha, beta and sigma on the board.
= 学生们在黑板上写下希腊字母阿尔法、贝塔和西格玛。
the Greek letter sigma = 希腊字母西格玛

sign
/saɪn/
核心：名词，可数：迹象、征兆、标志：a sign of something、show signs of；（路边、商店的）标牌、指示牌：a road sign；手势、示意：make a sign；符号、记号：a plus sign；（星座的）宫：a star sign。动词：签名、签署：sign a letter、sign your name；（与…）签约：sign a contract、sign for a club；（用手语）打手势、示意。习语：a sign of the times 是时代的标志；sign on the dotted line 是正式签字同意。
① The nurse handed me a pen and said, "Please sign here, at the bottom of the form."
= 护士递给我一支笔说：「请在这里签名，在表格的最下面。」
sign = 签名
近义对照：
signature = 签名（名词）
② The sign on the door says that the shop opens at nine.
= 门上的牌子写着那家店九点开门。
sign = 标志
③ There are signs that the economy is getting better, but prices are still high.
= 有迹象表明经济在好转，不过物价仍然很高。
signs that = 有迹象表明
④ At the end of the meal, he made a sign to the waiter to bring the bill.
= 饭快吃完时，他向服务员打手势，示意拿账单来。
make a sign = 打手势
⑤ The empty shops on the high street are a sign of the times.
= 大街上空荡荡的商店是这个时代的标志。
a sign of the times = 时代的标志
⑥ After months of talks, the company finally signed on the dotted line.
= 谈了几个月后，那家公司终于正式签字同意了。
sign on the dotted line = 正式签字同意
⑦ After three years in our local team, the young striker has signed for a big club in Spain.
= 在我们本地球队踢了三年后，那名年轻前锋已签约西班牙一家大俱乐部。
sign for a club = 签约某俱乐部
sign for = 签约加盟；签收
⑧ At our school, all visitors must sign in at reception and wear a badge.
= 在我们学校，所有来访者都必须在前台登记，佩戴胸牌。
sign in = 签到、登录
⑨ Remember to sign out at the front desk when you leave the building in the evening.
= 晚上离开大楼时记得在前台签退。
sign out = 签退、退出登录
⑩ The radio presenter signed off with a song by her favourite band.
= 那位电台主持人用她最爱的乐队的一首歌结束了节目。
sign off = （节目、信件）结束；签字批准
⑪ The manager has to sign off on every payment over a thousand pounds.
= 每笔超过一千英镑的付款都得经理签字批准。
sign off on something = 签字批准某事
⑫ After he lost his job, he had to sign on every two weeks to get his benefits.
= 他失业后，每两周得去登记一次才能领救济金。
sign on = （英式）登记领失业救济；签约受雇
⑬ After she retired, she signed up for a cooking course at the local college.
= 退休后，她在当地学院报名参加了一个烹饪课程。
sign up = 报名、注册
⑭ All the countries have signed up to the new agreement on climate change.
= 所有国家都签署同意了那份新的气候变化协议。
sign up to something = 签署同意某事
⑮ He signed away his right to the house when he moved abroad.
= 他移居国外时签字放弃了对那所房子的权利。
sign away = 签字放弃
⑯ Before she died, she signed the farm over to her eldest son.
= 她去世前，签字把农场转让给了大儿子。
sign over = 签字转让
⑰ At break time, the deaf children signed to each other across the busy playground.
= 课间休息时，那些失聪的孩子隔着热闹的操场互相打手语。
sign to somebody = 向某人打手语

signal
/ˈsɪɡnəl/
核心：名词，可数：信号、暗号：give a signal；（交通、铁路的）信号灯：a traffic signal；（电视、手机的）信号：a weak signal；迹象、预示。动词：发信号、打手势示意：signal to somebody；表明、预示。形容词（书面）：显著的、重大的：a signal success。
① When the guard waved the green flag, it was the signal for the train to leave.
= 列车员一挥绿旗，就是火车出发的信号。
signal = 信号
② The car in front of us stopped suddenly because the traffic signal had turned red.
= 我们前面那辆车突然停了下来，因为交通信号灯变红了。
traffic signal = 交通信号灯
③ Check the signal strength on your phone before you try to make a call.
= 打电话前先看看你手机的信号强度。
signal strength = 信号强度
④ When the head teacher walked in, the teacher signalled to the children to be quiet.
= 校长进来时，老师打手势示意孩子们安静。

⑤ The new law signals a change in the way the country treats refugees.
= 那项新法表明国家对待难民的方式有了转变。
signal a change = 预示转变
⑥ The old signal box by the railway has been turned into a small museum.
= 铁路边那座旧信号楼已经改成了一个小博物馆。
a signal box = （铁路）信号楼
⑦ Engineers are always trying to improve the signal-to-noise ratio, so that the sound is clearer.
= 工程师们一直在努力提高信噪比，让声音更清晰。
the signal-to-noise ratio = 信噪比

significant

signifier
/ˈsɪɡnɪfaɪə(r)/

① In the linguistics class, the teacher explained that the sound of a word is the signifier and its meaning is something else.
= 语言学课上，老师解释说，一个词的读音是能指，它的意义则是另一回事。

signify
/ˈsɪɡnɪfaɪ/

③ The farmers know that dark clouds over the hills signify that a storm is coming.
= 农民们知道，山上的乌云表明暴风雨要来了。

silicon
/ˈsɪlɪkən/
核心：名词，不可数（化学）：硅（一种元素，用于制造电脑芯片）。注意和 silicone（硅树脂、硅胶）区分。
① Silicon is used to make the tiny chips inside computers and phones.
= 硅用来制造电脑和手机里的微型芯片。
silicon = 硅
② The engineer explained that every phone has hundreds of silicon chips inside it.
= 工程师解释说，每部手机里面都有几百个硅芯片。
a silicon chip = 硅芯片

silicone
/ˈsɪlɪkəʊn/
核心：名词，不可数：硅树脂、硅酮、硅胶（用于防水、密封、医疗植入物等）。注意和 silicon（硅）区分。
① We filled the gap between the bath and the wall with white silicone so that water could not get through.
= 我们用白色硅胶填了浴缸和墙之间的缝，防止渗水。
silicone = 硅胶
② She bought a set of silicone baking cases that do not need any oil.
= 她买了一套不用抹油的硅胶烘焙模具。
silicone baking cases = 硅胶烘焙模具

silicosis
/ˌsɪlɪˈkəʊsɪs/
核心：名词，不可数：矽肺、硅肺病（长期吸入石粉引起的肺病）。
① Many of the old miners in the village suffered from silicosis after years of breathing stone dust.
= 村里许多老矿工常年吸入石粉，患上了矽肺。
suffer from silicosis = 患矽肺
silicosis = 矽肺
② Workers who cut stone must wear masks to protect them from silicosis.
= 切割石料的工人必须戴口罩，以防矽肺。
protect somebody from silicosis = 保护某人免患矽肺

silk
/sɪlk/
核心：名词：丝、丝绸（不可数）：a silk scarf；（复数 silks）（赛马骑师的）丝质彩衣；（英式）御用大律师（Queen's / King's Counsel 的别称）。
① She wore a beautiful red silk scarf that her grandmother had bought in China.
= 她戴着一条漂亮的红色丝巾，是她奶奶在中国买的。
a silk scarf = 丝巾
silk = 丝绸
② In early summer, the silk moth lays its eggs on the leaves of the mulberry tree.
= 初夏时节，蚕蛾把卵产在桑树的叶子上。
the silk moth = 蚕蛾
③ The posters were printed by silk screen, so each one is slightly different.
= 那些海报是用丝网印刷的，所以每张都略有不同。
silk screen = 丝网印刷
④ The jockeys in their bright silks waited for the start of the race.
= 穿着鲜艳丝质彩衣的骑师们等着比赛开始。
silks = 骑师彩衣

silken
/ˈsɪlkən/
核心：形容词（书面）：柔滑如丝的、光滑的：silken hair；丝绸做的。
① The little girl had long silken hair that shone in the sun.
= 那个小女孩一头柔顺的长发在阳光下闪闪发亮。
silken hair = 柔滑如丝的头发
silken = 柔滑如丝的
② On the opening night, the singer's silken voice filled the old theatre.
= 首演之夜，那位歌手柔美的嗓音回荡在老剧院里。
a silken voice = 柔美的嗓音

silkiness
/ˈsɪlkinəs/
核心：名词，不可数：柔滑、光滑如丝。
① The woman at the shop said that the cream gives your skin a wonderful silkiness.
= 店里的女士说，这种霜能让你的皮肤格外柔滑。
silkiness = 柔滑
② Cooks love this flour for the silkiness it gives to fresh pasta.
= 厨师们喜欢这种面粉，因为它能让新鲜意面口感柔滑。
the silkiness of pasta = 意面的柔滑

silkworm
/ˈsɪlkwɜːm/
核心：名词，可数：蚕。
① On the farm in China, the silkworms eat mulberry leaves and spin cocoons made of silk.
= 在中国的那个农场里，蚕吃桑叶，吐丝结茧。
silkworms = 蚕
silkworm = 蚕
② One spring, the children kept silkworms in a shoebox and watched them grow every day.
= 有一年春天，孩子们在鞋盒里养蚕，每天看着它们长大。
keep silkworms = 养蚕

silky
/ˈsɪlki/
核心：形容词：柔滑的、光滑如丝的：silky hair；（声音、举止）柔和的、温文的。副词 silkily 是柔滑地、圆滑地。名词 silkiness 另立条。
① Our old cat has long, silky fur that she loves having brushed in the evening.
= 我们家的老猫有一身柔滑的长毛，晚上很喜欢让人梳。
silky fur = 柔滑的毛
silky = 柔滑的
② The waiter spoke in a silky voice and smiled at every customer.
= 服务员说话声音柔和，对每位顾客都微笑。
a silky voice = 柔和的嗓音
③ "Of course, madam," he said silkily, and held the door open for her.
= "当然，女士。"他圆滑地说，替她拉开了门。
silkily = 圆滑地

sill
/sɪl/
核心：名词，可数：窗台（同 windowsill）；（门的）门槛。
① My mother keeps a row of small plants on the kitchen window sill.
= 我妈妈在厨房窗台上摆了一排小盆栽。
the window sill = 窗台
sill = 窗台
② The cat likes to sit on the sill and watch the birds in the garden.
= 那只猫喜欢坐在窗台上看花园里的鸟。
on the sill = 在窗台上

silliness
/ˈsɪlinəs/
核心：名词，不可数：愚蠢、傻气；胡闹。
① The teacher had had enough of the silliness at the back of the class and moved the boys to the front.
= 老师受够了后排的胡闹，把那几个男孩调到了前排。
the silliness = 胡闹
silliness = 傻气
② We laughed at the silliness of the film, with its talking dogs and flying cars.
= 我们笑那部电影的荒唐：会说话的狗、会飞的汽车。
the silliness of something = 某事的荒唐

silly
/ˈsɪli/
核心：形容词：傻的、愚蠢的：a silly mistake、Don't be silly；可笑的、荒唐的：a silly hat；（因喝酒、打击）昏头昏脑的：bore somebody silly（使某人闷得要死）。名词，可数（口语）：傻瓜（多用于对孩子或亲昵）。
① It was a silly mistake, and I lost two marks because of it.
= 那是个低级错误，我因此丢了两分。
silly = 傻的
a silly mistake = 低级错误
② "Don't be silly, of course you can come with us," she said to her little brother.
= "别傻了，你当然可以跟我们去。"她对弟弟说。
Don't be silly = 别傻了
③ He wore a silly hat with ears on it to the children's party.
= 他戴着一顶带耳朵的滑稽帽子去参加儿童派对。
a silly hat = 滑稽的帽子
④ The headmaster's speech went on for two hours and bored everyone in the hall silly.
= 校长的讲话持续了两个小时，把礼堂里的人都闷得要死。
bore somebody silly = 使某人闷得要死
⑤ "You've put your shoes on the wrong feet, you silly," she laughed.
= "你鞋穿反了，小傻瓜。"她笑着说。
you silly = 小傻瓜

silo
/ˈsaɪləʊ/
核心：名词，可数（复数 silos）：（农场的）筒仓、谷仓；（导弹的）地下发射井；（比喻）各自为政的部门：work in silos。
① The farm at the end of our road has two tall metal silos for storing grain.
= 我们路尽头那个农场有两座高高的金属筒仓，用来储存粮食。
silos = 筒仓
silo = 谷仓
② During the Cold War, the missiles were kept in underground silos in the desert.
= 冷战期间，那些导弹存放在沙漠里的地下发射井里。
an underground silo = 地下发射井
③ The departments in the company work in silos and never share information.
= 那家公司的各部门各自为政，从不共享信息。
work in silos = 各自为政

silt
/sɪlt/
核心：名词，不可数：淤泥、泥沙。动词：（使）淤塞：silt up。
① After the flood, the fields were covered with a thick layer of silt from the river.
= 洪水过后，田里盖了一层厚厚的河泥。
silt = 淤泥
a layer of silt = 一层淤泥
② The old harbour has silted up, so big boats can no longer use it.
= 那个老港口已经淤塞，大船再也进不去了。
silt up = 淤塞

silty
/ˈsɪlti/
核心：形容词：多淤泥的、含泥沙的：silty water、silty soil。
① The water in the river was brown and silty after the heavy rain.
= 大雨过后，河水又浑又满是泥沙。
silty water = 浑浊多泥沙的水
silty = 含泥沙的
② Rice grows well in the silty soil near the mouth of the river.
= 稻米在河口附近多淤泥的土壤里长得很好。
silty soil = 多淤泥的土壤

silver
/ˈsɪlvə(r)/
核心：名词：银（不可数）；银器、银餐具（不可数）；银币、硬币（不可数）；银牌（可数或不可数）。形容词：银色的、银白色的；银制的。动词：使镀银、使变成银色。
① She won a silver medal in the hundred metres at the Olympics.
= 她在奥运会一百米比赛中获得了银牌。
silver medal = 银牌
② My grandmother keeps the silverware in a wooden box and only uses it at Christmas.
= 我奶奶把银餐具放在一个木盒里，只在圣诞节用。
silverware = 银器
③ The ring is made of silver, so it is not very expensive.
= 那枚戒指是银的，所以不太贵。
silver = 银
④ She has long silver hair that she ties back in a plait.
= 她一头银白色的长发，编成一条辫子扎在脑后。
silver hair = 银发
⑤ The village has its own silver band, which plays at every festival.
= 那个村子有自己的铜管乐队，每个节日都会演奏。
a silver band = （英式）铜管乐队
⑥ There is no silver bullet for unemployment; the government will need many different plans.
= 解决失业问题没有灵丹妙药，政府需要很多不同的方案。
a silver bullet = 灵丹妙药
⑦ The band got a silver disc when their first album sold a hundred thousand copies.
= 那支乐队的首张专辑卖了十万张，获得了银唱片奖。
a silver disc = 银唱片（奖）
⑧ My grandfather gave me an old silver dollar that his father had brought back from America.
= 我祖父给了我一枚他父亲从美国带回来的旧银元。
a silver dollar = 银元
⑨ Cover the fish with silver foil and bake it for twenty minutes.
= 用锡纸盖住鱼，烤二十分钟。
silver foil = （英式）锡纸
⑩ The children wrapped the presents in silver paper and tied them with red ribbon.
= 孩子们用银色包装纸包好礼物，系上红丝带。
silver paper = 银色包装纸、锡纸
⑪ The man at the antique shop told us that the candlesticks are silver plate, not solid silver.
= 古董店的人告诉我们，那对烛台是镀银的，不是纯银的。
silver plate = 镀银器皿
⑫ For my cousin's wedding, they bought a set of silver-plated spoons as a present.
= 我表姐结婚时，他们买了一套镀银勺子作为礼物。
silver-plated = 镀银的
⑬ The hotel restaurant offers silver service, with waiters serving each dish at the table.
= 那家酒店餐厅提供银盘服务，由服务员在桌边分菜。
silver service = （餐厅的）银盘服务
⑭ The silver medallist cried on the podium, because she had hoped to win gold.
= 那位银牌得主在领奖台上哭了，因为她原本希望拿金牌。
the silver medallist = 银牌得主
⑮ My grandmother is a silver surfer who spends every evening chatting to her friends online.
= 我奶奶是个银发网民，每晚都在网上和朋友聊天。
a silver surfer = （口语）上网的老年人
⑯ The salesman had a silver tongue and could persuade anyone to buy anything.
= 那个推销员能说会道，什么东西都能说服人买。
a silver tongue = 能说会道
⑰ At the trial, a silver-tongued lawyer persuaded the jury that his client was innocent.
= 审判中，一位巧舌如簧的律师说服陪审团相信他的当事人是无辜的。
silver-tongued = 能说会道的
⑱ My parents had a big party for their silver wedding, twenty-five years after they married.
= 我父母结婚二十五周年时办了一场盛大的银婚派对。
a silver wedding = 银婚（结婚二十五周年）

silverback
/ˈsɪlvəbæk/
核心：名词，可数：银背大猩猩（年长的雄性大猩猩，背上有银白色的毛，通常是群体首领）。
① In the forest, the old silverback sat quietly while the young gorillas played around him.
= 森林里，那只年老的银背大猩猩安静地坐着，小猩猩们在他周围玩耍。
the silverback = 银背大猩猩
silverback = 银背大猩猩
② The guide told us that a fully grown silverback can weigh more than two hundred kilograms.
= 导游告诉我们，一只成年银背大猩猩体重可达两百多公斤。
a fully grown silverback = 成年银背大猩猩

silverfish
/ˈsɪlvəfɪʃ/
核心：名词，可数（复数 silverfish）：衣鱼、蠹鱼（一种银灰色的小昆虫，常见于潮湿的地方）。
① We found silverfish in the bathroom, because the floor was always damp.
= 我们在浴室里发现了衣鱼，因为地板总是潮湿的。
silverfish = 衣鱼
② In a damp library, silverfish can damage old books by eating the paper and glue.
= 在潮湿的图书馆里，衣鱼会啃食纸张和胶水，损坏旧书。
silverfish can damage something = 衣鱼会损坏某物

silverside
/ˈsɪlvəsaɪd/
核心：名词，不可数（英式）：（牛的）后腿外侧肉（常用来腌制或焖炖）。
① When I was a child, my grandmother cooked salted silverside with carrots and potatoes every Sunday.
= 我小时候，奶奶每个星期天都做腌牛后腿肉配胡萝卜和土豆。
salted silverside = 腌牛后腿肉
silverside = 牛后腿外侧肉
② The butcher said that silverside is good for slow cooking because it is quite lean.
= 肉铺老板说，牛后腿外侧肉很瘦，适合慢炖。
silverside is good for = 牛后腿肉适合

silversmith
/ˈsɪlvəsmɪθ/
核心：名词，可数：银匠、银器匠。
① The silversmith made the ring by hand in his little workshop behind the shop.
= 那位银匠在店后面的小作坊里手工做了那枚戒指。
the silversmith = 银匠
silversmith = 银器匠
② In the eighteenth century, the little town by the river was famous for its silversmiths.
= 十八世纪时，河边那个小镇以银匠闻名。
its silversmiths = 它的银匠们

silverware
/ˈsɪlvəweə(r)/
核心：名词，不可数：银器、银餐具；（美式）（金属的）刀叉餐具；（口语）奖杯。
① The family kept their best silverware in a locked cupboard in the dining room.
= 那家人把最好的银器锁在餐厅的柜子里。
silverware = 银器
the best silverware = 最好的银器
② After you dry the dishes, please put the silverware in the drawer next to the sink.
= 擦干碗碟后，请把刀叉放进水槽旁边的抽屉里。
put the silverware away = 把刀叉收好
③ The fans are unhappy, because the team has not won any silverware for twenty years.
= 球迷们很不满，因为那支球队二十年没拿过任何奖杯了。
win silverware = 拿奖杯

silvery
/ˈsɪlvəri/
核心：形容词：银色的、银光闪闪的：silvery light；（声音）清脆悦耳的：a silvery laugh。
① From the window of our cabin, the lake looked silvery in the light of the full moon.
= 从我们小屋的窗口望出去，满月的光照下，湖面银光闪闪。
silvery = 银光闪闪的
② We heard the silvery laugh of a child from the garden next door.
= 我们听到隔壁花园传来孩子清脆的笑声。
a silvery laugh = 清脆的笑声

sim
/sɪm/
核心：名词，可数（口语）：模拟游戏、模拟程序（口语简称）。
① My brother plays a city-building sim, where he plans roads, schools and hospitals.
= 我弟弟玩一款城市建造模拟游戏，在里面规划道路、学校和医院。
a sim = 模拟游戏
sim = 模拟程序
② Pilots train for hours on a flight sim before they fly a real plane.
= 飞行员在开真飞机之前要在飞行模拟器上训练好几个小时。
a flight sim = 飞行模拟器

simian
/ˈsɪmiən/
核心：形容词：猿的、猴的；像猿猴的。名词，可数：猿、猴。
① For ten years, the scientists studied simian behaviour in the forests of central Africa.
= 十年来，科学家们在中非森林里研究猿猴的行为。
simian behaviour = 猿猴的行为
simian = 猿猴的
② The actor wore a mask with simian features for his role in the play.
= 那位演员在剧中戴了一副长着猿猴面孔的面具。
simian features = 猿猴般的面貌

similar
/ˈsɪmələ(r)/
核心：形容词：相似的、类似的：similar to something、in a similar way。副词 similarly 是类似地、同样地。
① My sister and I look very similar; people often think we are twins.
= 我和我妹妹长得很像，人们常以为我们是双胞胎。
similar = 相似的
② Your bag is very similar to mine, so let's put our names on them.
= 你的包和我的很像，咱们在上面写上名字吧。
be similar to = 与…相似
similar = 相似
近义对照：
different = 不同
③ The two schools in our town teach maths to young children in a similar way.
= 我们镇上的两所学校教小孩子数学的方法相似。
in a similar way = 用相似的方法
④ At the wedding, the boys were similarly dressed in blue shirts and black trousers.
= 在婚礼上，男孩们都穿着同样的蓝衬衫和黑裤子。
similarly = 类似地

similarity
/ˌsɪməˈlærəti/
核心：名词：相似、类似（不可数）；相似之处、相似点（可数）：similarities between A and B。
① There is a strong similarity between the two paintings, so they may be by the same artist.
= 那两幅画非常相似，可能出自同一位画家之手。
a similarity between A and B = A 和 B 的相似
similarity = 相似
② The teacher asked us to write about the similarities and differences between cats and dogs.
= 老师让我们写猫和狗的相同点和不同点。
similarities and differences = 相同点和不同点

simile
/ˈsɪmɪli/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：明喻（用 like、as 把两样东西作比较的修辞）。比较 metaphor（暗喻）。
① The English teacher asked us to find three similes in the poem.
= 英语老师让我们在那首诗里找出三个明喻。
similes = 明喻
simile = 明喻
② Writers often use simile to help readers picture what a place looks like.
= 作家常用明喻帮助读者想象某个地方的样子。
use simile = 使用明喻

similitude
/sɪˈmɪlɪtjuːd/
核心：名词，不可数（书面）：相似、类似。
① The writer was struck by the similitude between the two old towns on opposite sides of the sea.
= 那位作家惊讶于大海两岸那两座古镇的相似。
the similitude between A and B = A 和 B 的相似
similitude = 相似
② There is a certain similitude in the way the brothers walk and talk.
= 兄弟俩走路和说话的样子有几分相似。
a certain similitude = 几分相似

simmer
/ˈsɪmə(r)/
核心：动词：（用文火）炖、煨、慢煮：simmer the soup for an hour；（怒气、矛盾）酝酿、即将爆发：simmer with anger。名词，单数：文火、慢煮：bring something to a simmer。
① Let the soup simmer for twenty minutes until the vegetables are soft.
= 让汤用小火炖二十分钟，直到蔬菜变软。
simmer = 炖
② Bring the tomato sauce to a simmer over a low heat and then add the cream.
= 用小火把番茄酱汁煮到微微冒泡，再加奶油。
bring something to a simmer = 把某物煮到微沸
③ She was simmering with anger, but she said nothing in front of the children.
= 她心里憋着一肚子火，但当着孩子们的面什么也没说。
simmer with anger = 憋着一肚子火
④ When the class got too noisy, the teacher told everyone to simmer down and open their books.
= 班上太吵时，老师叫大家安静下来，打开课本。
simmer down = 平静下来
比 boil 火小，水面只是微微冒泡。

Simon says
/ˌsaɪmən ˈsez/
核心：名词，不可数：「西蒙说」（一种儿童游戏：只有在指令前说了「西蒙说」时才照做，否则出局）。
① The children played Simon says at the party, and the last one standing won a prize.
= 孩子们在派对上玩「西蒙说」，最后留下的那个赢了奖品。
play Simon says = 玩「西蒙说」
Simon says = 「西蒙说」游戏
② In Simon says, you are out if you touch your nose when the leader didn't say "Simon says" first.
= 在「西蒙说」游戏里，领头的人没先说「西蒙说」，你就去摸鼻子，那你就出局了。
in Simon says = 在「西蒙说」游戏中

simpatico
/sɪmˈpætɪkəʊ/
核心：形容词（口语）：（人）讨人喜欢的、合得来的、志趣相投的。
① I liked my new colleagues at once; they were very simpatico and easy to talk to.
= 我一下子就喜欢上了新同事，他们很合得来，也很好说话。
simpatico = 合得来的
② The two singers were so simpatico that they decided to make an album together.
= 那两位歌手意气相投，决定合作出一张专辑。
be so simpatico = 意气相投

simper
/ˈsɪmpə(r)/
核心：动词，不及物：傻笑、假笑、扭捏地笑。名词，可数：傻笑、假笑。副词 simperingly 是傻笑着、扭捏地。
① She simpered at the rich old man and laughed at all his jokes.
= 她冲那个有钱的老头扭捏作态地笑，他讲什么笑话她都笑。
simper at somebody = 冲某人扭捏地笑
simper = 傻笑
② He gave a silly simper when the teacher praised his drawing in front of the class.
= 老师当着全班夸他的画时，他傻乎乎地笑了一下。
a silly simper = 傻乎乎的一笑
③ "Oh, you're too kind," she said simperingly, and looked down at the floor.
= "哦，您太客气了。"她扭捏地说，低头看着地板。
simperingly = 扭捏地
贬义，指做作、讨好的笑。

simple
/ˈsɪmpl/
核心：形容词：简单的、容易的：a simple question；朴素的、简朴的：a simple meal、simple clothes；（结构）简单的、单一的；纯粹的、完全的：the simple truth；（旧，冒犯）头脑简单的。习语：pure and simple 是纯粹的、不折不扣的。
① The question was so simple that everyone in the class knew the answer.
= 那个问题很简单，班上人人都知道答案。
simple = 简单的
② After the long walk, we had a simple meal of bread, cheese and soup by the fire.
= 走了很久之后，我们在炉火边吃了一顿简单的饭：面包、奶酪和汤。
simple = 朴素的
③ The old coffee machine has a simple design, with only three parts to clean.
= 那台旧咖啡机设计简单，只有三个零件要清洗。
simple = 结构简单
近义对照：
easy = 做起来不难
④ It was laziness, pure and simple; he just didn't want to do the work.
= 那就是懒，不折不扣的懒，他就是不想干活。
pure and simple = 纯粹的
⑤ The simple truth is that we cannot afford a holiday this year.
= 实际情况就是，我们今年负担不起度假。
the simple truth = 实际情况
⑥ He had a simple fracture of the arm, and the doctors said it would heal quickly.
= 他手臂是单纯性骨折，医生说很快就会愈合。
a simple fracture = 单纯性骨折
⑦ The bank pays simple interest on the account, so you only earn interest on the money you put in.
= 那家银行的账户付单利，所以你只能从存入的本金上赚利息。
simple interest = 单利
⑧ The film has a simple-minded story about good men and bad men.
= 那部电影讲的是一个关于好人与坏人、头脑简单的故事。
simple-minded = 头脑简单的、过分简单化的

simpleton
/ˈsɪmpltən/
核心：名词，可数（旧，贬义）：傻子、笨蛋。
① In the old story, everyone thinks the youngest son is a simpleton, but he is the one who wins the princess.
= 在那个古老的故事里，大家都以为小儿子是个傻子，结果却是他娶到了公主。
a simpleton = 傻子
simpleton = 笨蛋
② Don't treat me like a simpleton; I understand perfectly well what you are doing.
= 别把我当傻子，我清楚得很你在干什么。
treat somebody like a simpleton = 把某人当傻子

simplex
/ˈsɪmpleks/
核心：形容词（术语）：单一的、简单的；（通信）单向的。
① The old radio system was simplex, so people could only talk one at a time.
= 那套旧无线电系统是单工的，人们只能轮流说话。
simplex = 单向的
② In mathematics, a simplex is the simplest shape in any number of dimensions.
= 在数学里，单纯形是任意维度中最简单的形状。
a simplex = 单纯形
多见于数学、通信等专业领域。

simplicity
/sɪmˈplɪsəti/
核心：名词，不可数：简单、容易：for the sake of simplicity；朴素、简朴。习语：be simplicity itself 是简单至极。
① The great thing about the plan is its simplicity: anyone can understand it.
= 那个计划最好的地方是简单，谁都能看懂。
its simplicity = 它的简单
simplicity = 简单
② She loved the simplicity of life in the mountains, with no phone and no television.
= 她喜欢山里的简朴生活，没有电话也没有电视。
the simplicity of life = 生活的简朴
③ Using the new machine is simplicity itself; you just press one button.
= 用那台新机器简单至极，你只要按一个按钮。
be simplicity itself = 简单至极

simplification
/ˌsɪmplɪfɪˈkeɪʃn/
核心：名词：简化（不可数或可数）；简化的说法（可数）：an oversimplification。
① The simplification of the tax forms means that people can fill them in more easily.
= 税表的简化意味着人们填起来更容易了。
the simplification of something = 某事的简化
simplification = 简化
② Saying that the war was caused by one man is a simplification of a very complicated story.
= 说那场战争是一个人引起的，是对一段非常复杂的历史的简化。
a simplification of something = 对某事的简化说法

simplify
/ˈsɪmplɪfaɪ/
核心：动词，及物（simplified, simplified）：简化、使简单易懂：simplify the rules。
① The new government wants to simplify the tax rules for small businesses.
= 新政府想简化针对小企业的税务规定。
simplify the rules = 简化规定
simplify = 简化
② The teacher simplified the story so that the younger children could understand it.
= 老师把那个故事讲得简单了些，好让年纪小的孩子听懂。
simplify a story = 把故事简化

simplistic
/sɪmˈplɪstɪk/
核心：形容词（贬义）：过分简单化的、把问题看得太简单的。副词 simplistically 是过分简单地。
① It is simplistic to say that poor people are poor because they don't work hard.
= 说穷人穷是因为不努力，这种说法太过简单化了。
it is simplistic to say = 说…太过简单化
simplistic = 过分简单化的
② The report simplistically blames the teachers for everything that is wrong with the school.
= 那份报告把学校的所有问题都简单地归咎于老师。
simplistically = 过分简单地
和 simple（简单的）不同，simplistic 带批评意味。

simply
/ˈsɪmpli/
核心：副词：简单地、简明地：put it simply；朴素地：dress simply；只是、仅仅：simply because…；（加强语气）简直、实在：simply wonderful；（用于否定）根本：simply cannot。
① To put it simply, we have spent too much money this month.
= 简单地说，我们这个月钱花得太多了。
to put it simply = 简单地说
simply = 简单地
② At the party, she was simply dressed in a white shirt and jeans.
= 在派对上，她穿得很朴素：一件白衬衫，一条牛仔裤。
simply dressed = 穿着朴素
③ I came home early simply because I was tired, not because I was ill.
= 我早回家只是因为累了，不是因为生病。
simply because = 只是因为
④ After the long climb, the view from the top of the mountain was simply wonderful.
= 爬了很久之后，山顶的景色简直太美了。
simply wonderful = 简直太美了
⑤ I simply cannot understand why he left the party without saying goodbye.
= 我根本无法理解他为什么不辞而别，离开了派对。
simply cannot = 根本不能

simulacrum
/ˌsɪmjuˈleɪkrəm/
核心：名词，可数（复数 simulacra）（书面）：模拟物、仿制品、假象。
① The theme park is a simulacrum of an old Wild West town, with saloons and a sheriff.
= 那座主题公园仿造了一个旧时西部小镇，有酒馆，还有治安官。
a simulacrum of something = 某物的仿制品
simulacrum = 模拟物
② After the election, critics said the new parliament was only a simulacrum of democracy.
= 选举后，批评者说新议会只是民主的假象。
a simulacrum of democracy = 民主的假象

simulate
/ˈsɪmjuleɪt/
核心：动词，及物：模拟、模仿：simulate a flight；假装、装出（感情）：simulate interest。
① The computer program simulates the effect of an earthquake on a tall building.
= 那个电脑程序模拟地震对高楼的影响。
simulate something = 模拟某事
simulate = 模拟
② The trainee pilots use a machine that simulates flying in a storm.
= 实习飞行员使用一台模拟在暴风雨中飞行的机器。
simulate flying = 模拟飞行
③ She simulated interest in his holiday photos, but she was really bored.
= 她假装对他的度假照片感兴趣，其实无聊得很。
simulate interest = 假装感兴趣

simulated
/ˈsɪmjuleɪtɪd/
核心：形容词：模拟的、仿造的、人造的：simulated leather。
① The sofa is covered in simulated leather, so it is much cheaper than real leather.
= 那张沙发蒙的是人造革，所以比真皮的便宜得多。
simulated leather = 人造革
simulated = 仿造的
② Before the mission, the soldiers trained in a simulated town built in the desert.
= 执行任务前，士兵们在沙漠里搭建的一座模拟城镇中训练。
a simulated town = 模拟城镇

simulation
/ˌsɪmjuˈleɪʃn/
核心：名词：模拟、仿真（可数或不可数）：a computer simulation；假装（不可数）。
① Scientists used a computer simulation to show how the flood would spread through the city.
= 科学家用电脑模拟展示洪水会怎样在城里蔓延。
a computer simulation = 电脑模拟
simulation = 模拟
② The firefighters took part in a simulation of a fire in a tall office building.
= 消防员参加了一次高层办公楼火灾的模拟演练。
a simulation of something = 某事的模拟演练

simulator
/ˈsɪmjuleɪtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：模拟器、模拟装置：a flight simulator。
① New pilots spend hundreds of hours in a flight simulator before they fly a real plane.
= 新飞行员在开真飞机之前要在飞行模拟器里练几百个小时。
a flight simulator = 飞行模拟器
simulator = 模拟器
② The driving school has a simulator where learners can practise driving on icy roads.
= 那所驾校有一台模拟器，学员可以在里面练习在结冰的路上开车。
a simulator = 模拟装置

simulcast
/ˈsɪmlkɑːst/
核心：动词，及物：（在电视和电台上、或在几个频道上）同时播出。名词，可数：同时播出的节目。
① The concert was simulcast on television and radio, so millions of people could enjoy it.
= 那场音乐会在电视和电台上同时播出，几百万人都能欣赏。
be simulcast = 同时播出
simulcast = 同时播出
② The radio station ran a simulcast of the football match with the local television channel.
= 那家电台和当地电视频道同时转播了那场足球赛。
a simulcast of something = 某节目的同时播出

simultaneity
/ˌsɪmltəˈniːəti/
核心：名词，不可数（书面）：同时性、同时发生。
① The simultaneity of the two explosions made the police think they were planned together.
= 两次爆炸同时发生，让警方认为是事先一起策划的。
the simultaneity of something = 某事的同时发生
simultaneity = 同时性
② The film shows the simultaneity of events by splitting the screen into four parts.
= 那部电影把屏幕分成四块，来表现事件的同时发生。
show the simultaneity of events = 表现事件的同时发生

simultaneous
/ˌsɪmlˈteɪniəs/
核心：形容词：同时的、同时发生的：simultaneous translation。副词 simultaneously 是同时地。
① The conference in Geneva offers simultaneous translation into six languages for all the speeches.
= 日内瓦那次会议为所有发言提供六种语言的同声传译。
simultaneous translation = 同声传译
simultaneous = 同时的
② On Saturday afternoon there were simultaneous protests in twenty cities across the country.
= 星期六下午，全国二十个城市同时爆发了抗议。
simultaneous protests = 同时爆发的抗议
③ At the end of the race, the two runners crossed the finishing line almost simultaneously.
= 比赛结束时，那两名选手几乎同时冲过终点线。
simultaneously = 同时地
④ In the maths lesson, we learned how to solve simultaneous equations with two unknown numbers.
= 数学课上，我们学了怎样解含两个未知数的联立方程。
simultaneous equations = 联立方程

sin
/sɪn/
核心：名词：（宗教、道德上的）罪、罪孽（可数或不可数）：commit a sin；（口语）罪过、不应该的事：it's a sin to…。动词（sinned, sinned）：犯罪、作孽。习语：live in sin 是（旧）未婚同居。
① In the Sunday school class, the priest told the children that lying was a sin.
= 主日学课上，神父告诉孩子们撒谎是罪过。
sin = 罪过
词族：
sinful = 有罪的
② It's a sin to waste good food when so many people are hungry.
= 那么多人在挨饿，浪费好好的食物真是罪过。
it's a sin to do something = 做某事是罪过
③ In the old days, people believed that sickness was a punishment for sin.
= 过去，人们相信疾病是对罪孽的惩罚。
sin = 道德或宗教上的罪
近义对照：
crime = 法律上的罪
④ The old woman went to the church every week to ask God to forgive her because she had sinned.
= 那位老妇人每周都去教堂，祈求上帝饶恕她犯下的罪。
sin = 犯罪、作孽
⑤ The player was sent to the sin bin for ten minutes after a dangerous tackle.
= 那名球员一次危险铲抢后被罚下场十分钟。
the sin bin = （橄榄球、冰球等）受罚席

since
/sɪns/
核心：介词、连词：自从、自…以来：since 2010、since I was a child；副词：此后、从那以后：ever since。连词：既然、因为：since you are here…。习语：ever since 是从那以后一直。
① I have lived in this town since I was five years old.
= 我从五岁起就住在这个镇上。
since = 自从
② Tom and I have been best friends since our first day at the village school.
= 汤姆和我从上村小的第一天起就是最好的朋友。
since something = 自某事以来
③ Since you are here, can you help me carry these boxes upstairs?
= 既然你来了，能帮我把这些箱子搬上楼吗？
since = 既然
④ He left the village in 1990 and has lived in Canada ever since.
= 他 1990 年离开了村子，从那以后一直住在加拿大。
ever since = 从那以后一直
since 表示时间时，主句常用完成时：I have known her since…

sincere
/sɪnˈsɪə(r)/
核心：形容词：真诚的、诚恳的、真心的：a sincere apology、be sincere about something。副词 sincerely 是真诚地；Yours sincerely 是信件结尾的「谨上」。名词 sincerity 另立条。
① The letter from the bank said, please accept our sincere apologies for the mistake with your account.
= 银行来信说，对您账户出的差错，请接受我们诚挚的歉意。
sincere = 真诚的
词族：
sincerity = 真诚
② I don't think he was sincere when he said he was sorry.
= 我觉得他说抱歉的时候并不真心。
be sincere = 真心的
③ You have worked so hard for this, and I sincerely hope that you get the job.
= 你为此付出了那么多努力，我真心希望你得到那份工作。
sincerely = 真诚地
④ Her formal letter to the bank manager ended with Yours sincerely and her full name.
= 她写给银行经理的正式信件以「谨上」和她的全名结尾。
Yours sincerely = 谨启
英式信件开头写了对方姓名时用 Yours sincerely，只写 Dear Sir 时用 Yours faithfully。

sincerity
/sɪnˈserəti/
核心：名词，不可数：真诚、诚恳、诚意。习语：in all sincerity 是坦白说、真心地。
① Nobody in the room doubted the sincerity of her apology, because she was crying.
= 屋里没有人怀疑她道歉的诚意，因为她都哭了。
the sincerity of something = 某事的诚意
sincerity = 真诚
② In all sincerity, I think you should take the job in London.
= 说真心话，我觉得你应该接受伦敦那份工作。
in all sincerity = 说真心话

Sindhi
/ˈsɪndi/
核心：名词：信德人（巴基斯坦信德省的居民，可数）；信德语（不可数）。形容词：信德的、信德人的。
① My neighbour is a Sindhi whose family came to London from Karachi.
= 我邻居是信德人，他家从卡拉奇搬到了伦敦。
a Sindhi = 信德人
Sindhi = 信德人
② In that family, the children learn Sindhi at home and English at school.
= 在那个家里，孩子们在家学信德语，在学校学英语。
learn Sindhi = 学信德语

sine
/saɪn/
核心：名词，可数（数学）：正弦（缩写 sin）。另有两个来自拉丁语的固定说法：sine die（无限期地）、sine qua non（必要条件）。
① In the maths lesson, we learned how to find the sine of an angle.
= 数学课上，我们学了怎样求一个角的正弦。
the sine of an angle = 某角的正弦
sine = 正弦
② The meeting was adjourned sine die, and nobody knows when it will start again.
= 那次会议被无限期休会了，没人知道什么时候会重开。
sine die = （拉丁语，正式）无限期地
③ For the job, a good knowledge of French is a sine qua non.
= 那份工作，精通法语是必备条件。
a sine qua non = （拉丁语，正式）必要条件

sinecure
/ˈsɪnɪkjʊə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（不需要做什么事却有报酬的）闲职、挂名差事。
① His uncle gave him a sinecure in the family business, so he was paid well for doing almost nothing.
= 他叔叔在家族企业里给了他一份闲职，他几乎不干活却拿高薪。
a sinecure = 闲职
sinecure = 挂名差事
② The job of museum director is no sinecure; she works twelve hours a day.
= 博物馆馆长可不是闲职，她一天工作十二个小时。
no sinecure = 不是闲职

sinew
/ˈsɪnjuː/
核心：名词：腱、筋（可数或不可数）；（复数 sinews，书面）力量、支柱：the sinews of power。
① The meat was full of sinew, so it was hard to cut.
= 那块肉全是筋，很难切。
sinew = 筋
② The old professor told his students that trade and money are the sinews of a strong country.
= 老教授告诉学生们，贸易和资金是一个强国的支柱。
the sinews of something = 某事的支柱

sinewy
/ˈsɪnjuːi/
核心：形容词：（身体）筋骨结实的、强健的；（肉）多筋的、老的。
① The old farmer had thin, sinewy arms from years of hard work.
= 那位老农常年劳作，手臂瘦而筋骨结实。
sinewy arms = 筋骨结实的手臂
sinewy = 强健的
② The chicken was so old and sinewy that nobody could eat it.
= 那只鸡太老太多筋，谁都嚼不动。
sinewy = 多筋的

sinfonietta
/ˌsɪnfəniˈetə/
核心：名词，可数：小型交响乐团；小交响曲。
① The city sinfonietta gives free concerts in the park on Sunday afternoons every summer.
= 市小型交响乐团每年夏天的星期天下午都在公园里举办免费音乐会。
the city sinfonietta = 市小型交响乐团
sinfonietta = 小型交响乐团
② Last year the composer wrote a short sinfonietta for her daughter's school orchestra.
= 去年那位作曲家为她女儿学校的乐团写了一首小交响曲。
a short sinfonietta = 一首小交响曲

sinful
/ˈsɪnfl/
核心：形容词：有罪的、罪恶的；（口语）不应该的、浪费的。副词 sinfully 是罪恶地；（口语）放纵地。名词 sinfulness 另立条。
① The preacher told the crowd that their sinful lives would lead them to hell.
= 那个布道者告诉人群，他们罪恶的生活会让他们下地狱。
sinful = 有罪的
a sinful life = 罪恶的生活
② After the party, it seems sinful to throw away all that food, so let's give it to the neighbours.
= 派对之后，把那么多食物扔掉似乎太不应该了，咱们送给邻居吧。
it seems sinful to do something = 做某事似乎太不应该
③ The birthday cake was sinfully rich, with layers of chocolate, cream and nuts.
= 那块生日蛋糕浓郁得让人罪恶，一层层巧克力、奶油和坚果。
sinfully rich = 浓郁得让人放纵

sinfulness
/ˈsɪnflnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：罪恶、邪恶。
① The old poem is about a man who regrets the sinfulness of his younger days.
= 那首古诗讲一个人悔恨自己年轻时的罪孽。
the sinfulness of something = 某事的罪恶
sinfulness = 罪恶
② The preacher spoke for an hour about the sinfulness of the modern world.
= 那个布道者讲了一个小时现代社会的罪恶。
the sinfulness of the world = 世界的罪恶

sing
/sɪŋ/
核心：动词（sang, sung）：唱、唱歌：sing a song、sing to somebody；（鸟）鸣叫；（水壶、风）发出鸣响。习语：sing somebody's praises 是对某人赞不绝口。
① She loves to sing, and she sings in the church choir every Sunday.
= 她喜欢唱歌，每个星期天都在教堂唱诗班唱。
sing = 唱
词族：
singer = 歌手
song = 歌曲
② My mother sang to me every night at bedtime when I was little.
= 我小时候，妈妈每晚睡前都给我唱歌。
sang = 唱（过去式）
③ Before the school concert, the teacher asked me if I had ever sung in front of a big audience.
= 学校音乐会前，老师问我有没有在很多观众面前唱过歌。
have sung = 唱过
④ The birds were singing in the trees outside the tent when we woke up.
= 我们醒来时，帐篷外的树上鸟儿在鸣叫。
birds sing = 鸟儿鸣叫
⑤ Her teachers sing her praises, because she is the hardest worker in the class.
= 她的老师们对她赞不绝口，因为她是班上最用功的学生。
sing somebody's praises = 对某人赞不绝口
⑥ Everyone in the pub sang along when the band played the old song.
= 乐队演奏那首老歌时，酒吧里所有人都跟着唱。
sing along = 跟着唱
⑦ The poet sings of love and the sea in his early poems.
= 那位诗人在早期诗作中歌颂爱情和大海。
sing of something = （书面）歌颂某事
⑧ "Here they come!" the boy sang out as the bus turned the corner.
= 公交车转过拐角时，那个男孩高声喊道："他们来了！"
sing out = 高声喊
⑨ At the rehearsal, the music teacher told the children to sing up because she couldn't hear them at the back.
= 排练时，音乐老师叫孩子们唱大声点，因为她在后面听不见。
sing up = （英式）唱大声点
⑩ On Christmas Eve, the whole family has a sing-song around the piano.
= 平安夜，全家人围着钢琴一起唱歌。
a sing-song = （英式）一起唱歌
⑪ She spoke in a sing-song voice that made her sound like a little girl.
= 她说话带着唱歌似的腔调，听上去像个小姑娘。
a sing-song voice = 抑扬顿挫的语调

singalong
/ˈsɪŋəlɒŋ/
核心：名词，可数：（大家一起的）合唱、一起唱歌。也写作 sing-along。
① The concert ended with a singalong of old songs that everyone knew.
= 音乐会以大家都熟悉的老歌大合唱结束。
a singalong = 合唱
singalong = 一起唱歌
② The new children's film has lots of singalong songs that kids love to learn.
= 那部新儿童电影有很多孩子们爱学着跟唱的歌。
singalong songs = 适合跟唱的歌

Singaporean
/ˌsɪŋəˈpɔːriən/
核心：形容词：新加坡的、新加坡人的。名词，可数：新加坡人。
① We ate delicious Singaporean food at the night market, including chilli crab.
= 我们在夜市吃了美味的新加坡菜，包括辣椒螃蟹。
Singaporean food = 新加坡菜
Singaporean = 新加坡的
② My cousin married a Singaporean, and they live in an apartment near the harbour.
= 我表姐嫁给了一个新加坡人，他们住在港口附近的一套公寓里。
a Singaporean = 新加坡人

singe
/sɪndʒ/
核心：动词（singeing, singed）：烧焦、燎（表面或边缘）：singe your hair。名词，可数：烧焦的痕迹。
① At the birthday party, he leaned too close to the candle and singed his eyebrows.
= 在生日派对上，他靠蜡烛太近，把眉毛燎了。
singe = 燎
singe your eyebrows = 燎了眉毛
② The iron was too hot and left a brown singe on my white shirt.
= 熨斗太烫，在我的白衬衫上留下了一块焦痕。
a singe = 焦痕
注意 -ing 形式是 singeing，和 singing（唱歌）区分。

singer
/ˈsɪŋə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：歌手、歌唱家、演唱者：a pop singer、an opera singer。
① My little sister practises every day, because she wants to be a singer when she grows up.
= 我妹妹天天练习，因为她长大后想当歌手。
singer = 歌手
② The opera singer has a powerful voice that fills the whole theatre.
= 那位歌剧演唱家嗓音洪亮，响彻整个剧院。
an opera singer = 歌剧演唱家

singing
/ˈsɪŋɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：唱歌、歌唱；（鸟的）鸣叫：the singing of birds。
① We heard singing from the church as we walked past on Sunday morning.
= 星期天早上我们路过教堂时，听到里面传来歌声。
singing = 唱歌
② She takes singing lessons with an old opera singer every Thursday after school.
= 她每个星期四放学后跟一位老歌剧演唱家上声乐课。
singing lessons = 声乐课
③ On my first morning in the countryside, the singing of the birds woke me up at five o'clock.
= 在乡下的第一个早晨，鸟儿的鸣叫在五点钟把我吵醒了。
the singing of birds = 鸟鸣

single
/ˈsɪŋɡl/
核心：形容词：单个的、唯一的：a single word；（用于否定、加强语气）一个也（没有）：not a single…；单身的、未婚的；单人的：a single room；（英式）（车票）单程的：a single ticket。名词，可数：单曲唱片；单人房；（英式）单程票；（复数 singles）（网球等）单打；未婚者。动词：挑出、单独选出（常和 out 连用）。副词 singly 是单独地、一个一个地。
① She was single for many years before she met her husband at a friend's wedding.
= 她单身了很多年，后来在朋友的婚礼上遇到了她丈夫。
single = 单身的
② Not a single person came to the meeting, because of the snow.
= 因为下雪，一个人也没来开会。
single = 单个的（加强语气）
③ I booked a single room at the hotel, because I was travelling alone.
= 我在酒店订了一间单人房，因为我一个人旅行。
single = 单人的
近义对照：
double = 双人的
④ At the ticket office I asked for a single to Manchester, because I was coming back by car.
= 我在售票处要了一张去曼彻斯特的单程票，因为我回程坐车。
a single = （英式）单程票
⑤ The band's new single went straight to number one in the first week.
= 那支乐队的新单曲第一周就直接冲上了榜首。
a single = 单曲
⑥ The teacher singled out my essay as the best in the class.
= 老师特别挑出我的作文，说是全班最好的。
single out = 挑出、单独选出
宾语是代词时放中间：single her out。
⑦ In the holiday cottage, the children's room has two single beds and a small desk.
= 度假小屋里，孩子们的房间有两张单人床和一张小书桌。
a single bed = 单人床
⑧ In the old story, the two kings fought in single combat to decide who would rule.
= 在那个古老的故事里，两位国王一对一决斗，决定谁来统治。
single combat = 一对一决斗
⑨ For dessert, pour some single cream over the strawberries just before you serve them.
= 做甜点时，端上桌之前往草莓上浇一点淡奶油。
single cream = （英式）淡奶油
⑩ The countries in the single market can trade with each other without paying taxes at the border.
= 单一市场内的国家互相贸易无须在边境缴税。
the single market = 单一市场
⑪ She grew up in a single-parent family, and her mother worked two jobs.
= 她在单亲家庭长大，母亲打两份工。
a single-parent family = 单亲家庭
⑫ At the job interview, he wore a dark single-breasted suit with three buttons.
= 面试时，他穿了一套有三粒扣子的深色单排扣西装。
single-breasted = 单排扣的
⑬ The children love riding on the top deck, but this route only has single-decker buses.
= 孩子们喜欢坐双层巴士的上层，可这条线路只有单层巴士。
a single-decker = 单层巴士
⑭ By the end of the month, the number of new cases has fallen into single figures.
= 到月底，新增病例数已降到个位数。
single figures = 个位数
⑮ At the age of sixty, she sailed single-handed around the world in a small boat.
= 六十岁时，她驾一条小船独自环游了世界。
single-handed = 单枪匹马的
⑯ After he retired, he single-handedly rebuilt the old farmhouse over ten years.
= 退休后，他花了十年时间独自重建了那座老农舍。
single-handedly = 单枪匹马地
⑰ She is very single-minded about her career and works in the office every weekend.
= 她一心扑在事业上，每个周末都在办公室工作。
single-minded = 一心一意的
⑱ He single-mindedly trained for the Olympics for four years, getting up at five every morning.
= 他一心一意为奥运会训练了四年，每天早上五点起床。
single-mindedly = 一心一意地
⑲ In the end, her single-mindedness made her the best swimmer in the country.
= 最终，她的专心致志让她成为全国最好的游泳运动员。
single-mindedness = 专心致志
⑳ Ireland uses the single transferable vote, so voters list the candidates in order of preference.
= 爱尔兰采用单记可让渡投票制，选民按偏好给候选人排序。
the single transferable vote = 单记可让渡投票制
㉑ The café has stopped using single-use plastic cups and gives you a discount if you bring your own.
= 那家咖啡馆不再用一次性塑料杯，你自带杯子还有折扣。
single-use = 一次性的
㉒ From seven o'clock, the guests arrived singly or in small groups.
= 从七点起，客人们有的单独来，有的三五成群地来。
singly = 单独地、一个一个地
副词 singly 多和 in pairs、in groups 对比着用。
㉓ The museum sells the old postcards singly or in sets of ten.
= 博物馆的旧明信片单张卖，也十张一套卖。
sell something singly = 单个出售某物

singleness
/ˈsɪŋɡlnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：专一、一心一意：singleness of purpose；单身状态。
① She worked with great singleness of purpose and finished the book in a year.
= 她一心一意地工作，一年就写完了那本书。
singleness of purpose = 目标专一
singleness = 专一
② The novel is about a woman who is happy with her singleness and does not want to marry.
= 那部小说讲的是一个满足于单身、不想结婚的女人。
her singleness = 她的单身状态

singlet
/ˈsɪŋɡlət/
核心：名词，可数（英式）：（男式）无袖汗衫、背心；（运动员的）背心式运动衫。
① At the start of the race, the runners wore white singlets with their numbers on the front.
= 比赛开始时，赛跑选手们穿着前面印有号码的白背心。
singlets = 背心
singlet = 运动背心
② In the hot weather, he worked in the garden in a singlet and shorts.
= 天热时，他穿着背心和短裤在花园里干活。
in a singlet = 穿着背心

singleton
/ˈsɪŋɡltən/
核心：名词，可数：单身者；单胎（非双胞胎的婴儿）；（纸牌）单张。
① Every month the magazine is full of advice for singletons looking for love.
= 那本杂志每个月都满是给寻找爱情的单身者的建议。
singletons = 单身者
singleton = 单身者
② The doctor explained that twins are often born a few weeks earlier than singletons.
= 医生解释说，双胞胎往往比单胎婴儿早出生几周。
singletons = 单胎婴儿

singular
/ˈsɪŋɡjələ(r)/
核心：形容词：（语法）单数的：a singular noun；（书面）非凡的、异常的：a singular talent；奇特的。名词，单数（语法）：单数形式：in the singular。副词 singularly 是非常、异常地。名词 singularity 另立条。
① In the grammar lesson, we learned that a singular noun takes a singular verb.
= 语法课上我们学到，单数名词要配单数动词。
a singular noun = 单数名词
singular = 单数的
② The verb must be in the singular if the subject is singular.
= 主语是单数时，动词必须用单数。
in the singular = 用单数
③ She showed a singular talent for music from the age of four.
= 她从四岁起就显示出非凡的音乐天赋。
a singular talent = 非凡的天赋
④ So far, the new manager has been singularly unsuccessful in solving the problem.
= 到目前为止，新经理在解决那个问题上非常不成功。
singularly = 非常

singularity
/ˌsɪŋɡjuˈlærəti/
核心：名词：（物理）奇点（可数）；（书面）奇特、与众不同（不可数）。
① Many scientists believe that the universe began from a singularity billions of years ago.
= 许多科学家认为，宇宙起源于几十亿年前的一个奇点。
a singularity = 奇点
singularity = 奇点
② The singularity of her style made her paintings easy to recognize in any gallery.
= 她独特的风格让她的画在任何画廊都很容易辨认。
the singularity of something = 某物的独特

Sinhalese
/ˌsɪnhəˈliːz/
核心：名词：僧伽罗人（斯里兰卡的主要民族，可数，复数同形）；僧伽罗语（不可数）。形容词：僧伽罗人的、僧伽罗语的。也作 Sinhala。
① Most people in Sri Lanka are Sinhalese, and the second largest group is Tamil.
= 斯里兰卡大多数人是僧伽罗人，第二大民族是泰米尔人。
the Sinhalese = 僧伽罗人
Sinhalese = 僧伽罗人
② When we landed in Colombo, the signs at the airport were in Sinhalese, Tamil and English.
= 我们在科伦坡降落时，机场的指示牌用僧伽罗语、泰米尔语和英语写成。
