sinister
/ˈsɪnɪstə(r)/
核心：形容词：凶险的、不祥的、险恶的：a sinister look、something sinister。
① There was something sinister about the man who kept watching the school gate.
= 那个一直盯着学校大门的男人有点阴森可怕。
something sinister = 有点阴森可怕
sinister = 不祥的
比 strange 语气重，暗示可能有危险或坏事。
② The police do not think there was anything sinister about his death.
= 警方认为他的死没有什么蹊跷。
anything sinister = 什么蹊跷

sink
/sɪŋk/
核心：动词（sank, sunk）：（使）下沉、沉没：the ship sank；（价值、数量）下降、减少；（人）（因疲累）倒下、坐下：sink into a chair；（心情）消沉：my heart sank；挖（井）、打（桩）；（体育）击球入洞。名词，可数：（厨房的）水槽、洗涤槽；（英式）洗脸盆。
① In the fog, the small fishing boat hit a rock and began to sink.
= 雾中，那条小渔船撞上礁石，开始下沉。
sink = 下沉
② Put the dirty plates in the sink, and I'll wash them later.
= 把脏盘子放进水槽，我待会儿洗。
sink = 水槽
厨房的叫 sink，浴室里洗脸用的英式也叫 basin。
③ The ship sank in the storm, but all the sailors were saved.
= 那艘船在风暴中沉没了，不过所有水手都获救了。
sank = 沉没（过去式）
④ After two hundred years, the old ship has sunk deep into the sand at the bottom of the sea.
= 两百年过去，那艘旧船已经深深沉进了海底的沙子里。
has sunk = 已经沉没
⑤ My heart sank when I saw that the last train had gone.
= 看到末班车已经开走，我的心一下子沉了下去。
my heart sank = 心一沉
⑥ After the long walk, I sank into a chair and closed my eyes.
= 走了很久之后，我一屁股坐进椅子里，闭上了眼睛。
sink into a chair = 倒在椅子里
⑦ It took a few days for the bad news to sink in.
= 过了好几天，那个坏消息才让人真正明白过来。
sink in = 被充分理解
⑧ When I tried to take the bone away, the dog's teeth sank into my leg.
= 我想把骨头拿走时，那条狗的牙咬进了我的腿。
sink into something = 陷入、嵌入某物

sinker
/ˈsɪŋkə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（钓鱼用的）铅坠。习语：hook, line and sinker 是完全地、全盘地（相信）。
① Tie a small sinker to the line so that the hook goes down to the bottom.
= 在鱼线上系一个小铅坠，让鱼钩沉到水底。
a sinker = 铅坠
sinker = 铅坠
② He told her he was a millionaire, and she believed him hook, line and sinker.
= 他告诉她自己是百万富翁，她完全信了。
hook, line and sinker = 完全地（相信）

sinkhole
/ˈsɪŋkhəʊl/
核心：名词，可数：（地面突然塌陷形成的）落水洞、天坑。
① A huge sinkhole opened up in the middle of the road and swallowed two cars.
= 马路中间突然塌出一个巨大的天坑，吞没了两辆车。
a sinkhole = 天坑
sinkhole = 落水洞
② Heavy rain can wash away the rock under the ground and cause sinkholes.
= 大雨会冲走地下的岩石，造成地面塌陷。
cause sinkholes = 造成天坑

sinner
/ˈsɪnə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（宗教、道德上的）罪人。
① Standing in the square, the preacher told the crowd that all sinners could be forgiven.
= 布道者站在广场上告诉人群，所有的罪人都能得到宽恕。
sinners = 罪人
sinner = 罪人
② In the story, a poor sinner is saved by the kindness of a stranger.
= 在那个故事里，一个可怜的罪人被一个陌生人的善心救赎了。
a poor sinner = 可怜的罪人

sinuous
/ˈsɪnjuəs/
核心：形容词（书面）：蜿蜒的、弯曲的；（动作）柔美的、灵活的。副词 sinuously 是蜿蜒地、柔美地。
① From the top of the hill, we could see a sinuous river winding through the green valley.
= 从山顶上，我们能看到一条蜿蜒的河流穿过绿色的山谷。
a sinuous river = 蜿蜒的河流
sinuous = 蜿蜒的
② In the dark theatre, the dancer moved sinuously across the stage like a snake.
= 昏暗的剧院里，那位舞者像蛇一样柔美地扭动着穿过舞台。
sinuously = 柔美地

sinus
/ˈsaɪnəs/
核心：名词，可数：（鼻）窦、鼻窦（面部骨头里与鼻腔相通的空腔）。
① I have a cold, and my sinuses are blocked, so I can't breathe through my nose.
= 我感冒了，鼻窦堵住了，没法用鼻子呼吸。
sinuses = 鼻窦
sinus = 鼻窦
② The doctor said the pain in my face was caused by a sinus infection.
= 医生说我脸上的疼痛是鼻窦感染引起的。
a sinus infection = 鼻窦感染

sinusitis
/ˌsaɪnəˈsaɪtɪs/
核心：名词，不可数：鼻窦炎。
① My mother gets sinusitis every winter and has terrible headaches for weeks.
= 我母亲每年冬天都犯鼻窦炎，头痛好几个星期。
sinusitis = 鼻窦炎
② The doctor gave him medicine for his sinusitis and told him to rest.
= 医生给他开了治鼻窦炎的药，叫他休息。
medicine for sinusitis = 治鼻窦炎的药

Sioux
/suː/
核心：名词，可数（复数 Sioux）：苏族人（北美原住民的一支，生活在北美大平原）；（不可数）苏语。
① The Sioux lived on the Great Plains and hunted buffalo for food and clothing.
= 苏族人生活在北美大平原上，靠猎野牛获取食物和衣服。
the Sioux = 苏族人
Sioux = 苏族人
② The museum in South Dakota has a large collection of Sioux clothing and art.
= 南达科他州那座博物馆收藏了大量苏族人的服饰和艺术品。
Sioux clothing = 苏族人的服饰

sip
/sɪp/
核心：动词（sipped, sipped）：小口喝、抿：sip your tea。名词，可数：一小口：take a sip of something。
① She sat by the window, sipping her hot tea and watching the rain.
= 她坐在窗边，小口喝着热茶，看着外面下雨。
sip = 小口喝
② Take a sip of water and a deep breath before you start your speech.
= 开始演讲前先喝一小口水，深吸一口气。
take a sip of something = 喝一小口某物
③ He sipped his wine slowly, because he had to drive home later.
= 他慢慢抿着酒，因为待会儿还得开车回家。
sip your wine = 抿酒
比 drink 量小、动作慢，常用于热饮和酒。

siphon
/ˈsaɪfn/
核心：名词，可数：虹吸管。动词，及物：用虹吸管吸出（液体）：siphon off；（不正当地）抽走、挪用（钱）：siphon money from something。也拼作 syphon。
① We used a plastic siphon to move the water from the fish tank into a bucket.
= 我们用一根塑料虹吸管把鱼缸里的水抽到桶里。
a siphon = 虹吸管
siphon = 虹吸管
② During the night, the thieves siphoned petrol from the cars parked in the street.
= 夜里，小偷们用管子偷吸了停在街上的汽车里的汽油。
siphon petrol = 用管子吸汽油
③ The manager was arrested for siphoning money from the company into his own account.
= 那位经理因把公司的钱挪进自己账户而被捕。
siphon money from something = 从某处挪用钱
④ He had to syphon the water out of the flooded cellar with a long hose.
= 他只好用一根长水管把被淹地窖里的水抽出来。
syphon = 虹吸（siphon 的另一拼法）

sir
/sɜː(r)/
核心：名词：（对男子的尊称）先生、长官（单数，不加名字）；Dear Sir（书信开头）；（大写 Sir，加在名前）爵士。
① The young waiter bowed to the old man and said good evening, sir, your table is ready.
= 年轻的服务员向老先生鞠了一躬，说先生晚上好，您的桌子准备好了。
sir = 先生（对陌生男性的礼貌称呼）
② Because she did not know the manager's name, she began her letter with Dear Sir.
= 因为她不知道经理的名字，她的信以「敬启者」开头。
Dear Sir = 敬启者
③ In the science lesson we learned that Sir Isaac Newton was one of the greatest scientists in history.
= 科学课上我们学到，艾萨克·牛顿爵士是历史上最伟大的科学家之一。
Sir = 爵士（加在名前）
学生在英国学校里也用 Sir 称呼男老师。

sire
/ˈsaɪə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（马、狗等动物的）父本、种畜；（旧）陛下（对国王的称呼）。动词，及物：（雄性动物）繁殖、成为…的父本；（幽默）生（孩子）。
① The sire of the winning horse also won the race twenty years ago.
= 那匹获胜马的父本二十年前也赢过这场比赛。
the sire = 父本
sire = 种畜
② In ten years, the champion dog on the farm has sired more than fifty puppies.
= 十年间，农场那只冠军犬已经繁殖了五十多只小狗。
sire puppies = 繁殖小狗
③ "The enemy is at the gates, sire," the soldier told the king.
= 那名士兵对国王说："陛下，敌人已到城门了。"
sire = （旧）陛下

siree
/sɪˈriː/
核心：感叹词（美式口语）：yes siree / no siree 是（加强语气）对、是的 / 不、绝不。也拼作 sirree。
① When his wife asked if he would help the lazy neighbour, the old farmer said no siree, the man could do it himself.
= 妻子问他会不会帮那个懒邻居，老农说绝不，那人自己能干。
no siree = 绝不
siree = （加强语气）
② When we asked our grandfather if he had enjoyed the party, he laughed and said yes siree, it was the best one in years.
= 我们问祖父派对好不好玩，他笑着说那当然，是这些年来最棒的一场。
yes siree = 那当然

siren
/ˈsaɪrən/
核心：名词，可数：警报器、汽笛；（希腊神话中）塞壬（用歌声诱惑水手的海妖）；（旧）妖艳迷人的女子。
① We heard the siren of a fire engine racing down the street.
= 我们听到一辆消防车鸣着警笛沿街疾驰而过。
the siren of a fire engine = 消防车的警笛
siren = 警报器
② In the old story, the sirens sang so beautifully that sailors steered their ships onto the rocks.
= 在那个古老的故事里，海妖的歌声太美妙了，水手们把船开上了礁石。
the sirens = 海妖塞壬
③ The film star was known as the screen siren of the 1950s.
= 那位电影明星被称为二十世纪五十年代的银幕尤物。
a screen siren = 银幕尤物
常用搭配：sound the siren（拉响警报）、a police siren。

sirloin
/ˈsɜːlɔɪn/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：牛里脊肉、西冷牛排：a sirloin steak。
① At the restaurant by the river, I ordered a sirloin steak with chips and a green salad.
= 在河边那家餐馆，我点了一份西冷牛排配薯条和蔬菜沙拉。
a sirloin steak = 西冷牛排
sirloin = 牛里脊肉

= 每年我奶奶都为圣诞午餐烤一大块牛里脊。
a piece of sirloin = 一块牛里脊

sirocco
/sɪˈrɒkəʊ/
核心：名词，可数（复数 siroccos）：西洛可风（从北非吹向南欧的热风，常带沙尘）。
① When the sirocco blows, the air in Sicily is hot and full of red dust.
= 西洛可风一刮，西西里的空气又热又满是红色沙尘。
the sirocco = 西洛可风
sirocco = 西洛可风
② The farmers worry that the sirocco will dry out their crops in summer.
= 农民们担心西洛可风夏天会把庄稼吹干。
the sirocco will dry something out = 西洛可风会吹干某物

sis
/sɪs/
核心：名词（口语，用于称呼）：姐、妹（sister 的简称）。
① "Thanks for the lift, sis," my brother said as he got out of the car.
= "谢谢你送我，姐。"我弟弟下车时说。
sis = 姐、妹
② My big sis always helps me with my maths homework when she comes home from work.
= 我姐下班回家后总帮我做数学作业。
my big sis = 我姐

sisal
/ˈsaɪsl/
核心：名词，不可数：剑麻、西沙尔麻（用来做绳子、地毯的植物纤维）。
① The floor of the hall is covered with a rough sisal carpet.
= 门厅地上铺着一块粗糙的剑麻地毯。
a sisal carpet = 剑麻地毯
sisal = 剑麻
② In the past, the farm grew sisal to make strong ropes for ships.
= 过去，那个农场种剑麻，用来给船做结实的绳子。
grow sisal = 种剑麻

sissy
/ˈsɪsi/
核心：名词，可数（口语，贬义）：女人气的男孩、胆小鬼。形容词：娘娘腔的、胆小的。带侮辱性，多是孩子间骂人的话，避免使用。
① The other boys called him a sissy because he cried when he fell over.
= 其他男孩叫他胆小鬼，因为他摔倒时哭了。
a sissy = 胆小鬼
sissy = 胆小鬼
② He refused to wear the pink shirt, because he thought it looked sissy.
= 他不肯穿那件粉衬衫，觉得看上去娘娘腔。
look sissy = 看上去娘娘腔

sister
/ˈsɪstə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：姐姐、妹妹、姐妹；（英式）护士长；修女：Sister Mary；（妇女运动中的）姐妹、同伴。形容词：姐妹（关系）的：a sister company、a sister ship。
① My sister is two years older than me, and she is studying at university.
= 我姐姐比我大两岁，在上大学。
sister = 姐姐或妹妹
② My older sister is a doctor, and my younger sister is still at school.
= 我姐姐是医生，我妹妹还在上学。
older sister = 姐姐
younger sister = 妹妹
③ Ask the sister on the ward if you can visit your grandmother after six o'clock.
= 问问病房的护士长，六点以后能不能来看你奶奶。
the sister = （英式）护士长
④ Our town has a sister city in France, and the schools send students there every year.
= 我们镇在法国有一个姐妹城市，学校每年都派学生过去。
a sister city = 姐妹城市
⑤ My sister-in-law is a teacher, and she married my brother last summer.
= 我嫂子是老师，去年夏天嫁给了我哥哥。
a sister-in-law = 嫂子、弟媳、大姑子、小姑子等
⑥ The company has a sister company in Germany that makes the same machines.
= 那家公司在德国有一家生产同样机器的姊妹公司。
a sister company = 姊妹公司

sisterhood
/ˈsɪstəhʊd/
核心：名词：姐妹情谊（不可数）；（妇女的）团体、联合会（可数）；修女会。
① The women on the team feel a strong sense of sisterhood and support each other.
= 队里的女人们有很强的姐妹情谊，互相扶持。
a sense of sisterhood = 姐妹情谊
sisterhood = 姐妹情谊
② The nuns belong to a small sisterhood that runs a school for poor children.
= 那些修女属于一个为穷孩子办学校的小修女会。
a sisterhood = 修女会

sisterly
/ˈsɪstəli/
核心：形容词：姐妹般的、姐妹之间的：sisterly love、a sisterly hug。
① She gave her little brother a sisterly hug before he went off to university.
= 弟弟去上大学前，她像姐姐一样给了他一个拥抱。
a sisterly hug = 姐姐般的拥抱
sisterly = 姐妹般的
② She gave me some sisterly advice about how to deal with my new boss.
= 她像姐姐一样给我出了些主意，教我怎么对付新老板。
sisterly advice = 姐妹般的忠告

Sisyphean
/ˌsɪsɪˈfiːən/
核心：形容词（书面）：（工作）徒劳无功的、永无休止的（来自希腊神话中西西弗斯推石上山的故事）。
① Keeping the garden free of weeds is a Sisyphean task; they grow back every week.
= 让花园里不长杂草是件徒劳的事，杂草每周都会长回来。
a Sisyphean task = 徒劳无功的任务
Sisyphean = 徒劳无功的
② Cleaning the house with three small children feels like a Sisyphean job.
= 家里有三个小孩，打扫房子感觉永远干不完。
a Sisyphean job = 永无休止的工作

sit
/sɪt/
核心：动词（sat, sat）：坐、坐着：sit on a chair、sit at a table；使坐下：sit somebody down；（议会、法庭）开会、开庭；参加（考试）：sit an exam；（东西）位于、放在；（衣服）合身。习语：sit tight 是待着别动、耐心等着；sit on the fence 是骑墙、保持中立。
① When the guests arrived, my mother told them to sit down and make themselves comfortable.
= 客人们到了，我妈妈请他们坐下，别拘束。
sit down = 坐下
sit = 坐下这个动作或状态
近义对照：
seat = 座位（名词）
② On the first warm day of spring, we sat in the garden all afternoon, drinking tea and talking.
= 开春第一个暖和天，我们在花园里坐了一下午，喝茶聊天。
sat = 坐（过去式）
③ The students in the top class sit their final exams in June, just before the summer holidays.
= 最高年级的学生六月参加期末考试，正好在暑假之前。
sit an exam = 参加考试
英式说 sit an exam，美式多说 take an exam。
④ In that country, parliament sits from October to July, with a break at Christmas.
= 在那个国家，议会从十月到七月开会，圣诞节有一段休会。
parliament sits = 议会开会
⑤ When it started to rain, my father told us to sit tight while he went to get the car.
= 下起雨来时，我父亲叫我们待着别动，他去把车开过来。
sit tight = 待着别动
⑥ The minister is sitting on the fence and won't say whether he supports the plan.
= 那位部长在骑墙，不肯说他是否支持那个计划。
sit on the fence = 骑墙、保持中立
⑦ During the long summer holidays, the teenagers sat about in the park all day doing nothing.
= 漫长的暑假里，那些青少年一整天坐在公园里无所事事。
sit about = 闲坐着
⑧ Don't just sit around waiting for something to happen; go out and look for a job.
= 别光坐着等事情发生，出去找工作吧。
sit around = 闲坐着
⑨ As the lights went down in the cinema, a voice told us to sit back, relax and enjoy the film.
= 影院灯光暗下来时，一个声音叫我们往后靠，放松，好好看电影。
sit back = 往后靠；袖手旁观
⑩ We cannot sit by and watch while the old library is closed.
= 我们不能坐视那座老图书馆被关掉。
sit by = 坐视不管
⑪ For her portrait, she sat for the painter every morning for a month in his studio by the sea.
= 为了那幅肖像，她连续一个月每天早上在海边画室里给那位画家当模特。
sit for somebody = 给某人当模特
⑫ The manager is on holiday, so his assistant is sitting in for him.
= 经理在休假，所以由他的助理代他工作。
sit in for somebody = 代替某人
⑬ The head teacher sat in on our lesson to see how the new teacher was doing.
= 校长来旁听我们的课，看看新老师教得怎么样。
sit in on something = 旁听某事
⑭ The council has been sitting on my complaint for three months and has done nothing.
= 市议会把我的投诉压了三个月，什么也没做。
sit on something = 把某事压着不办
⑮ He hurt his ankle and had to sit out the rest of the match.
= 他扭伤了脚踝，只好在比赛剩下的时间里坐着不上场。
sit out = 不参加、坐等结束
⑯ We had to sit through three hours of speeches before dinner was served.
= 晚饭上桌之前，我们不得不耐着性子听完三个小时的讲话。
sit through something = 耐着性子坐到结束
⑰ We sat up until two in the morning, talking about the old days.
= 我们熬到凌晨两点，聊着过去的事。
sit up = 熬夜不睡；坐直
叫人坐直时说 Sit up straight!
⑱ Sit yourself down by the fire and I'll make you a cup of tea.
= 你在炉边坐下，我给你泡杯茶。
sit yourself down = （口语）坐下
⑲ After dinner, my brother said it was time we sat down and did our homework together.
= 晚饭后，我哥哥说该是我们坐下来一起做作业的时候了。
sit down and do = 坐下来做（某事）
⑳ The students held a sit-in in the university library to protest about the fees.
= 学生们在大学图书馆静坐抗议学费。
a sit-in = 静坐抗议

sitar
/sɪˈtɑː(r)/
核心：名词，可数：西塔尔琴（一种长颈的印度弦乐器）。
① At the festival, the musician played the sitar while his friend played the drums.
= 在音乐节上，那位乐手弹西塔尔琴，他的朋友打鼓。
the sitar = 西塔尔琴
sitar = 西塔尔琴
② The Beatles used a sitar on some of their songs in the 1960s.
= 二十世纪六十年代，披头士在他们的一些歌里用了西塔尔琴。
use a sitar = 用西塔尔琴

sitcom
/ˈsɪtkɒm/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：情景喜剧（situation comedy 的简称）。
① My favourite sitcom is about four friends who share a flat in London.
= 我最喜欢的情景喜剧讲的是四个合租伦敦一套公寓的朋友。
a sitcom = 情景喜剧
sitcom = 情景喜剧
② The young actor became famous after starring in a popular American sitcom about a family of doctors.
= 那位年轻演员主演了一部讲医生之家的热门美国情景喜剧后一举成名。

site
/saɪt/
核心：名词，可数：地点、场所、位置：the site of something；（建筑的）工地、场地：a building site；网站：a website。动词，及物（常用被动）：使位于、选址：be sited near something。注意和 sight（视力、景象）同音。
① This is the site of an old Roman town, and you can still see the walls.
= 这里是一座古罗马城镇的遗址，你还能看到城墙。
site = 场地
site = 地点
② The sign at the gate says that everyone must wear a hard hat on the construction site.
= 门口的牌子上写着，在建筑工地上人人必须戴安全帽。
construction site = 工地
③ For two years, the engineers stayed on site until the new bridge was finished.
= 两年里，工程师们一直待在现场，直到新桥建好。
on site = 在现场
反义是 off site（在场外）。
④ From the old castle site on the hill, we had a wonderful sight of the whole valley.
= 从山上的古堡遗址望去，整个山谷的景色尽收眼底。
sight = 视力、景象
⑤ The council has decided that the new factory will be sited near the motorway, away from the houses.
= 市议会决定，新工厂将建在高速公路附近，远离住宅区。
be sited near something = 选址在某处附近
⑥ The marshes by the river are a site of special scientific interest, so nobody is allowed to build there.
= 河边的沼泽地是特殊科学价值保护区，所以谁也不许在那里建房。
a site of special scientific interest = （英国）特殊科学价值保护区

sitter
/ˈsɪtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（为画家、摄影师）当模特的人；（照看孩子的）临时保姆（babysitter 的简称）；（照看宠物、房子的）看管人。
① The painter asked his sitter to keep very still for an hour.
= 那位画家叫他的模特一个小时保持一动不动。
his sitter = 他的模特
sitter = 模特
② We need to find a sitter for the children on Saturday night.
= 我们得给星期六晚上找个临时保姆照看孩子。
a sitter = 临时保姆
③ A pet sitter came every day to feed our cat while we were on holiday.
= 我们度假时，一位宠物看护每天都来喂我们的猫。
a pet sitter = 宠物看护

sitting
/ˈsɪtɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数：（议会、法庭的）一次会议、开庭；（用餐的）一轮、一批：the first sitting；（当模特的）一次坐着让人画或拍。形容词：在任的、现任的：a sitting MP。习语：at one sitting 是一口气、一次坐着完成。
① The hotel serves dinner in two sittings, at seven and at half past eight.
= 那家酒店分两轮供应晚餐，七点一轮，八点半一轮。
two sittings = 两轮（用餐）
sitting = 一轮
② I read the whole book at one sitting because I couldn't put it down.
= 我一口气读完了整本书，因为放不下来。
at one sitting = 一口气
③ In the election, the sitting MP lost his seat to a young teacher from the village.
= 选举中，那位现任议员把席位输给了村里的一个年轻教师。
the sitting MP = 现任议员
④ On winter evenings, we sat in the sitting room and watched the fire.
= 冬天的晚上，我们坐在起居室里看着炉火。
the sitting room = （英式）起居室
也叫 living room、lounge。
⑤ The landlord could not sell the house, because it had a sitting tenant who had lived there for forty years.
= 房东卖不掉那所房子，因为里面有一位住了四十年的现住租户。
a sitting tenant = （英式）现住租户

situate
/ˈsɪtʃueɪt/
核心：动词，及物（正式）：使处于、把…放在（某种环境、背景中）；（建筑）选址。
① The writer tries to situate the story in the history of the town.
= 那位作家试图把那个故事放到小镇的历史背景中去讲。
situate something in something = 把某事置于某背景中
situate = 使处于
② The architects situated the new school close to the park so that children could play outside.
= 建筑师们把新学校建在公园附近，好让孩子们能在外面玩。
situate a building = 给建筑选址

situated
/ˈsɪtʃueɪtɪd/
核心：形容词：位于…的、坐落在…的：be situated in / on / near something；处于某种境地的：be well situated to do something。
① The hotel is situated on a hill above the town, with views of the sea.
= 那家酒店坐落在小镇上方的一座山上，能看到大海。
be situated on something = 坐落在某处
situated = 位于…的
② With her experience, she is well situated to run the new department.
= 凭她的经验，她很有条件管好那个新部门。
be well situated to do something = 很有条件做某事

situation
/ˌsɪtʃuˈeɪʃn/
核心：名词，可数：情况、形势、处境：the current situation、in a difficult situation；（书面）位置、地点；（旧）职位：Situations Vacant（招聘栏）。
① The situation in the hospital is getting worse because there are not enough nurses.
= 由于护士不够，医院的情况越来越糟。
situation = 情况
② The government will meet tomorrow morning to discuss the current situation in the north.
= 政府明天上午开会讨论北部目前的局势。
current situation = 目前情况
situation 指整体局面，常和 difficult、economic、political 连用。
③ The new manager handled the situation very calmly and nobody lost their job.
= 新经理非常冷静地处理了局面，没有人丢掉工作。
handle a situation = 处理局面
④ She found herself in a difficult situation, with no money and nowhere to sleep.
= 她陷入了困境，身无分文，无处过夜。
situation = 整体局面
近义对照：
condition = 状态、条件
⑤ The house has a beautiful situation on the edge of a lake.
= 那座房子位于湖边，地理位置极佳。
a beautiful situation = 极佳的位置
⑥ The old situation comedy about a family running a hotel is still shown on television every Saturday evening.
= 那部讲一家人开旅馆的老情景喜剧，现在每个星期六晚上还在电视上播。
a situation comedy = 情景喜剧（简称 sitcom）

situational
/ˌsɪtʃuˈeɪʃənl/
核心：形容词：情境的、视情况而定的：situational comedy、situational awareness。
① Pilots need good situational awareness so that they always know what is happening around the plane.
= 飞行员需要良好的态势感知，随时了解飞机周围的情况。
situational awareness = 态势感知
situational = 情境的
② The book teaches situational English for travellers, such as how to order food or ask for directions.
= 那本书教旅行者情景英语，比如怎么点菜、怎么问路。
situational English = 情景英语

six
/sɪks/
核心：数词：六、六个；六岁；六点。名词，可数：（板球）六分球；（数字）六。习语：at sixes and sevens 是乱七八糟、不知所措；six of one and half a dozen of the other 是半斤八两。
① There are six eggs in the box, so we can make a big omelette.
= 盒子里有六个鸡蛋，我们可以做一个大煎蛋卷。
six = 六
② The office was at sixes and sevens after the computers broke down.
= 电脑坏了以后，办公室里一片混乱。
at sixes and sevens = 乱七八糟
③ It doesn't matter which bus we take; it's six of one and half a dozen of the other.
= 坐哪趟车都无所谓，半斤八两。
six of one and half a dozen of the other = 半斤八两
④ After ten years at the firm, she earns a six-figure salary as a lawyer in the city.
= 在那家律所干了十年，她在城里当律师，年薪六位数。
a six-figure salary = 六位数的薪水
six-figure = 六位数的
⑤ In the old western film, the sheriff carried a six-gun on each hip.
= 在那部老西部片里，治安官两边腰上各挂一把六发左轮枪。
a six-gun = 六发左轮枪
⑥ At the end of the film, the cowboy pulled out his six-shooter and fired into the air.
= 电影结尾，那个牛仔掏出左轮手枪朝天开了一枪。
a six-shooter = 六发左轮手枪
⑦ On the way to the beach, we bought a six-pack of cold drinks for the picnic.
= 去海滩的路上，我们为野餐买了一盒六罐装的冷饮。
a six-pack = 六罐装；（口语）六块腹肌

sixfold
/ˈsɪksfəʊld/
核心：形容词、副词：六倍的（地）：a sixfold increase。
① There has been a sixfold increase in the number of people cycling to work.
= 骑车上班的人数增加了五倍（是原来的六倍）。
a sixfold increase = 增至六倍
sixfold = 六倍的
② The price of the old house has risen sixfold since my grandparents bought it.
= 自从我祖父母买下那座老房子以来，房价涨到了原来的六倍。
rise sixfold = 涨到六倍

sixpence
/ˈsɪkspəns/
核心：名词，可数（英式）：（旧）六便士硬币；六便士。
① My grandmother remembers the days when a bag of sweets cost sixpence.
= 我奶奶还记得一袋糖卖六便士的年代。
sixpence = 六便士
② In the old days, people put a silver sixpence in the Christmas pudding for good luck.
= 过去，人们在圣诞布丁里放一枚银六便士硬币图个好运。
a silver sixpence = 银六便士硬币

sixteen
/ˌsɪksˈtiːn/
核心：数词：十六、十六个；十六岁。
① In many countries, you can leave school at sixteen and start work.
= 在许多国家，十六岁就可以离开学校去工作。
at sixteen = 十六岁时
sixteen = 十六
② There are sixteen students in the class, so we can make four groups of four.
= 班上有十六个学生，所以我们可以分成四个四人组。
sixteen students = 十六个学生

sixteenth
/ˌsɪksˈtiːnθ/
核心：序数词：第十六；名词：十六分之一。
① The church was built in the sixteenth century and is the oldest building in the town.
= 那座教堂建于十六世纪，是镇上最古老的建筑。
the sixteenth century = 十六世纪
sixteenth = 第十六
② In music, a sixteenth note is a very short note, a quarter of a beat.
= 在音乐里，十六分音符是很短的音符，占四分之一拍。
a sixteenth note = （美式）十六分音符

sixth
/sɪksθ/
核心：序数词：第六；名词：六分之一。
① My son is in the sixth year at school and wants to study medicine.
= 我儿子在学校读六年级，想学医。
the sixth year = 第六年、六年级
sixth = 第六
② She had a sixth sense that something was wrong, so she phoned her mother.
= 她凭第六感觉得出事了，就给母亲打了电话。
a sixth sense = 第六感
③ At our school, the sixth-formers help the younger children with their reading at lunchtime.
= 在我们学校，午饭时间中学高年级生帮低年级孩子练阅读。
sixth-formers = （英国）中学最后两年的学生

sixtieth
/ˈsɪkstiəθ/
核心：序数词：第六十；名词：六十分之一。
① Last month we had a surprise party for my father's sixtieth birthday.
= 上个月我们给我父亲的六十岁生日办了一场惊喜派对。
a sixtieth birthday = 六十岁生日
sixtieth = 第六十
② Last summer the town celebrated the sixtieth anniversary of the bridge with fireworks.
= 去年夏天，镇上放烟花庆祝那座桥落成六十周年。
the sixtieth anniversary = 六十周年

sixty
/ˈsɪksti/
核心：数词：六十、六十个；六十岁。名词：（复数 the sixties）六十年代；（one's sixties）六十多岁。
① My grandfather is sixty, but he still runs five kilometres every morning.
= 我祖父六十岁了，但每天早上还跑五公里。
sixty = 六十
② The music of the sixties is still very popular with young people.
= 六十年代的音乐至今仍很受年轻人欢迎。
the sixties = 六十年代
③ In American music, a sixty-fourth note is half the length of a thirty-second note.
= 在美式乐理里，六十四分音符的时值是三十二分音符的一半。
a sixty-fourth note = （美式）六十四分音符

size
/saɪz/
核心：名词：大小、尺寸（不可数或可数）：the size of something；（衣服、鞋的）尺码、号（可数）：What size are you?；规模、数量。动词，及物：按大小分类；定尺寸。习语：cut somebody down to size 是挫某人的锐气；try something for size 是试试看合不合适。
① These shoes are the wrong size; do you have them in a size seven?
= 这双鞋尺码不对，有七号的吗？
size = 尺码
问尺码说 What size are you? 回答用数字：I'm a size twelve.
② My brother and I are the same size, so we often share clothes.
= 我和我哥哥身材一样，所以常常换着穿衣服。
the same size = 一样大
③ We were surprised by the size of the house; it had twelve bedrooms.
= 我们对那座房子的大小感到吃惊，它有十二间卧室。
the size of something = 某物的大小
④ The coach sized up the new player and decided to put him in the team.
= 教练打量了一下那个新球员，决定让他进队。
size up = 打量、估计
⑤ The champion was cut down to size when a young player beat him in the first round.
= 那位冠军首轮就输给了一个年轻选手，锐气大挫。
cut somebody down to size = 挫某人的锐气
⑥ The shop assistant told him to try the jacket for size, because she thought it would fit.
= 店员叫他试试那件夹克合不合身，她觉得他穿正合适。
try something for size = 试试合不合适
⑦ Doctors are worried that size zero models are a bad example for young girls.
= 医生们担心零码模特给小女孩们树立了坏榜样。
size zero = 零码（极瘦）

sizeable
/ˈsaɪzəbl/
核心：形容词：相当大的、可观的：a sizeable amount。也拼作 sizable。
① When her aunt died, she inherited a sizeable amount of money and a house by the sea.
= 姑姑去世后，她继承了一笔可观的钱和一座海边的房子。
a sizeable amount = 相当大的数目
比 big 正式，多修饰钱、数量、群体。
sizeable = 相当大的
② The town has a sizeable Polish community, with its own shops and church.
= 那个小镇有一个相当大的波兰人社区，有自己的商店和教堂。
a sizeable community = 相当大的社区

sizzle
/ˈsɪzl/
核心：动词，不及物：（油炸、烤时）发出咝咝声：sizzle in the pan；（口语）（天气）炎热。名词，单数：咝咝声。
① The sausages sizzled in the pan, and the smell filled the kitchen.
= 香肠在锅里咝咝作响，香味飘满了厨房。
sizzle = 发出咝咝声
sizzle in the pan = 在锅里咝咝作响
② We could hear the sizzle of meat on the barbecue from the garden next door.
= 我们能听到隔壁花园里烤肉的咝咝声。
the sizzle of meat = 肉的咝咝声

sizzling
/ˈsɪzlɪŋ/
核心：形容词：（口语）酷热的：a sizzling summer；（食物）咝咝作响的、热腾腾的；精彩的、火辣的。
① During the sizzling summer of 1976, the rivers dried up and the grass turned brown.
= 1976 年那个酷热的夏天，河流干涸，草都变黄了。
a sizzling summer = 酷热的夏天
sizzling = 酷热的
② The waiter brought a sizzling plate of chicken straight from the kitchen.
= 服务员直接从厨房端来一盘咝咝作响的鸡肉。
a sizzling plate = 热腾腾的铁板菜

ska
/skɑː/
核心：名词，不可数：斯卡音乐（源于牙买加、节奏轻快、带铜管乐的流行音乐，是雷鬼乐的前身）。
① My father loved ska when he was young and still dances to it in the kitchen.
= 我父亲年轻时喜欢斯卡音乐，现在还在厨房里跟着跳舞。
ska = 斯卡音乐
② The ska band had three trumpets and a saxophone, and everyone in the hall was dancing.
= 那支斯卡乐队有三把小号和一把萨克斯，礼堂里人人都在跳舞。
a ska band = 斯卡乐队

skank
/skæŋk/
核心：名词，不可数：（跟着雷鬼、斯卡音乐跳的）摇摆舞。动词：跳这种舞。另有一个侮辱女性的俚语用法，非常粗俗，不要使用。
① At the festival, everyone was doing the skank in front of the reggae stage.
= 音乐节上，雷鬼舞台前人人都在跳摇摆舞。
do the skank = 跳摇摆舞
skank = 摇摆舞
② The young people skanked to the music until two in the morning.
= 年轻人跟着音乐跳舞，一直跳到凌晨两点。
skank to the music = 跟着音乐跳摇摆舞

skanky
/ˈskæŋki/
核心：形容词（俚语，粗俗）：脏兮兮的、令人恶心的；（对人）带侮辱意味。语气很粗，避免使用。
① The flat was skanky, with dirty dishes in the sink and old pizza boxes on the floor.
= 那套公寓脏兮兮的，水槽里堆着脏碗，地上扔着旧比萨盒。
skanky = 脏兮兮的
② He refused to sleep on the skanky old mattress in the back room.
= 他不肯睡在后屋那张脏兮兮的旧床垫上。
a skanky old mattress = 脏兮兮的旧床垫

skate
/skeɪt/
核心：名词，可数：冰鞋、旱冰鞋：a pair of skates；（复数 skate 或 skates）鳐鱼。动词，不及物：溜冰、滑冰、滑旱冰；（比喻）回避、轻描淡写地带过：skate over something。习语：get your skates on 是（英式口语）赶快。
① On the coldest morning of the year, she put on her skates and glided across the frozen lake.
= 在一年中最冷的那个早晨，她穿上冰鞋，在结冰的湖面上滑了过去。
skates = 冰鞋
skate = 冰鞋
② At the Christmas fair, the children skated round the rink holding hands.
= 在圣诞集市上，孩子们手拉着手绕着溜冰场滑。
skate = 溜冰
③ My mother shouted up the stairs that we should get our skates on, or we would miss the train.
= 我妈妈冲楼上喊，叫我们快点，不然要误火车了。
get your skates on = 赶快
④ The minister skated over the question of how much the new bridge would cost.
= 那位部长对新桥要花多少钱的问题轻描淡写地带过了。
skate over something = 对某事轻描淡写地带过
⑤ The fish shop by the harbour sells fresh skate on Fridays, straight from the boats.
= 港口边那家鱼店每周五卖刚从船上卸下的新鲜鳐鱼。
skate = 鳐鱼
⑥ To the skatepark he always wore a pair of flat skate shoes with thick laces.
= 去滑板公园时，他总穿一双系粗鞋带的平底滑板鞋。
skate shoes = 滑板鞋

skateboard
/ˈskeɪtbɔːd/
核心：名词，可数：滑板。动词，不及物：玩滑板。
① My brother got a skateboard for his birthday and fell off it on the first day.
= 我弟弟生日时得到一块滑板，第一天就摔了下来。
a skateboard = 滑板
skateboard = 滑板
② The teenagers skateboarded down the hill and across the empty car park.
= 那些青少年踩着滑板冲下山坡，穿过空荡荡的停车场。
skateboard down something = 踩滑板冲下某处

skateboarder
/ˈskeɪtbɔːdə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：玩滑板的人、滑板运动员。
① After school, the skateboarders practise their jumps on the steps outside the library.
= 放学后，那些玩滑板的人在图书馆外的台阶上练跳跃。
skateboarders = 玩滑板的人
skateboarder = 滑板运动员
② At only fourteen, she is one of the best young skateboarders in the country.
= 她才十四岁，就是全国最优秀的年轻滑板运动员之一。
young skateboarders = 年轻的滑板运动员

skateboarding
/ˈskeɪtbɔːdɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：滑板运动。
① A sign at the entrance says that skateboarding is not allowed in the shopping centre.
= 入口处的牌子上写着购物中心里禁止玩滑板。
skateboarding = 滑板运动
② He took up skateboarding at the age of eight and now does it every day.
= 他八岁开始玩滑板，现在天天玩。
take up skateboarding = 开始玩滑板

skatepark
/ˈskeɪtpɑːk/
核心：名词，可数：滑板公园、滑板场。
① The town built a new skatepark so that the teenagers would stop skating in the streets.
= 镇上建了一座新的滑板公园，好让青少年不再在街上玩滑板。
a skatepark = 滑板公园
skatepark = 滑板场
② The skatepark is full of children on bikes and scooters at the weekend.
= 周末滑板场里满是骑自行车和踩滑板车的孩子。
the skatepark = 滑板场

skater
/ˈskeɪtə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：溜冰者、滑冰运动员；玩滑板的人。
① The skaters spun and jumped on the ice while the music played.
= 音乐响起，滑冰运动员们在冰上旋转跳跃。
the skaters = 滑冰运动员
skater = 溜冰者
② She is a figure skater and trains at the ice rink every morning at five.
= 她是花样滑冰运动员，每天早上五点在冰场训练。

/ˈskelɪtn/

a skeleton staff = 最基本的人手
④ My grandmother always said that every family has a skeleton in the cupboard.

skeuomorph
/ˈskjuːəmɔːf/

/ˌskjuːəˈmɔːfɪk/

skew

② The car skewed across the icy road and stopped in a field.

③ Just before the wedding photo, his mother told him his tie was skew-whiff and straightened it.

= 每个星期天早上，我奶奶都用一口旧铁煎锅煎鸡蛋。
a skillet = 平底煎锅
skillet = 煎锅
② Heat a little oil in a skillet and cook the onions until they are soft.
= 在煎锅里热一点油，把洋葱炒软。
heat oil in a skillet = 在煎锅里热油

skim
/skɪm/
核心：动词（skimmed, skimmed）：撇去（液体表面的浮物）：skim the fat off the soup；掠过、擦过（表面）：skim across the water；略读、浏览：skim through a book；（口语）（偷偷）抽走（钱）：skim off。
① When the soup has cooled a little, skim the fat off the top before you serve it.
= 汤稍微凉一点后，上汤之前把表面的油撇掉。
skim something off something = 从某物上撇去某物
skim = 撇去
② In the evening light, the birds skimmed across the surface of the lake.
= 傍晚的光线里，鸟儿掠过湖面。
skim across something = 掠过某处
③ I only had time to skim through the report before the meeting.
= 开会前我只来得及把报告浏览一遍。
skim through something = 浏览某物
④ The manager was skimming off money from the shop for years before anyone noticed.
= 那个经理多年来一直偷偷从店里抽钱，过了很久才有人发现。
skim off = 偷偷抽走（钱）
⑤ My doctor told me to drink skimmed milk instead of full-fat milk.
= 我的医生叫我喝脱脂牛奶，不喝全脂牛奶。
skimmed milk = 脱脂牛奶

skimp
/skɪmp/
核心：动词，不及物：（在…上）舍不得花钱、马虎、偷工减料：skimp on something。
① My grandmother always says don't skimp on the butter, or the cake will be dry.
= 我奶奶总说黄油别放得太省，不然蛋糕会干。
skimp on something = 在某物上省
skimp = 舍不得花钱
② The builders skimped on the materials, and the roof leaked after a year.
= 建筑工偷工减料，一年后屋顶就漏了。
skimp on materials = 在材料上偷工减料

skimpy
/ˈskɪmpi/
核心：形容词：（衣服）太小的、暴露的：a skimpy dress；（数量）不足的、太少的：a skimpy meal。
① She wore a skimpy dress to the party, even though it was snowing outside.
= 她穿了一条很露的裙子去派对，尽管外面在下雪。
a skimpy dress = 暴露的裙子
skimpy = 太小的
② The hotel breakfast was skimpy: one piece of toast and a small cup of coffee.
= 酒店的早餐少得可怜：一片吐司和一小杯咖啡。
skimpy = 太少的

skin
/skɪn/
核心：名词：（人、动物的）皮、皮肤（不可数或可数）：dry skin；（动物的）毛皮、皮革（可数）；（水果、蔬菜的）皮（可数或不可数）；（液体表面结的）一层皮：a skin on the milk。动词（skinned, skinned）：剥皮；擦破（皮肤）：skin your knee。习语：by the skin of your teeth 是好不容易、险些；get under somebody's skin 是惹恼某人；jump out of your skin 是吓了一大跳；save your skin 是保全自己；have a thick skin 是脸皮厚、不在乎别人批评。
① The sun was so strong that the skin on my arms turned red.
= 太阳太毒了，我手臂上的皮肤都晒红了。
skin = 皮
人的 = 皮肤
② Peel the skin off the oranges before you put them in the salad.
= 把橙子皮剥掉再放进沙拉里。
果的 = 果皮
③ The chemist told me this cream is good for dry skin and for sensitive skin.
= 药剂师告诉我，这种霜适合干性皮肤，也适合敏感皮肤。
dry skin = 干性皮肤
sensitive skin = 敏感皮肤
④ He fell off his bike and skinned his knee on the road.
= 他从自行车上摔下来，膝盖在路上擦破了皮。
skin your knee = 擦破膝盖
⑤ We ran all the way to the station and caught the last train by the skin of our teeth.
= 我们一路跑到车站，好不容易才赶上了末班火车。
by the skin of your teeth = 好不容易
⑥ When I am trying to work, his constant whistling in the office really gets under my skin.
= 我想干活的时候，他在办公室里老是吹口哨，真让我心烦。
get under somebody's skin = 惹恼某人
⑦ I nearly jumped out of my skin when the door slammed behind me.
= 门在我身后砰地关上，我差点吓得跳起来。
jump out of your skin = 吓一大跳
⑧ When the window was broken, he blamed his friend to save his own skin.
= 窗户被打破后，他为了保全自己把责任推给了朋友。
save your own skin = 保全自己
⑨ You need a thick skin to work as a politician, because people criticize you every day.
= 当政客得脸皮厚，因为天天有人批评你。
a thick skin = 脸皮厚
⑩ Everyone says she is beautiful, but her beauty is only skin-deep; she is not a kind person.
= 人人都说她漂亮，可她的美只是表面的，她不是个善良的人。
skin-deep = 肤浅的、表面的
⑪ The skin-diver swam down to look at the fish around the old wreck.
= 那位潜水员潜下去看旧沉船周围的鱼。
a skin-diver = 无装备潜水者
⑫ We went skin diving in the warm clear water near the island.
= 我们在岛附近温暖清澈的水里潜水。
skin diving = （戴面罩）潜水
⑬ The young men sat behind the shed and skinned up before the party.
= 那几个年轻人坐在棚屋后面，派对前卷了支大麻烟。
skin up = （英式俚语）卷大麻烟
词族：
skinny = 瘦得皮包骨的

/ˈskɪnkeə(r)/

skinflint
/ˈskɪnflɪnt/

skinful
/ˈskɪnfʊl/

skinhead
/ˈskɪnhed/
核心：名词，可数：（英国 20 世纪 60 年代起的）光头党、剃光头的年轻人（常与暴力、种族主义联系在一起）。
① A group of skinheads in heavy boots were shouting outside the football ground.
= 一群穿着厚靴子的光头党在足球场外大喊大叫。

skip
/skɪp/
核心：动词（skipped, skipped）：蹦跳、跳着走：skip along；（英式）跳绳：skip with a rope；不参加、略过、跳过：skip breakfast、skip a class；（话题）跳来跳去；（口语）匆匆离开：skip town。名词，可数：蹦跳；（英式）（装建筑垃圾的）大型垃圾箱。
① The little girl skipped happily down the road, holding her mother's hand.
= 小女孩拉着妈妈的手，蹦蹦跳跳地沿着路走。
skip = 蹦跳
② I got up late and skipped breakfast this morning, so I'm very hungry now.
= 我今天早上起晚了，没吃早饭，所以现在很饿。
skip breakfast = 不吃早饭
③ If the first question is too hard, skip it and come back to it later.
= 要是第一题太难，就先跳过，待会儿再回来做。
skip something = 跳过某物
④ At break time, the children skipped in the playground with a long rope.
= 课间休息时，孩子们在操场上用一根长绳跳绳。
skip = 跳绳
⑤ Buy a skipping rope; it's one of the best ways to get fit.
= 买根跳绳吧，这是健身最好的办法之一。
a skipping rope = 跳绳（用具）
⑥ Outside our house, the builders filled the skip with old bricks and wood.
= 我们家门外，建筑工人把旧砖和木头装满了垃圾箱。
a skip = 大型垃圾箱
⑦ While the waiter was in the kitchen, the man skipped off without paying for his meal.
= 趁服务员在厨房，那个人没付饭钱就溜了。
skip off = 溜走
⑧ He skipped out of the hotel early in the morning so that he didn't have to pay.
= 他一大早就溜出了酒店，好不付钱。
skip out = 偷偷溜走
⑨ Her husband skipped out on her and the children when the baby was born.
= 宝宝出生时，她丈夫抛下她和孩子们跑了。
skip out on somebody = 抛弃某人

skipper
/ˈskɪpə(r)/

skirmish
/ˈskɜːmɪʃ/

/ˈskɜːmɪʃɪŋ/

skirt

skitter

skittish
/ˈskɪtɪʃ/
核心：形容词：（马等）易受惊的；（人）紧张不安的、反复无常的；（市场）不稳的。副词 skittishly 是紧张不安地。名词 skittishness 另立条。
① The horse was skittish and jumped every time a car went past.
= 那匹马很容易受惊，每次有车经过都会跳起来。
skittish = 易受惊的
② Investors have been skittish since the bank crisis, and prices go up and down every day.
= 银行危机以来投资者一直心神不宁，股价天天起伏。
skittish investors = 心神不宁的投资者
③ The young deer moved skittishly through the trees, stopping at every sound.
= 小鹿紧张不安地在树林里走，听到一点声音就停下。
skittishly = 紧张不安地

skittishness
/ˈskɪtɪʃnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：易受惊、紧张不安；（市场的）不稳定。
① The rider was worried about the skittishness of the young horse in the crowd.
= 骑手担心那匹小马在人群中容易受惊。
the skittishness of a horse = 马的易受惊
skittishness = 紧张不安
② The skittishness of the markets made many people afraid to buy shares.
= 市场的不稳定让很多人不敢买股票。
the skittishness of the markets = 市场的不稳定

/ˈskɪtl/

skivvy
/ˈskɪvi/
核心：名词，可数（英式口语，贬义）：（干粗活的）女仆、打杂的人。动词，不及物：干粗活、打杂。
① She felt like a skivvy, cooking and cleaning for the whole family every day.
= 她每天为全家做饭打扫，觉得自己像个女佣。
a skivvy = 打杂的人
skivvy = 女仆
② He spent the summer skivvying in a hotel kitchen to save money for university.
= 他整个夏天在一家酒店厨房里打杂，攒钱上大学。
skivvy in a kitchen = 在厨房打杂

skua
/ˈskjuːə/
核心：名词，可数：贼鸥（一种大型褐色海鸟，常抢夺别的鸟的食物）。
① A skua chased the gull until it dropped the fish it was carrying.
= 一只贼鸥追着那只海鸥，直到它扔下嘴里叼着的鱼。
a skua = 贼鸥
skua = 贼鸥
② The great skuas on the island attack anyone who walks too close to their nests.
= 岛上的大贼鸥会攻击走得离它们窝太近的人。
the great skuas = 大贼鸥

skulduggery
/skʌlˈdʌɡəri/
核心：名词，不可数（幽默）：欺诈、阴谋诡计、见不得人的勾当。也拼作 skullduggery。
① The election was full of skulduggery, with votes bought and boxes stolen.
= 那场选举充满了阴谋诡计，有人买选票，有人偷票箱。
full of skulduggery = 充满阴谋诡计
skulduggery = 阴谋诡计
② The prize-winning novel is a story of political skullduggery in eighteenth-century London.
= 那部获奖小说讲的是十八世纪伦敦的政治阴谋。
political skullduggery = 政治阴谋

skulk
/skʌlk/
核心：动词，不及物：鬼鬼祟祟地躲藏、潜行（多因心怀不轨或害怕）。
① A man was skulking in the shadows near the back door of the shop.
= 一个男人鬼鬼祟祟地躲在商店后门附近的暗处。
skulk in the shadows = 躲在暗处
skulk = 鬼鬼祟祟地躲藏
② The dog skulked off into the garden after it had eaten the cake.
= 那条狗偷吃了蛋糕后，偷偷溜进了花园。
skulk off = 偷偷溜走

skull
/skʌl/
核心：名词，可数：头骨、颅骨；（口语）脑袋。
① The doctor said he had broken his skull when he fell off the ladder.
= 医生说他从梯子上摔下来时把头骨摔裂了。
skull = 头骨
break your skull = 摔裂头骨
② The museum has the skull of a dinosaur that is two metres long.
= 那座博物馆有一个两米长的恐龙头骨。
the skull of a dinosaur = 恐龙头骨
③ The pirate ship flew a black flag with a white skull and crossbones.
= 那艘海盗船挂着一面画着白色骷髅和交叉骨头的黑旗。
skull and crossbones = 骷髅交叉骨图案

skullcap
/ˈskʌlkæp/
核心：名词，可数：（犹太人、穆斯林等戴的）无檐便帽、小圆帽。
① The old man wore a small black skullcap when he went to the synagogue.
= 那位老人去犹太会堂时戴着一顶黑色小圆帽。
a skullcap = 小圆帽
skullcap = 无檐便帽
② At the end of the street, the boys in the religious school all wore white skullcaps.
= 街尽头那所宗教学校的男孩都戴着白色小圆帽。
white skullcaps = 白色小圆帽

skunk
/skʌŋk/
核心：名词：臭鼬（可数，北美的一种黑白相间、遇险时喷出恶臭液体的动物）；（不可数，俚语）一种强效大麻。
① The dog came home smelling terrible, because a skunk had sprayed it.
= 那条狗回家时臭气熏天，因为被一只臭鼬喷了。
a skunk = 臭鼬
skunk = 臭鼬
② Skunk cabbage grows in wet woods and has a very strong smell.
= 臭菘长在潮湿的树林里，气味非常浓烈。
skunk cabbage = 臭菘（一种有臭味的沼泽植物）

skunkweed
/ˈskʌŋkwiːd/
核心：名词，不可数（俚语）：一种强效大麻（同 skunk）。
① The police found a large amount of skunkweed in the back of the van.
= 警方在面包车后面发现了大量强效大麻。
skunkweed = 强效大麻
② Doctors warn that smoking skunkweed can cause serious mental health problems in young people.
= 医生警告，吸强效大麻可能给年轻人带来严重的心理健康问题。
smoking skunkweed = 吸强效大麻

skunkworks
/ˈskʌŋkwɜːks/
核心：名词，可数（复数同形）（口语）：（公司里秘密研发新产品的）秘密小组、臭鼬工厂。
① The new phone was designed in the company's skunkworks, by a team of only ten people.
= 那款新手机是在公司的秘密研发小组里由一个只有十个人的团队设计的。
the company's skunkworks = 公司的秘密研发小组
skunkworks = 秘密小组
② Many big ideas started in a skunkworks far away from the main office.
= 许多大创意都诞生于远离总部的秘密研发小组。
a skunkworks far away = 远离总部的秘密小组

sky
/skaɪ/
核心：名词，可数或不可数（复数 skies）：天、天空：in the sky、a blue sky；（复数 the skies）（某地的）天气、天空：sunny skies。习语：the sky's the limit 是前途无量、没有上限；out of a clear blue sky 是突然、出乎意料。
① There were thousands of stars in the sky, because we were far from the city lights.
= 天空中有成千上万颗星星，因为我们离城市的灯光很远。
in the sky = 在天空中
sky = 天空
② The weather forecast promises sunny skies for the rest of the week.
= 天气预报说本周剩下几天都是晴天。
sunny skies = 晴朗的天空
③ The skies over the mountains turned dark as the storm came closer.
= 暴风雨越来越近，山那边的天空暗了下来。
the skies = 天空
④ With your talent, the sky's the limit; you could become a famous singer.
= 凭你的天赋，前途无量，你能成为著名歌手。
the sky's the limit = 前途无量
⑤ Out of a clear blue sky, he told us he was leaving his job and moving to Spain.
= 他突然告诉我们他要辞职搬去西班牙。
out of a clear blue sky = 突然、出乎意料
⑥ Before the baby was born, she painted the walls of the little bedroom sky blue.
= 宝宝出生前，她把那间小卧室的墙刷成了天蓝色。
sky blue = 天蓝色
⑦ When the new railway station opened, house prices in the area went sky-high.
= 新火车站开通后，那一带的房价飞涨。
sky-high = 极高的、飞涨的
⑧ A sky marshal sat at the back of the plane, dressed like an ordinary passenger.
= 一名空中警察坐在飞机后部，打扮得跟普通乘客一样。
a sky marshal = 空中警察
⑨ Sky surfing is a dangerous sport in which you jump from a plane on a board.
= 空中冲浪是一项危险的运动，你踩着一块板从飞机上跳下。
sky surfing = 空中冲浪

skybox
/ˈskaɪbɒks/
核心：名词，可数（美式）：（体育场高处的）豪华包厢。
① The company rented a skybox at the stadium so that its best customers could watch the game.
= 那家公司在体育场租了一个豪华包厢，让最好的客户看比赛。
a skybox = 豪华包厢
skybox = 豪华包厢
② From the skybox, we had a perfect view of the whole football field.
= 从豪华包厢里，我们能把整个球场看得清清楚楚。
from the skybox = 从豪华包厢

skydiver
/ˈskaɪdaɪvə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：跳伞运动员、跳伞爱好者。
① The skydivers jumped out of the plane and opened their parachutes high above the beach.
= 跳伞运动员们从飞机上跳下，在海滩上空高处打开了降落伞。
the skydivers = 跳伞运动员
skydiver = 跳伞运动员
② My aunt is an experienced skydiver who has made more than five hundred jumps.
= 我姑姑是个经验丰富的跳伞爱好者，跳过五百多次。
an experienced skydiver = 经验丰富的跳伞爱好者

skydiving
/ˈskaɪdaɪvɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：跳伞运动、特技跳伞。
① For his sixtieth birthday, my father went skydiving for the first time.
= 我父亲六十岁生日时，第一次去跳伞。
go skydiving = 去跳伞
skydiving = 跳伞运动
② Skydiving is safer than most people think, but you need good training.
= 跳伞比大多数人想的安全，但需要好好训练。
skydiving is safer = 跳伞更安全

skyjack
/ˈskaɪdʒæk/
核心：动词，及物：劫持（飞机）（同 hijack）。
① Two men tried to skyjack the plane, but the passengers stopped them.
= 两个男人企图劫持那架飞机，但被乘客们制止了。
skyjack a plane = 劫持飞机
skyjack = 劫持
② The plane was skyjacked on its way to Paris and forced to land in the desert.
= 那架飞机在飞往巴黎途中被劫持，被迫降落在沙漠里。
be skyjacked = 被劫持

skyjacker
/ˈskaɪdʒækə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：劫机者（同 hijacker）。
① The skyjackers demanded money and a plane to fly them to another country.
= 劫机者们要钱，还要一架飞机把他们送去另一个国家。
the skyjackers = 劫机者
skyjacker = 劫机者
② The police arrested the skyjacker as soon as the plane landed at the small airport.
= 飞机一降落在那座小机场，警察就逮捕了那名劫机者。
arrest the skyjacker = 逮捕劫机者

skyjacking
/ˈskaɪdʒækɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数或不可数：劫机。
① There were several skyjackings in the 1970s, so airports started checking passengers' bags.
= 二十世纪七十年代发生了好几起劫机事件，所以机场开始检查乘客的行李。
skyjackings = 劫机事件
skyjacking = 劫机
② The film tells the true story of a skyjacking that ended without anyone being hurt.
= 那部电影讲的是一起没人受伤就结束了的真实劫机事件。
the true story of a skyjacking = 真实的劫机事件

skylark
/ˈskaɪlɑːk/
核心：名词，可数：云雀（一种边飞边唱的小鸟）。动词，不及物（旧）：嬉戏、胡闹。
① We lay in the grass and listened to a skylark singing high above the field.
= 我们躺在草地上，听一只云雀在田野上空高歌。
a skylark = 云雀
skylark = 云雀
② The boys were skylarking on the beach instead of helping with the boats.
= 那几个男孩在海滩上嬉闹，没去帮忙收拾船。
skylark on the beach = 在海滩上嬉闹

skylight
/ˈskaɪlaɪt/
核心：名词，可数：天窗。
① The attic is very bright, because it has a big skylight in the roof.
= 阁楼非常亮，因为屋顶上有一扇大天窗。
a skylight = 天窗
skylight = 天窗
② Rain was coming in through the old skylight, so we put a bucket under it.
= 雨从旧天窗漏进来，所以我们在下面放了个桶。
the old skylight = 旧天窗

skyline
/ˈskaɪlaɪn/
核心：名词，可数：（城市建筑映衬在天空下的）轮廓线、天际线。
① The famous skyline of New York looks beautiful at night with all the lights on.
= 纽约著名的天际线在夜晚灯火通明时非常美。
the skyline of a city = 城市的天际线
skyline = 天际线
② The new tower will change the skyline of the old town forever.
= 那座新塔楼将永远改变老城的天际线。
change the skyline = 改变天际线

skyrocket
/ˈskaɪrɒkɪt/
核心：动词，不及物：（价格、数量）猛涨、飞涨。
① The price of petrol and bread skyrocketed in the months after the war started.
= 战争开始后的几个月里，汽油和面包价格飞涨。
skyrocket = 猛涨
② The band's popularity skyrocketed after their song was used in a film.
= 那支乐队的歌被用进一部电影后，人气一路飙升。
popularity skyrockets = 人气飙升

skyscraper
/ˈskaɪskreɪpə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：摩天大楼。
① The city centre is full of glass skyscrapers, some of them more than a hundred floors high.
= 市中心满是玻璃摩天大楼，有些超过一百层。
glass skyscrapers = 玻璃摩天大楼
skyscraper = 摩天大楼
② Her office is on the sixtieth floor of a skyscraper, and she can see the sea from her desk.
= 她的办公室在一座摩天大楼的六十层，坐在桌前就能看到大海。
the floor of a skyscraper = 摩天大楼的某层

skywards
/ˈskaɪwədz/
核心：副词：向天空、朝上。也作 skyward（美式常用）。
① As the crowd cheered, the rocket rose skywards with a huge roar and a cloud of smoke.
= 人群欢呼声中，火箭伴着巨大的轰鸣和一团烟雾升向天空。
rise skywards = 升向天空
skywards = 向天空
② The children in the playground pointed skywards when they saw the big red balloon.
= 操场上的孩子们看到那个大红气球时，都朝天上指。
point skywards = 朝天上指

slab
/slæb/
核心：名词，可数：（石头、木头、混凝土等的）厚板、厚片：a slab of stone；（食物的）一大块：a slab of cake。
① The path through the garden is made of large slabs of grey stone.
= 花园里的小路是用大块灰色石板铺成的。
slabs of stone = 石板
slab = 厚板
② He cut himself a thick slab of chocolate cake and ate it in front of the television.
= 他给自己切了一大块巧克力蛋糕，坐在电视机前吃。
a slab of cake = 一大块蛋糕
多用于石头、混凝土、肉、蛋糕等厚而平的块状物。

slack
/slæk/
核心：形容词：（绳子等）松弛的、不紧的：a slack rope；（生意）萧条的、清淡的：business is slack；（人）马虎的、懈怠的。名词：（绳子的）松弛部分（不可数）；（复数 slacks）宽松长裤（另立条）。动词：懈怠、偷懒：slack off。形容词 slack-jawed 是（因吃惊）张大嘴的。副词 slackly 是松弛地、懈怠地。
① The rope was too slack, so the tent fell down in the wind.
= 绳子太松了，帐篷被风吹倒了。
slack = 松弛的
② In the little gift shop, business is always slack in January, after the Christmas shopping.
= 在那家小礼品店，圣诞购物季过后，一月份的生意总是很清淡。
business is slack = 生意清淡
③ The new manager said the staff had become slack and needed to work harder.
= 新经理说员工们变得懈怠了，需要更努力地工作。
become slack = 变得懈怠
④ Pull in the slack before you tie the boat to the post.
= 把船拴到桩子上之前，先把松着的绳子收紧。
the slack = 松弛的部分
⑤ The students in my class started to slack off as soon as the exams were over.
= 考试一结束，我们班的学生就开始懈怠了。
slack off = 懈怠、松劲
⑥ The children watched the magician slack-jawed as he made the rabbit disappear.
= 魔术师把兔子变没了，孩子们看得目瞪口呆。
slack-jawed = 张大嘴的、目瞪口呆的
⑦ The flag hung slackly from the pole, because there was no wind.
= 没有风，旗子松垂在旗杆上。
slackly = 松弛地

slacken
/ˈslækən/
核心：动词：（使）放松、变松：slacken the rope；（使）减缓、减弱：slacken your pace。
① He slackened the rope a little so that the dog could reach the water.
= 他把绳子放松了一点，好让狗能够着水。
slacken the rope = 放松绳子
slacken = 放松
② The rain slackened in the afternoon, so we went out for a walk.
= 下午雨势减弱了，所以我们出去散了散步。
the rain slackens = 雨势减弱
③ Demand for the new phone has slackened since the price went up.
= 涨价以后，那款新手机的需求放缓了。
demand slackens = 需求放缓

slacker
/ˈslækə(r)/
核心：名词，可数（口语，贬义）：偷懒的人、逃避工作的人。
① Her father called her boyfriend a slacker, because he slept until noon every day.
= 她父亲说她男朋友是个懒鬼，因为他每天睡到中午。
a slacker = 懒鬼
slacker = 偷懒的人
② The coach made it clear that there was no room for slackers on the team.
= 教练说得很清楚，队里容不下偷懒的人。
no room for slackers = 容不下偷懒的人

slacklining
/ˈslæklaɪnɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：走扁带（在两点间拉一条松弛的扁带并在上面行走、保持平衡的运动）。
① On sunny afternoons, the students were slacklining between two trees in the park.
= 晴朗的下午，学生们在公园里两棵树之间走扁带。
be slacklining = 在走扁带
slacklining = 走扁带
② Slacklining is good for your balance, but it takes a lot of practice.
= 走扁带对平衡感有好处，不过需要大量练习。
slacklining is good for = 走扁带有益于

slackness
/ˈslæknəs/
核心：名词，不可数：松弛；懈怠、马虎；（生意的）萧条。
① The slackness of the rope meant that the boat drifted away from the shore.
= 绳子太松，船漂离了岸边。
the slackness of the rope = 绳子的松弛
slackness = 松弛
② The official report blamed the factory accident on slackness in the safety checks.
= 那份官方报告把工厂事故归咎于安全检查上的马虎。
slackness in something = 某方面的马虎

slacks
/slæks/
核心：名词，复数（旧）：（便装）宽松长裤：a pair of slacks。
① My grandfather always wore grey slacks and a jacket, even at the weekend.
= 我祖父总穿灰色宽松长裤和夹克，周末也不例外。
grey slacks = 灰色宽松长裤
slacks = 宽松长裤
② She bought a pair of linen slacks for her holiday in Italy.
= 她为去意大利度假买了一条亚麻宽松长裤。
a pair of slacks = 一条宽松长裤

slacktivism
/ˈslæktɪvɪzəm/
核心：名词，不可数（贬义）：懒人行动主义（只在网上点赞、转发、签名，而不付出实际行动的「支持」）。
① Critics say that sharing a post about a disaster without giving any money is just slacktivism.
= 批评者说，转发一条关于灾难的帖子却一分钱不捐，只是懒人式的行动。
just slacktivism = 只是懒人式的行动
slacktivism = 懒人行动主义
② The charity in our town wants real volunteers who will help in the shop, not slacktivism.
= 我们镇上那家慈善机构要的是肯在店里帮忙的真正志愿者，而不是光在网上表态。
not slacktivism = 不是光在网上表态

slacktivist
/ˈslæktɪvɪst/
核心：名词，可数（贬义）：懒人行动主义者（只在网上表示支持的人）。
① He calls himself an activist, but he is really a slacktivist who never leaves his computer.
= 他自称社会活动家，其实是个从不离开电脑的网上表态派。
a slacktivist = 网上表态派
slacktivist = 懒人行动主义者
② Slacktivists share the petition online, but few of them go to the protest.
= 网上表态派会转发请愿书，但很少有人去参加抗议。
slacktivists = 网上表态派

slag
/slæɡ/
核心：名词，不可数：矿渣、炉渣。动词（slagged, slagged）（英式口语）：slag somebody off 是说某人坏话、大肆批评。另有一个侮辱女性的英式粗俗用法，不要使用。
① The old mine left huge piles of slag on the hills above the village.
= 那座旧矿在村子上方的山坡上留下了大堆矿渣。
piles of slag = 大堆矿渣
slag = 矿渣
② Children used to slide down the slag heap behind the old coal mine.
= 孩子们以前常从旧煤矿后面的矿渣堆上滑下来。
a slag heap = 矿渣堆
③ He's always slagging off his old school, but he did very well there.
= 他老是说他母校的坏话，可他在那儿学得很好。
slag off = 说…的坏话

slagging
/ˈslæɡɪŋ/
核心：名词，单数（英式、爱尔兰口语）：批评、贬损、取笑：give somebody a slagging。
① The team got a slagging from the newspapers after losing six goals to nil.
= 球队以零比六输球后，被报纸骂得狗血淋头。
get a slagging = 遭到痛批
slagging = 批评
② At the pub, his friends gave him a good-natured slagging about his new haircut.
= 在酒吧里，朋友们善意地取笑了他的新发型。
a good-natured slagging = 善意的取笑

slake
/sleɪk/
核心：动词，及物（书面）：解（渴）、满足（欲望）：slake your thirst；（化学）使（石灰）消解、熟化。
① After the long walk, we stopped at a stream to slake our thirst.
= 走了很久之后，我们在一条小溪边停下来解渴。
slake your thirst = 解渴
slake = 解（渴）
② Builders mixed slaked lime with sand and water to make the mortar.
= 建筑工人把熟石灰和沙子、水混在一起做砂浆。
slaked lime = 熟石灰

slalom
/ˈslɑːləm/
核心：名词，可数：（滑雪、皮划艇的）障碍滑行赛、回转赛。
① She won the gold medal in the slalom at the Winter Olympics.
= 她在冬奥会回转赛中获得了金牌。
the slalom = 回转赛
slalom = 障碍滑行赛
② The canoe slalom is held on a fast river with gates hanging over the water.
= 皮划艇激流回旋赛在一条湍急的河上举行，水面上悬着一道道门。
the canoe slalom = 皮划艇激流回旋赛

slam
/slæm/
核心：动词（slammed, slammed）：砰地关上：slam the door；猛地放下、猛摔：slam something down；猛撞：slam into something；（口语）猛烈抨击。名词，可数：砰的一声；（网球等的）大满贯（grand slam）。
① She ran out of the room and slammed the door behind her.
= 她跑出房间，砰地关上了身后的门。
slam the door = 砰地关门
slam = 砰地关上
② When he heard the news, he slammed his fist on the table and shouted at us.
= 他听到那个消息，一拳砸在桌上，冲我们大吼。
slam something on the table = 把某物重重砸在桌上
③ On the icy hill, the car skidded on the ice and slammed into a wall.
= 在结冰的山坡上，那辆车打滑，猛地撞上了一堵墙。
slam into something = 猛撞某物
④ The new film was slammed by the critics as boring and too long.
= 那部新电影被评论家痛批，说它沉闷又冗长。
be slammed by somebody = 遭某人痛批

slammer
/ˈslæmə(r)/
核心：名词，单数（俚语）：监狱（the slammer）。
① My neighbour's son spent five years in the slammer for robbing a bank.
= 我邻居的儿子因抢银行坐了五年牢。
in the slammer = 在坐牢
slammer = 监狱
② His father warned him that if he kept driving like that, he would end up in the slammer.
= 他父亲警告他，要是他再那样开车，早晚得进监狱。
end up in the slammer = 最后进监狱

slander
/ˈslɑːndə(r)/
核心：名词：（口头的）诽谤、诋毁（不可数或可数）。动词，及物：诽谤、诋毁。比较 libel（书面诽谤）。
① The singer sued the newspaper for slander after it said he had stolen the song.
= 那家报纸说那位歌手抄袭了那首歌，歌手以诽谤罪起诉了它。
sue somebody for slander = 以诽谤罪起诉某人
slander = 诽谤
② She accused her neighbour of slandering her in front of the whole village.
= 她指责邻居当着全村人的面诋毁她。
slander somebody = 诽谤某人

slanderous
/ˈslɑːndərəs/
核心：形容词：诽谤的、诋毁的：slanderous remarks。
① The politician said the stories about him were slanderous and completely untrue.
= 那位政客说关于他的那些传言是诽谤，完全不属实。
slanderous = 诽谤的
② The newspaper had to apologize for printing slanderous comments about the teacher.
= 那家报纸因刊登诋毁那位老师的言论而不得不道歉。
slanderous comments = 诋毁的言论

slang
/slæŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：俚语。动词，及物（英式口语）：辱骂、骂。名词 slanging match 是（英式）对骂。
① Teenagers today use a lot of slang that their parents simply don't understand.
= 如今的青少年用很多父母根本听不懂的俚语。
slang = 俚语
② The detective in the old film uses so much London slang that I needed the subtitles.
= 那部老电影里的侦探满口伦敦俚语，我只好看字幕。
London slang = 伦敦俚语
③ The two drivers got out of their cars and had a slanging match in the middle of the road.
= 两个司机下了车，在马路中间对骂起来。
a slanging match = 对骂

slangy
/ˈslæŋi/
核心：形容词（口语）：俚语多的、满口俚语的。
① His slangy way of talking made it hard for the old lady to understand him.
= 他说话满嘴俚语，让那位老太太很难听懂。
slangy = 俚语多的
a slangy way of talking = 满口俚语的说话方式
② The new film has slangy dialogue that sounds like real teenagers talking at school.
= 那部新电影的对白俚语很多，听上去就像真正的中学生在学校里说话。
slangy dialogue = 俚语多的对白

slant
/slɑːnt/
核心：动词：（使）倾斜、歪斜；（使）（报道等）有倾向性、有偏向。名词，单数：倾斜、斜面：at a slant；倾向、角度、观点：a new slant on something。
① The old house slants to one side, and the doors don't close properly.
= 那座老房子向一边倾斜，门都关不严。
slant = 倾斜
② The old picture hung at a slant on the wall, so I straightened it before the guests arrived.
= 那幅旧画在墙上挂歪了，所以客人来之前我把它扶正了。
at a slant = 斜着
③ Before the election, the newspaper slanted the story to make the government look bad.
= 选举前，那家报纸带着倾向性报道那件事，让政府显得很糟。
slant a story = 带倾向性地报道
④ The new film gives a fresh slant on an old fairy tale.
= 那部新电影从一个新颖的角度讲述了一个古老的童话。
a fresh slant on something = 对某事的新角度

slanted
/ˈslɑːntɪd/
核心：形容词：倾斜的、斜的；有偏向的、有倾向性的：a slanted report。
① The little cottage by the river has a slanted roof covered with old red tiles.
= 河边那座小屋有一个铺着旧红瓦的斜屋顶。
a slanted roof = 斜屋顶
slanted = 倾斜的
② Critics said the television programme gave a slanted view of the miners' strike.
= 批评者说那档电视节目对矿工罢工的报道有偏向。
a slanted view = 有偏向的看法

slanting
/ˈslɑːntɪŋ/
核心：形容词：倾斜的、斜的：slanting rain、slanting handwriting。
① My grandmother has neat slanting handwriting that is very easy to read.
= 我奶奶的字迹整齐、向右斜，很好认。
slanting handwriting = 斜体字迹
slanting = 倾斜的
② The slanting rain soaked us before we could reach the bus shelter.
= 斜着下的雨还没等我们跑到公交候车亭就把我们淋透了。
slanting rain = 斜雨

slap
/slæp/
核心：动词（slapped, slapped）：（用手掌）打、掴：slap somebody's face；啪地放下、随手一放：slap something on the table；（口语）随意涂抹：slap on paint。名词，可数：一巴掌、一掌。副词（口语）：正好、直接地：slap in the middle。习语：a slap in the face 是一记耳光、侮辱；a slap on the wrist 是轻微的惩罚。
① When he laughed at her in front of everyone, she was so angry that she slapped him across the face.
= 他当着大家的面嘲笑她，她气坏了，打了他一记耳光。
slap somebody across the face = 打某人耳光
slap = 掴
② He slapped the money on the counter and walked out of the shop.
= 他把钱啪地往柜台上一放，走出了商店。
slap something on the counter = 把某物啪地放在柜台上
③ Losing the job to a younger man was a real slap in the face for him.
= 那份工作被一个年轻人抢走，对他来说真是当头一棒。
a slap in the face = 一记耳光、侮辱
④ The company only got a slap on the wrist for polluting the river.
= 那家公司污染了河流，却只受到了轻微惩罚。
a slap on the wrist = 轻微的惩罚
⑤ The new hotel was built slap in the middle of the old town.
= 那家新酒店就建在老城区正中央。
slap in the middle = 正好在中间
⑥ He was arrested for slapping his wife about, and the neighbours called the police.
= 他因殴打妻子被捕，是邻居们报的警。
slap about = 殴打（常拆开用：slap somebody about）
⑦ At his old school, the bigger boys used to slap him around in the playground.
= 在他以前的学校，那些大男孩常在操场上欺负他、扇他。
slap around = 扇打欺负（常拆开用：slap somebody around）
⑧ When she suggested a new idea, the manager slapped her down in front of everyone.
= 她提出一个新想法时，经理当着所有人的面粗暴地驳回了她。
slap down = 粗暴地驳斥
⑨ To pay for the new hospitals, the government has slapped a new tax on cigarettes.
= 为了支付新医院的费用，政府给香烟加征了一项新税。
slap something on something = 对某物强加（税、罚款）
⑩ Our boss is so slap-happy that nothing is ever done on time.
= 我们老板马马虎虎，什么事都不能按时做完。
slap-happy = 马虎的、漫不经心的
⑪ After the wedding, we all went for a slap-up meal at the best hotel in town.
= 婚礼后，我们全都去镇上最好的酒店吃了一顿大餐。
a slap-up meal = （英式口语）丰盛的大餐

slapdash
/ˈslæpdæʃ/
核心：形容词：马虎的、草率的、粗心的：a slapdash job。
① The builders did a slapdash job, and the roof started leaking after a month.
= 建筑工干活马虎，屋顶一个月后就开始漏水。
a slapdash job = 马虎的活
slapdash = 马虎的
② His homework is always slapdash, full of spelling mistakes and crossed-out words.
= 他的作业总是很潦草，满是拼写错误和划掉的字。
slapdash homework = 潦草的作业

slaphead
/ˈslæphed/
核心：名词，可数（英式俚语，贬义）：秃子、光头佬（带嘲弄意味，不礼貌）。
① The boys at the back of the bus called the bald driver a slaphead, and he stopped the bus.
= 公交车后排的男孩们管那位秃头司机叫秃子，司机把车停了下来。
a slaphead = 秃子
slaphead = 光头佬
② My uncle laughs when his friends call him a slaphead, because he has been bald since he was twenty.
= 朋友们叫我叔叔秃子时他只是笑笑，因为他二十岁就秃了。
call somebody a slaphead = 叫某人秃子
带嘲弄意味，只在熟人间开玩笑时用。

slapstick
/ˈslæpstɪk/
核心：名词，不可数：闹剧、打闹喜剧（靠摔跤、扔蛋糕等动作逗笑的喜剧）。
① The children laughed at the slapstick, especially when the clown fell into the bath.
= 孩子们看闹剧笑得前仰后合，特别是小丑掉进浴缸那一段。
slapstick = 闹剧
② The film is full of slapstick comedy, with people slipping on banana skins.
= 那部电影满是打闹喜剧的桥段，有人踩到香蕉皮滑倒。
slapstick comedy = 打闹喜剧

slash
/slæʃ/
核心：动词：（用刀等）砍、割：slash somebody's tyres；大幅削减（价格、开支）：slash prices。名词，可数：砍、长而深的伤口；（印刷中的）斜线号（/）。
① While I was asleep, someone slashed the tyres of my car in the street outside.
= 我睡着时，有人把我停在外面街上的车胎划破了。
slash the tyres = 划破轮胎
slash = 割
② The shop has slashed its prices by half for the winter sale.
= 那家店冬季大减价时把价格砍掉了一半。
slash prices = 大幅降价
③ He had a deep slash across his arm from the broken glass.
= 他的手臂被碎玻璃划了一道深深的口子。
a slash = 长而深的伤口
④ Write the date with a slash between the day and the month, like this: 5/6.
= 写日期时在日和月之间加一条斜线，像这样：5/6。
a slash = 斜线号
⑤ Over the last fifty years, slash-and-burn farming has destroyed huge areas of the rainforest.
= 过去五十年里，刀耕火种的农业已经毁掉了大片雨林。
slash-and-burn = 刀耕火种的

slasher
/ˈslæʃə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（用刀伤人的）凶手；（口语）（恐怖片中的）砍杀片：a slasher film。
① My brother loves slasher films, but I can't watch them because of all the blood.
= 我哥哥喜欢砍杀片，可我看不了，血太多了。
a slasher film = 砍杀片
slasher = 砍杀片
② The police warned people to stay inside until they caught the slasher.
= 警方提醒大家待在家里，直到抓住那个持刀伤人的凶手。
the slasher = 持刀伤人的凶手

slat
/slæt/
核心：名词，可数：（木、金属、塑料的）板条、狭板。
① One of the wooden slats in the bed broke when the children jumped on it.
= 孩子们在床上跳时，床的一根木板条断了。
a wooden slat = 木板条
slat = 板条
② Light came through the slats of the blinds and made stripes on the floor.
= 光从百叶窗的板条间透进来，在地板上投下一道道条纹。
the slats of the blinds = 百叶窗的板条

slate
/sleɪt/
核心：名词：板岩、石板（不可数）；（屋顶的）石板瓦（可数）；（旧时学生用的）写字石板（可数）；（美式）候选人名单。动词，及物：（英式口语）严厉批评：be slated by somebody；（美式）预定、计划：be slated for something。习语：a clean slate 是清白的记录、新的开始；wipe the slate clean 是一笔勾销、重新开始。
① The old houses in the village have roofs made of grey slate.
= 村里的老房子都是灰色石板瓦屋顶。
grey slate = 灰色石板
slate = 板岩
② Several slates blew off the roof of the old church in the storm last night.
= 昨晚的暴风雨中，老教堂屋顶上有好几片石板瓦被刮了下来。
slates = 石板瓦
③ Her new film was slated by the critics, but people still went to see it.
= 她的新电影被评论家狠批了一通，可大家还是去看了。
be slated by somebody = 被某人狠批
④ After years of delays, the new bridge is slated for completion next spring.
= 拖了好几年之后，那座新桥预定明年春天完工。
be slated for something = 预定于某时
⑤ He left prison and wanted to start again with a clean slate.
= 他出狱后想重新做人。
a clean slate = 清白的记录、新的开始
⑥ The two brothers agreed to wipe the slate clean and forget their old argument.
= 兄弟俩同意一笔勾销，忘掉过去的争吵。
wipe the slate clean = 一笔勾销
⑦ On the cold winter morning, she wore a slate-grey coat and black boots.
= 那个寒冷的冬日早晨，她穿着一件石板灰色的外套和一双黑靴子。
slate-grey = 石板灰色的

slated
/ˈsleɪtɪd/
核心：形容词：铺石板瓦的；（美式）预定的、计划好的：be slated to do something。
① The church has a slated roof that is more than two hundred years old.
= 那座教堂有一个两百多年历史的石板瓦屋顶。
a slated roof = 石板瓦屋顶
slated = 铺石板瓦的
② After twenty years at the firm, she is slated to become the next head of the company.
= 在公司干了二十年后，她被定为公司的下一任总裁。
be slated to do something = 被定为做某事

slather
/ˈslæðə(r)/
核心：动词，及物（口语）：厚厚地涂、大量地抹：slather something on / with something。
① He slathered butter on his toast and then added a thick layer of jam.
= 他在吐司上厚厚地抹了黄油，又加了一层厚果酱。
slather something on something = 在某物上厚厚地抹某物
slather = 厚厚地涂
② Before going to the beach, the children were slathered with sun cream.
= 去海滩前，孩子们被厚厚地涂了一层防晒霜。
be slathered with something = 被厚厚地涂上某物

slatted
/ˈslætɪd/
核心：形容词：由板条构成的、有板条的：a slatted bench。
① We sat on a slatted wooden bench in the park and fed the ducks.
= 我们坐在公园里一张木条长凳上喂鸭子。
a slatted bench = 木条长凳
slatted = 有板条的
② The slatted doors of the cupboard let the air in, so the clothes stay dry.
= 柜子的百叶门透气，所以衣服能保持干燥。
slatted doors = 百叶门

slattern
/ˈslætən/
核心：名词，可数（旧，贬义）：邋遢女人、懒婆娘。
① In the old novel, the farmer's wife is described as a lazy slattern who never cleans the house.
= 在那部老小说里，农夫的妻子被描写成一个从不打扫屋子的懒婆娘。
a lazy slattern = 懒婆娘
slattern = 邋遢女人
② The cruel old landlord called his tired cook a slattern because her apron was dirty.
= 那个刻薄的老房东因为厨娘的围裙脏了，就骂她是邋遢女人。
call somebody a slattern = 骂某人是邋遢女人
带性别偏见的旧词，现代少用。

slatternly
/ˈslætənli/
核心：形容词（旧，贬义）：（女人）邋遢的、不整洁的。
① The house was dirty and slatternly, with clothes lying on every chair.
= 那座房子又脏又乱，每把椅子上都扔着衣服。
slatternly = 邋遢的
② The writer describes a slatternly woman who sits all day in her dressing gown.
= 那位作家描写了一个整天穿着睡袍坐着的邋遢女人。
a slatternly woman = 邋遢的女人

slaty
/ˈsleɪti/
核心：形容词：（颜色）石板色的、蓝灰色的；含板岩的。
① From the cliff, the sea was a cold slaty grey under the winter sky.
= 从悬崖上望去，冬日的天空下，大海是冰冷的蓝灰色。
slaty grey = 石板灰
slaty = 石板色的
② The farmer complained that the slaty soil on the hill was very hard to dig.
= 农场主抱怨说那种含板岩的土很难挖。
slaty soil = 含板岩的土

slaughter
/ˈslɔːtə(r)/
核心：名词，不可数：（为食用的）屠宰；屠杀、大屠杀；（口语）（比赛中的）惨败。动词，及物：屠宰；屠杀；（口语）大胜、彻底击败。
① At the end of the summer, the lambs are taken to the market for slaughter.
= 夏末，那些小羊被送到市场上宰杀。
for slaughter = 用来屠宰
slaughter = 屠宰
② The slaughter of civilians in the war shocked the whole world and led to new laws.
= 战争中对平民的屠杀震惊了全世界，并促成了新的法律。
the slaughter of somebody = 对某人的屠杀
③ To our great shame, our team was slaughtered eight goals to nil in the final.
= 说来丢人，我们队在决赛中以零比八惨败。
be slaughtered = 惨败

slaughterhouse
/ˈslɔːtəhaʊs/
核心：名词，可数：屠宰场。
① The farmer sold his cattle to the slaughterhouse in the next town.
= 那个农场主把牛卖给了邻镇的屠宰场。
the slaughterhouse = 屠宰场
slaughterhouse = 屠宰场
② Down by the river, the old slaughterhouse has been turned into an art gallery.
= 河边那座旧屠宰场已经改成了一家美术馆。
the old slaughterhouse = 旧屠宰场

Slav
/slɑːv/
核心：名词，可数：斯拉夫人（东欧、中欧讲斯拉夫语的民族）。
① My grandmother was a Slav from a small village in the mountains of southern Poland.
= 我奶奶是斯拉夫人，来自波兰南部山区的一个小村子。
a Slav = 斯拉夫人
Slav = 斯拉夫人
② The history book explains how the early Slavs moved into Eastern Europe.
= 那本历史书讲述了早期斯拉夫人是怎样迁入东欧的。
the early Slavs = 早期斯拉夫人

slave
/sleɪv/
核心：名词，可数：奴隶：a slave trade；（比喻）（受某事物支配的）奴隶：a slave to fashion。动词，不及物：苦干、拼命干活：slave away。
① Millions of Africans were taken across the ocean and sold as slaves.
= 数以百万计的非洲人被运过大洋卖作奴隶。
slaves = 奴隶
slave = 奴隶
② She is a slave to fashion and buys new clothes every week.
= 她是时尚的奴隶，每周都买新衣服。
a slave to something = 受某事支配的人
③ My mother slaved away in the kitchen all day to make the Christmas dinner.
= 我妈妈在厨房里忙了一整天做圣诞晚餐。
slave away = 苦干
④ Historians have shown that the factory used slave labour from the camps during the war.
= 历史学家已证明，那家工厂在战争期间使用了集中营的奴工。
slave labour = 奴隶劳动、奴工
⑤ The new boss is a real slave-driver who makes everyone work until ten at night.
= 新老板是个真正的工头，让大家干到晚上十点。
a slave-driver = （口语）压榨下属的人
⑥ Before the American Civil War, Mississippi was a slave state with huge cotton farms.
= 美国内战之前，密西西比是一个有大片棉花种植园的蓄奴州。
a slave state = 蓄奴州
⑦ The city grew rich from the slave trade in the eighteenth century.
= 那座城市在十八世纪靠奴隶贸易发了财。
the slave trade = 奴隶贸易

slaver
/ˈslævə(r)/
核心：动词，不及物：流口水；（贬义）垂涎、过分热切：slaver over something。名词（另读 /ˈsleɪvə(r)/，可数）：奴隶贩子；贩奴船。
① The hungry dog slavered at the smell of the meat on the barbecue.
= 那条饿狗闻到烤架上的肉香，直流口水。
slaver = 流口水
② The old slaver sailed from Africa to the Caribbean with hundreds of people locked below deck.
= 那艘旧贩奴船从非洲驶往加勒比，甲板下锁着几百人。
a slaver = 贩奴船

slavery
/ˈsleɪvəri/
核心：名词，不可数：奴隶制度；奴役、奴隶身份：sold into slavery。
① After many years of campaigning, slavery was ended in the British Empire in 1833.
= 经过多年的抗争，大英帝国在 1833 年废除了奴隶制。
slavery = 奴隶制度
② The children were sold into slavery and forced to work in the mines.
= 那些孩子被卖为奴，被迫在矿里干活。
be sold into slavery = 被卖为奴

Slavic
/ˈslɑːvɪk/
核心：形容词：斯拉夫人的、斯拉夫语的：Slavic languages。
① Our teacher explained that Russian, Polish and Czech are all Slavic languages.
= 我们老师解释说，俄语、波兰语和捷克语都是斯拉夫语言。
Slavic languages = 斯拉夫语言
Slavic = 斯拉夫的
② The small museum in the mountains has a collection of old Slavic folk art.
= 山里那座小博物馆收藏了一批古老的斯拉夫民间艺术品。
Slavic folk art = 斯拉夫民间艺术

slavish
/ˈsleɪvɪʃ/
核心：形容词（贬义）：奴性的、盲从的；（复制、模仿）毫无创意的、生搬硬套的：a slavish copy。副词 slavishly 是盲从地、生搬硬套地。
① The new building is a slavish copy of an old castle, with no ideas of its own.
= 那座新建筑是对一座古堡的生搬硬套，毫无自己的创意。
a slavish copy = 生搬硬套的复制
slavish = 盲从的
② He slavishly follows every instruction his boss gives him, even the silly ones.
= 老板的每个指示他都盲目照办，连那些愚蠢的也一样。
slavishly = 盲从地

Slavonic
/sləˈvɒnɪk/
核心：形容词：斯拉夫的、斯拉夫语的（同 Slavic，英式常用）。名词，不可数：斯拉夫语（族）。
① She studied Slavonic languages at university and now works as a translator in Prague.
= 她在大学学斯拉夫语言，现在在布拉格当翻译。
Slavonic languages = 斯拉夫语言
Slavonic = 斯拉夫的
② The monks showed us old church books that were written in Old Church Slavonic.
= 修士们给我们看了用古教会斯拉夫语写的古老教会典籍。
Old Church Slavonic = 古教会斯拉夫语

slaw
/slɔː/
核心：名词，不可数（尤美式）：凉拌卷心菜丝（coleslaw 的简称）。
① The sandwich came with a small pot of slaw and some pickles on the side.
= 那份三明治配了一小罐凉拌卷心菜丝和一些腌黄瓜。
a pot of slaw = 一罐凉拌卷心菜丝
slaw = 凉拌卷心菜丝
② She makes her own slaw with red cabbage, carrots and a little lemon juice.
= 她用紫甘蓝、胡萝卜和一点柠檬汁自己做凉拌卷心菜丝。
make your own slaw = 自己做凉拌卷心菜丝

slay
/sleɪ/
核心：动词，及物（slew, slain）：（书面、旧）杀死、杀害（新闻标题中常用）；（口语）使笑得要死、使倾倒。
① In the old story, the young knight rides out to slay the dragon and save the princess.
= 在那个古老的故事里，年轻的骑士骑马出发去杀死恶龙、救出公主。
slay the dragon = 杀死恶龙
slay = 杀死
过去式是 slew，过去分词是 slain。
② The newspaper reported that three young soldiers were slain in the battle on the hill.
= 报纸报道说，三名年轻士兵在山上的战斗中阵亡。
be slain = 被杀
③ At the comedy club, her jokes slayed the audience, and everyone was crying with laughter.
= 在喜剧俱乐部，她的笑话让观众笑得要死，大家都笑出了眼泪。
slay the audience = 让观众笑翻
「杀死」义多见于新闻、故事，日常说 kill。

slaying
/ˈsleɪɪŋ/
核心：名词，可数（尤美式，新闻用语）：杀害、凶杀。
① The police are investigating the slaying of a shopkeeper in the city centre last night.
= 警方正在调查昨晚市中心一名店主遇害的案件。
the slaying of somebody = 某人遇害
slaying = 凶杀
② The police chief said there have been three slayings in the neighbourhood this year.
= 警察局长说，今年这一带发生了三起凶杀案。
three slayings = 三起凶杀案

sleaze
/sliːz/
核心：名词，不可数：（尤指政治上的）丑闻、腐败、肮脏勾当；（口语，可数）卑鄙的人。
① The government lost the election after years of sleaze and broken promises.
= 经过多年的丑闻和食言之后，政府输掉了选举。
years of sleaze = 多年的丑闻
sleaze = 丑闻
② The newspaper is full of sleaze about rich people and their secret lives.
= 那份报纸满是关于富人和他们秘密生活的龌龊事。
full of sleaze = 满是龌龊事

sleaziness
/ˈsliːzinəs/

① The sleaziness of the bar made her want to leave as soon as she walked in.
= 那家酒吧又脏又低俗，她一进去就想走。
the sleaziness of something = 某处的低俗
sleaziness = 低俗
② By the time of the election, voters were tired of the sleaziness of the old politicians.
= 到选举时，选民们已经受够了那些老政客的卑鄙行径。
the sleaziness of politicians = 政客的卑鄙

sleazy
/ˈsliːzi/
核心：形容词（口语）：（地方）肮脏低俗的、下流的：a sleazy bar；（人）不道德的、卑鄙的。
① They stayed in a sleazy hotel near the station because it was the only one with free rooms.
= 他们住进了车站附近一家又脏又低俗的旅馆，因为只有那里还有空房。
a sleazy hotel = 低俗的旅馆
sleazy = 肮脏低俗的
② The film is about a sleazy businessman who cheats old people out of their money.
= 那部电影讲的是一个骗老人钱财的卑鄙商人。
a sleazy businessman = 卑鄙的商人

sleb
/sleb/
核心：名词，可数（英式口语）：名人、明星（celebrity 的俚语缩略）。
① The magazine is full of photos of slebs at parties and on holiday.
= 那本杂志满是名人在派对和度假时的照片。
slebs = 名人
sleb = 明星
② A few slebs turned up at the opening of the new restaurant.
= 几个名人出席了那家新餐馆的开业仪式。
a few slebs = 几个名人

sled
/sled/
核心：名词，可数（美式）：雪橇（英式说 sledge）。动词（sledded, sledded）：坐雪橇滑行。
① The children pulled their sled to the top of the hill and rode it down again.
= 孩子们把雪橇拉到山顶，又坐着滑了下来。
a sled = 雪橇
sled = 雪橇
② After the big snowfall, we sledded down the snowy hill behind the school all afternoon.
= 那场大雪过后，我们整个下午都在学校后面的雪坡上滑雪橇。
sled down something = 坐雪橇滑下某处

sledding
/ˈsledɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数（美式）：坐雪橇滑雪、滑雪橇；（比喻）进展：hard sledding（困难的进展）。
① When it snows in Central Park, the hills are full of families out sledding.
= 中央公园一下雪，山坡上满是出来滑雪橇的一家人。

sledding = 滑雪橇
② Getting the new law passed will be hard sledding, because many people are against it.
= 让那项新法通过会很艰难，因为很多人反对。
hard sledding = 进展艰难

sledge
/sledʒ/
核心：名词，可数（英式）：雪橇（美式说 sled）。动词：坐雪橇滑行；用雪橇运送。
① We took the old wooden sledge out of the shed when the first snow fell.
= 下第一场雪时，我们把那架旧木雪橇从棚子里拿了出来。
a wooden sledge = 木雪橇
sledge = 雪橇
② On the first snowy morning, the children sledged down the hill, shouting with excitement.
= 下雪的第一个早晨，孩子们兴奋地大喊着，坐雪橇滑下了山坡。
sledge down something = 坐雪橇滑下某处

sledgehammer
/ˈsledʒhæmə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：大锤。习语：take a sledgehammer to crack a nut 是杀鸡用牛刀、小题大做。
① The workers used a heavy sledgehammer to knock down the old garden wall.
= 工人们用一把沉重的大锤砸倒了那堵旧花园墙。
a sledgehammer = 大锤
sledgehammer = 大锤
② Sending ten police cars for one small thief was taking a sledgehammer to crack a nut.
= 为了一个小偷出动十辆警车，真是杀鸡用牛刀。
take a sledgehammer to crack a nut = 杀鸡用牛刀

sledging
/ˈsledʒɪŋ/
核心：名词，不可数：（英式）滑雪橇；（板球）对击球手的言语挑衅。
① When we were children, we went sledging on the hill near our house every winter.
= 我们小时候，每年冬天都去家附近的山上滑雪橇。
go sledging = 去滑雪橇
sledging = 滑雪橇
② The umpire warned the bowler about sledging after he insulted the batsman.
= 投球手辱骂击球手后，裁判警告他不许言语挑衅。
sledging = （板球）言语挑衅

sleek
/sliːk/
核心：形容词：（毛发）光滑发亮的：sleek hair；（汽车、外形）线条流畅的、造型优雅的：a sleek car；（人）衣着光鲜的、油滑的。副词 sleekly 是光滑地、流畅地。名词 sleekness 另立条。
① Our neighbour's cat had sleek black fur that shone in the afternoon sunlight.
= 邻居家的猫一身光滑的黑毛，在午后的阳光下闪闪发亮。
sleek fur = 光滑的毛
sleek = 光滑发亮的
② He drove a sleek silver sports car that everyone in the street admired.
= 他开着一辆线条流畅的银色跑车，街上人人都羡慕。
a sleek car = 线条流畅的车
③ Her hair was sleekly brushed back and tied with a black ribbon.
= 她的头发梳得光溜溜地往后，用一根黑丝带扎着。
sleekly = 光滑地

sleekness
/ˈsliːknəs/
核心：名词，不可数：光滑、光亮；（外形的）流畅、优雅。
① The sleekness of the horse's coat showed that it was well looked after.
= 那匹马的毛光滑发亮，看得出被照料得很好。
the sleekness of something = 某物的光滑
sleekness = 光滑
② Designers praised the sleekness of the new train, with its long pointed nose.
= 设计师们称赞那列新火车外形流畅，车头又长又尖。
the sleekness of the train = 火车外形的流畅

sleep
/sliːp/
核心：动词（slept, slept）：睡、睡觉：sleep well、sleep late；（房间、汽车等）可供…睡：The tent sleeps four。名词：睡眠（不可数）：get some sleep、go to sleep；（单数）一觉：have a good sleep；（不可数）（眼角的）眼屎。习语：lose sleep over something 是为某事担心得睡不着；put an animal to sleep 是让动物安乐死；sleep like a log 是睡得很沉；sleep on it 是考虑一晚再决定。
① I always sleep badly the night before an exam, because I am worried about it.
= 考试前一晚我总睡不好，因为心里担心。
sleep = 睡觉
② You look very tired after the night shift; you should go home and get some sleep.
= 上完夜班你看上去很累，应该回家睡一会儿。
get some sleep = 睡一会儿
sleep = 睡觉这件事
近义对照：
go to bed = 上床（不一定睡着）
③ When I got home, I slept for twelve hours after the long flight from Australia.
= 从澳大利亚长途飞回来，到家后我睡了十二个小时。
slept = 睡（过去式）
④ The tent sleeps four people, so there is room for all of us.
= 这顶帐篷能睡四个人，所以我们都睡得下。
sleep four people = 可供四人睡
⑤ Don't lose sleep over the exam tomorrow; you have worked very hard all year.
= 别为明天的考试愁得睡不着，你一整年都很努力。
lose sleep over something = 为某事睡不着
⑥ Our old dog was very ill, so the vet put him to sleep.
= 我们的老狗病得很重，所以兽医让它安乐死了。
put an animal to sleep = 让动物安乐死
⑦ After the long walk in the mountains, I slept like a log until nine o'clock.
= 在山里走了很远的路之后，我一觉睡到九点，睡得很沉。
sleep like a log = 睡得很沉
⑧ You don't have to decide now; sleep on it and tell me tomorrow.
= 你不必现在就决定，考虑一晚，明天告诉我。
sleep on it = 考虑一晚再决定
⑨ It's Saturday and there's no school, so I'm going to sleep in until ten.
= 今天星期六不上学，我要睡懒觉睡到十点。
sleep in = 睡懒觉
⑩ He went to bed early to sleep off the effects of the long journey.
= 他早早上床，想通过睡觉来消除长途旅行的疲劳。
sleep off = 用睡觉消除（疲劳、醉意等）
⑪ My friends came to sleep over after the party, because it was too late to go home.
= 派对后朋友们留下来过夜，因为太晚了回不了家。
sleep over = 在别人家过夜
⑫ The newspaper said that the singer sleeps around and has a new girlfriend every month.
= 那份报纸说那位歌手到处乱搞，每个月都换女朋友。
sleep around = （口语，贬义）乱搞
⑬ They had been sleeping together for months before they told their friends.
= 他们已经同居了好几个月才告诉朋友们。
sleep together = 发生关系、同居
⑭ He found out that his wife was sleeping with his best friend.
= 他发现妻子跟他最好的朋友有染。
sleep with somebody = 和某人发生关系
⑮ The driving teacher told me to slow down, because there was a sleeping policeman outside the school.
= 驾驶教练叫我开慢点，因为学校门口有减速带。
a sleeping policeman = （英式）减速带

sleeper
/ˈsliːpə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（形容词＋）睡觉…的人：a light / heavy sleeper；卧铺列车、卧铺车厢；（英式）（铁路的）枕木；（口语）意外走红的电影、书等；（间谍）潜伏者。
① I'm a very light sleeper, so the smallest noise in the street wakes me up.
= 我睡觉很轻，街上一点点声音就会把我吵醒。
a light sleeper = 睡觉轻的人
sleeper = 睡觉的人
② My brother is a heavy sleeper and doesn't even hear the alarm clock.
= 我弟弟睡得很沉，连闹钟都听不见。
a heavy sleeper = 睡得沉的人
③ We took the sleeper from London to Scotland and woke up in the mountains.
= 我们坐卧铺列车从伦敦去苏格兰，醒来时已在山里了。
the sleeper = 卧铺列车
④ The film was a sleeper that nobody expected to become so popular.
= 那部电影出人意料地走红了，谁也没想到它会这么受欢迎。
a sleeper = 意外走红的作品
⑤ The old railway sleepers were used to make steps in the garden.
= 那些旧铁路枕木被拿来在花园里做台阶。
railway sleepers = 铁路枕木

sleepiness
/ˈsliːpinəs/
核心：名词，不可数：困倦、瞌睡。
① The medicine can cause sleepiness, so don't drive after you take it.
= 这种药会让人犯困，所以服药后别开车。
cause sleepiness = 使人犯困
sleepiness = 困倦
② She fought against her sleepiness and kept reading until the end of the chapter.
= 她强忍着困意，一直读到那一章结束。
fight against your sleepiness = 强忍困意

sleepless
/ˈsliːpləs/
核心：形容词：失眠的、不眠的：a sleepless night。副词 sleeplessly 是失眠地、彻夜难眠地。名词 sleeplessness 另立条。
① I had a sleepless night before my driving test, worrying about every mistake I might make.
= 驾照考试前一晚我一夜没睡着，担心自己可能犯的每一个错误。
a sleepless night = 不眠之夜
sleepless = 失眠的
② She lay sleeplessly in bed, listening to the rain on the roof.
= 她躺在床上彻夜难眠，听着雨打屋顶的声音。
sleeplessly = 彻夜难眠地
常见搭配：spend a sleepless night、after several sleepless nights。

sleeplessness
/ˈsliːpləsnəs/
核心：名词，不可数：失眠。
① Stress at work was causing her sleeplessness and terrible headaches every week.
= 工作压力让她失眠，每周都头痛得厉害。
cause sleeplessness = 导致失眠
sleeplessness = 失眠
② The doctor gave him advice on how to deal with sleeplessness without taking pills.
= 医生给了他一些不吃药应对失眠的建议。
deal with sleeplessness = 应对失眠

sleepover
/ˈsliːpəʊvə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：（孩子在朋友家的）过夜聚会、睡衣派对。
① My daughter is having a sleepover on Saturday, and five of her friends are coming.
= 我女儿星期六要办一个过夜聚会，她有五个朋友要来。
have a sleepover = 办过夜聚会
sleepover = 过夜聚会
② The girls stayed up all night talking and laughing at the sleepover.
= 女孩们在过夜聚会上又说又笑了一整夜。
at the sleepover = 在过夜聚会上

sleepsuit
/ˈsliːpsuːt/

① The baby was asleep in a soft white sleepsuit with little yellow ducks on it.
= 宝宝穿着一件印有小黄鸭的柔软白色连体睡衣睡着了。
a sleepsuit = 婴儿连体睡衣
sleepsuit = 连体睡衣
② We bought three sleepsuits as a present for our friends' new baby.

sleepwalk
/ˈsliːpwɔːk/
核心：动词，不及物：梦游；（比喻）糊里糊涂地陷入（困境）：sleepwalk into something。
① My little brother sometimes sleepwalks at night and wakes up in the kitchen.
= 我弟弟有时会梦游，醒来时发现自己在厨房里。
sleepwalk = 梦游
② The country is sleepwalking into a crisis, and nobody is doing anything about it.
= 那个国家正糊里糊涂地走向危机，却没人采取任何措施。
sleepwalk into something = 糊里糊涂地陷入某事

sleepwalker
/ˈsliːpwɔːkə(r)/
核心：名词，可数：梦游者。
① You should never wake a sleepwalker suddenly, because it can frighten them.
= 千万别突然叫醒梦游的人，那会吓着他们。
a sleepwalker = 梦游者
sleepwalker = 梦游者
② The sleepwalker walked out of the house and down the street in his pyjamas.
= 那个梦游者穿着睡衣走出家门，沿街走去。
the sleepwalker = 那个梦游者

sleepy
/ˈsliːpi/
核心：形容词：困倦的、想睡的：feel sleepy；（地方）冷清的、寂静的：a sleepy village。副词 sleepily 是困倦地、睡眼惺忪地。
① The warm room made me feel sleepy, and I nearly fell asleep in the meeting.
= 屋里暖洋洋的，让我直犯困，开会时差点睡着。
feel sleepy = 犯困
sleepy = 困倦的
② They live in a sleepy little village in the hills where nothing ever happens.
= 他们住在山里一个什么事也没发生过的宁静小村子里。
a sleepy village = 宁静的村子
③ When the phone rang at midnight, he answered it sleepily, rubbing his eyes.
= 半夜电话响了，他揉着眼睛，睡眼惺忪地接了起来。
sleepily = 睡眼惺忪地

sleepyhead
/ˈsliːpihed/
核心：名词，单数（口语，亲昵）：瞌睡虫、贪睡的人（多用来称呼孩子）。
① Every morning my mother calls my little sister a sleepyhead, because she never wants to get up.
= 我妈妈每天早上都叫我妹妹小懒虫，因为她从来不想起床。
sleepyhead = 瞌睡虫
② Our son is a real sleepyhead who would stay in bed until lunchtime if we let him.
= 我们儿子是个十足的瞌睡虫，要是由着他，他能在床上躺到中午。
a real sleepyhead = 十足的瞌睡虫

sleet
/sliːt/

① We walked home through cold wind and sleet, and our coats were soaking wet.
= 我们顶着寒风和雨夹雪走回家，外套都湿透了。

= 我们刚离开车站就下起了雨夹雪，可我们没带伞。
it sleets = 下雨夹雪

sleeve
/sliːv/
核心：名词，可数：袖子：long sleeves、roll up your sleeves；（英式）（唱片的）封套；（机器的）套筒。习语：have something up your sleeve 是留有一手、暗藏妙计；wear your heart on your sleeve 是喜怒形于色；roll up your sleeves 是卷起袖子准备大干。
① For the job interview, she wore a white blouse with long sleeves and a dark skirt.
= 面试时，她穿着一件长袖白衬衫和一条深色裙子。
long sleeves = 长袖
sleeve = 袖子
② The coach always has a few tricks up his sleeve for the last ten minutes of the match.
= 教练总会为比赛最后十分钟留几手。
have something up your sleeve = 留有一手
③ He wears his heart on his sleeve, so you always know when he is upset.
= 他喜怒形于色，所以他一不高兴你总能看出来。
wear your heart on your sleeve = 喜怒形于色
④ There is a lot of work to do, so let's roll up our sleeves and start.
= 要干的活很多，我们卷起袖子开始吧。
roll up your sleeves = 卷起袖子大干
⑤ The sleeve notes on the old record tell the story of how the band met.
= 那张老唱片的封套说明讲了乐队成员是怎么认识的。
sleeve notes = （唱片的）封套说明

sleigh

slender
/ˈslendə(r)/
核心：形容词：（人、物）细长的、苗条的：slender fingers；（数量、机会）微薄的、不多的：a slender chance。
① The young musician had long slender fingers, perfect for playing the piano.
= 那位年轻乐手的手指修长纤细，非常适合弹钢琴。
slender fingers = 修长的手指
slender = 细长的
② After a very close game, the team won by a slender margin of one point.
= 经过一场难分难解的比赛，那支球队以一分的微弱优势获胜。

③ They are a young couple with slender means and a small flat.

slender means = 微薄的收入

slenderness
/ˈslendənəs/

sleuth
